title: Current contracts · v1.22
---

# XYZ.js v1.22.1 — current contracts

**Normative for package 1.22.1, Apache-2.0, browser runtime, zero runtime dependencies.** npm remains unpublished. This page describes current supported profiles including P104–P118, not an acceptance report or upstream compatibility promise. [GitHub v1.16](https://github.com/JS-PACKAGE/XYZ.js/releases/tag/v1.16) is published after the existing CI gate, with its downloaded archive/checksum verified; the historical v1.14 archive does not contain P104–P118. Historical dates, test counts, release versions and originally excluded features remain in [ACCEPTANCE](https://github.com/JS-PACKAGE/XYZ.js/blob/main/ACCEPTANCE.md). English / 繁體中文 / 日本語：目前契約／現在の契約。Older exclusions do not override the current profiles below.

## Public API and distribution

The supported public entry is `xyz.js` (or the complete built tree's `engine/src/index.js` on the static site). It exports core, graphics, math, assets, input and audio; ECS is an internal model, not a separate root export. Use the generated **API v1.22.1** portal for exact classes, types, methods and overloads. Its search includes API names, comments and this document; inherited members can be shown with the visibility filters.

`pnpm docs:api` generates `.vite/site/api/1.22.1/` and its documentation landing pages. `pnpm build:site` builds the examples and the same searchable API into the complete `.vite/site/` distribution. Serve over HTTP/HTTPS and open `docs/` or `api/1.22.1/`; generated HTML is not tracked or included in the engine tarball. Relative API links and search assets stay within the version directory, so deployment under a path prefix does not require URL rewriting. Source documentation is not a claim that the hosted site has been deployed.

For standalone consumers, build and pack, then use `node scripts/create-game.mjs /absolute/my-game --template 2d --package /absolute/xyz.js-1.22.1.tgz --name my-game` (or `3d`). Deploy the complete starter `dist/`. No-bundler engine deployment likewise requires the complete engine `dist/`, including the unchanged official `dist/vendor/opm/` distribution and licenses.

CommonJS and ESM use separate constructor graphs. Choose one format for every
engine object in an application; cross-format Texture/Scene/Mesh instances are
not interchangeable. Canonical type declarations do not imply shared runtime
identity. CDN metadata selects ESM; the GitHub release does not publish npm.

## Capability matrix

These are implementation profiles, **not three-backend equivalence or physical qualification**. Check `game.graphics.capabilities` and optional renderer methods instead of inferring support from the backend name.

| Surface                                                                                    | WebGPU                                          | WebGL2                                          | Canvas2D / shared boundary                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Sprite, atlas, text, HUD/UI, raster paths, isolation, masks, basic blends, render textures | Supported                                       | Supported                                       | Supported; raster paths/text are texture-backed, not native vector tessellation                       |
| Native Material2D, Filter2D, Mesh2D, shader postprocessing                                 | Supported, native WGSL                          | Supported, native GLSL                          | Explicitly unsupported; no software shader/3D fallback                                                |
| Mesh/PBR, instancing, skinning, shadows, 3D postprocessing                                 | Supported                                       | Supported subject to capabilities/extensions    | No visible 3D; HDR/weighted transparency on GL require float color attachments                        |
| Native compressed textures / supplied mips                                                 | Capability-gated formats                        | Capability-gated formats                        | Native sources rejected; select a real raster fallback                                                |
| GPU 3D particles                                                                           | Native analytic vertex particles; prepare first | Native analytic vertex particles; prepare first | Explicitly unsupported; CPU Sprite-particle profiles remain available                                 |
| Initialization                                                                             | `auto` tries WebGPU → WebGL2 → Canvas2D         | Forced backend never switches                   | `auto` fallback is initialization-only                                                                |
| Runtime loss                                                                               | Default same-backend recovery                   | Default same-backend recovery                   | Recreate renderer-owned targets/snapshots after recovery; failure or `recoverGraphics:false` is fatal |

WebGPU and AudioWorklet require a secure origin (localhost is allowed). Device availability is not implied by `navigator.gpu`; the current public compute profile is the bounded WebGPU-only ComputeProgram/ComputeBuffer API. Native shader/particle descriptors must be prepared before rendering; callers own their destruction after removing consumers. Auto presentation uses a copy to the original canvas and is not a performance-equivalent forced backend.

Mesh programs/pipelines are lazy, feature-keyed and bounded to 64 cached entries.
Zero-strength physical lobes are omitted from ordinary shader source. Cold synchronous
draws still compile synchronously; explicit preparation uses available parallel/async
driver compilation. Native hooks retain conservative full shading and recovery preparation.

## Shared subsystem profiles

| Surface                         | Current support and important bounds                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Lifecycle / simulation          | Game/Scene ownership, atomic prepared-scene publication, cooperative abort, fixed gameplay updates and opt-in physics interpolation. Time is seconds; pause/hidden time does not accumulate. Do not update physics again inside `fixedUpdate`.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Assets / glTF                   | Task-based preload, manifest/bundles, ResourcePool leases/scopes, bounded streamed reads and cleanup. glTF triangles, UV0/UV1, independent per-map texture transforms, four/eight skin influences, morph and `COLOR_0`; `COLOR_1` and unsupported required extensions reject. Meshopt is built in; Draco/Basis use explicitly supplied external codecs.                                                                        |
| Asset production                | Semantic platform bundles contain capability-selected variants and a codec-free raster fallback. Preflight accepts `TEXCOORD_0` and `TEXCOORD_1`, requires each textured primitive's selected stream, and keeps material-map transforms independent. Default KTX2 decoding is base-level RGBA8; native sources preserve supported payloads/mips.                                                                               |
| Physics2D                       | Dynamic/static/kinematic bodies, sleep, five joints, static concave decomposition/chains, convex character sweep/slide/platform carry and bounded relative translation/rotation CCD. Sensors are discrete; no dynamic concave/compound/deforming sweeps.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Physics3D / navigation          | Bounded primitive dynamic bodies, joints, relative/angular CCD, capsule character/crouch/moving support, queries; graphs, polygon navmeshes, layered/partitioned sampled worlds and collision-derived navigation use documented work/storage quotas, not unlimited world size.                                                                                                                                                 |
| Animation                       | Ordered layers, masks, reference-relative additive animation, fades/crossfades, state machines, 1D/triangulated 2D blend trees, two-bone IK, GPU skinning with lazy CPU queries and conservative animated bounds.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Content / input / accessibility | Tiled JSON including bounded infinite chunks/groups/parallax/animation/templates; input contexts, remapping and portable settings/saves; semantic-only DOM focus/activation mirror. A semantic mirror is not DOM-rendered gameplay or assistive-technology certification.                                                                                                                                                      |
| Audio                           | Immutable official OPM.js v1.13.1 bytes (tag v1.13.1), eight slots including release on one manager-owned AudioContext shared with native samples/streams (P137). Gesture unlock precedes decode/play; an OPM start failure leaves samples/streams usable (`opmState`/`opmError`). Game pause does not automatically pause audio. Native effects/automation/ducking/world bindings do not imply audible hardware verification. |
| Scale / rendering budgets       | Visibility/LOD/HLOD, streaming, workers, bounded light/shadow selection and observability. Worker results still incur main-thread Geometry validation/copy. Per Scene: up to 1024 point and 1024 spot lights; per draw: 32 each; shadows: four cascades/eight point/eight spot.                                                                                                                                                |
| Deployment / compatibility      | 1.x additive profiles, optional renderer extensions, standalone 2D/3D starters, strict-CSP hosting and opt-in offline assets. Offline support does not cache arbitrary missing resources or relax origin/security rules.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Official OPM.js v1.13.1 (tag `v1.13.1`) normalizes voices to v8 internally; the engine-exposed `OPMVoice` retains the published `version: 1` contract. `game.audio.opm` is the engine's legacy compatibility facade backed by the first official instance, not that native instance itself; it is available only while `opmState` is `'ready'`. It preserves writable context/node handles and a mutable voice `Map` with v1 voices. Direct escape-hatch use still bypasses engine budgeting and lifecycle management; managed playback and teardown continue to use official `panic()`/`dispose()` internally without modifying vendor bytes.

Since P137 `AudioManager` owns one AudioContext; the eight OPM instances borrow it through the official `context` option and never suspend or close it. `unlock()` resolves once that context runs even if OPM cannot start (insecure origin, missing vendor files, blocked worklet): the failure is reported through the Game `error` event and `opmError`, OPM asset playback throws, and samples/streams still play. `unlock()` rejects only when no AudioContext can be created or resumed. Executed evidence is in the P137 ACCEPTANCE section.

P138 adds the opt-in `opmModuleUrl` Game option to load official OPM.js from a commit-pinned CDN or another same-origin path instead of the vendored copy. It accepts HTTPS (or same-origin HTTP) module URLs only and requires the module to report the vendored version; it cannot integrity-check CDN bytes, and the page's CSP must allow that origin. The vendored copy remains the default and is still packaged. Evidence is in the P138 ACCEPTANCE section.

'twopinscrewheads' | 'amazonite' | 'blackeyedpeas' | 'adzukibeans' | 'anigre' | 'makore' | 'wavewashers' | 'cupwashers' | 'larvikite' | 'squareholewashers' | 'tigerwood' | 'snakewood' | 'safflowerseeds' | 'thulite' | 'unakite' | 'eudialyte' | 'lignumvitae' | 'iroko' | 'purpleheart' | 'yellowheart' | 'bloodwood' | 'basswood' | 'tabwashers' | 'sphericalwashers' | 'mungbeans' | 'thomsonite' | 'dalmatianstone' | 'nickelsilver' | 'hopsack' | 'monkscloth' | 'lyocell' | 'sateen' | 'surah' | 'serge' | 'habotai' | 'batiste' | 'ramie'`) and `ProceduralMaterialOptions` (`size?: number; seed?: number; contrast?: number; roughnessBias?: number; repeats?: number; tint?: readonly [number, number, number]`). `tint` multiplies the authored sRGB pigment per channel (each finite 0–4); mortar/grout tones and the normal, metallic-roughness and occlusion maps are unchanged, and omitting it keeps output byte-identical. Passing `workerPool` (from `createProceduralWorkerPool()`, a borrowed `NativeWorkerPool` running the published `workers/procedural-material.worker.js` or an app module installing `trustedProceduralMapsJob` as `procedural.maps`) moves generation off the calling thread with byte-identical output; without it generation stays synchronous. The caller owns and destroys the pool; texture acquisition still happens on the main thread. A caller-owned `ProceduralMapCache({ maxBytes?, maxEntries? })` (default 64 MiB / 32 entries, LRU) passed as `cache` reuses generated CPU maps for identical kind/size/seed/contrast/roughnessBias/tint, shares concurrent identical generations and never caches failures; each preset still creates and owns its own textures, and `clear()` drops retained maps. Set `heightMap: true` to also generate a linear height map (R, 0 deepest–1 highest); `createMaterial()` binds it as `opticalMaps.heightTexture`, which PBR parallax (`finish.heightScale`) then samples instead of normal Z. It does not enable parallax by itself, is included in cache keys and worker transfers, and is destroyed with the preset. `await ProceduralMaterial.create(kind, options?)` generates deterministic, seamlessly periodic immutable maps once on the CPU, without external assets or new dependencies. `size` is an integer 32–1024 (default 256); `seed` is an unsigned 32-bit integer 0–4294967295 (default 1). Invalid inputs reject.

The kind union additionally accepts `chainstitch`: five columns of six linked, tapered hollow gold embroidery loops above dark blue woven backing. Its 0.12 m tile retains periodic opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and existing ownership; no yarn geometry, transparent holes or cloth simulation.

The kind union additionally accepts `wingnuts`: sixteen alternating quarter-turned silver wing nuts, with paired raised oblique wings, round hubs and recessed central bores above dark steel backing. The 0.12 m tile uses opaque metal maps and metallic factor 1, optional height and existing ownership; no nut geometry, transparent holes, real threads or mechanical simulation.

`pomegranateslice` adds nine red-rimmed round cross sections, each with six recessed chambers containing seven separate raised red arils, pale central pith and dividing membranes. The 0.15 m tile is opaque dielectric (metallic 0), with existing maps, optional height and ownership; no fruit geometry, transparency, transmission or biological simulation.

`pawprinttiles` adds sixteen ivory glazed square tiles with recessed cobalt paw stamps: four separate oval toe pads and a three-lobed lower palm, above rough neutral grout. One UV tile represents 0.6 m. Opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership stay unchanged; no tile geometry, physical stamping or animal simulation.

`dovetailjoint` adds two rows of four outward-flared trapezoidal wood tongues with recessed straight shoulders, oblique sides and flat tips. Adjacent boards alternate longitudinal and transverse grain with continuous board ownership across rows. The 0.48 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no joinery geometry or structural simulation.

`starsocketbolts` adds sixteen domed silver bolt caps with closed-bottom six-lobed star recesses, alternating by 30°. Socket floors remain above the dark steel backing. The 0.12 m tile uses opaque metal maps and default metallic 1, optional height and existing ownership; no bolt geometry, transparent holes, real threads or mechanical simulation.

`sanddollar` adds nine pale cream flat oval shells with five closed petal-outline grooves and five narrow radial lunules between petals. One tile covers 0.3 m. Opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership remain unchanged; lunules reveal shaded opaque backing, not transparent holes. Fine petal grooves are easier to inspect at larger texture/preview resolutions.

`brogueleather` adds nine six-petaled oval-punch medallions with round centers and double rows of small border holes on warm brown leather. Recesses reveal rough opaque dark backing. The 0.18 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no leather geometry, transparent openings or material simulation.

`clovebuds` adds sixteen brown dried flower buds, with raised round heads, four narrow calyx lobes and slender stems alternating by 90°. The 0.08 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; gaps are shaded opaque backing, not spice geometry or biological/food simulation.

`starcrosstiles` alternates sixteen teal eight-pointed stars with ivory cross-shaped glazed tiles and recessed neutral grout. Both straight and diagonal edges have smooth shoulders, including concave joins and adjacent star tips. The 0.8 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no tile geometry, transparent gaps or paving simulation.

`maplesamara` adds sixteen pairs of golden-brown V-shaped wings, flared toward their ends, with darker raised kernels at the roots and a short connecting stalk. Adjacent pairs flip by 180°; fine veins remain on the wing membranes. The 0.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no fruit geometry, translucent membranes, flight or biological simulation.

`lenoweave` adds six paired cream/ochre warp groups twisting between six horizontal wefts. Warp-over-warp and warp-over-weft ordering alternate; open cells show low opaque dark backing. The 0.06 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no yarn geometry, transparent mesh or cloth simulation.

`circlips` adds sixteen silver open C-shaped retaining rings, each with two terminal round lugs and recessed plier bores, alternating by 180°. Centers, gaps and bores reveal dark opaque steel backing. The 0.08 m tile retains metal maps/default metallic 1, optional height and ownership; no clip geometry, transparent holes, spring behavior or mechanical simulation.

`tuliptiles` adds sixteen ivory glazed square tiles with cobalt three-pointed tulip crowns, slender stems and two pointed leaves. Motifs sit slightly above the glaze; rough neutral grout is recessed. The 0.6 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no flower/tile geometry, transparent joints or plant simulation.

`gingerslice` adds sixteen knobbly rhizome cross sections composed of four joined unequal lobes, alternating by 90°. Pale yellow fibrous flesh stands above the thin, darker, rougher brown rind; concave necks distinguish them from round slices or perforated lotus root. The 0.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no rhizome geometry, transparency or food/plant simulation.

`fivebarplate` adds sixteen groups of five parallel round-ended silver ribs, alternating between ±45° over a rougher metal sheet, distinct from single-lozenge `diamondplate`. The 0.6 m tile retains metal maps/default metallic 1, optional height and ownership; no plate geometry, actual slip resistance or structural physics. Larger texture/preview resolutions help resolve individual thin ribs.

`cinnamonswirl` adds sixteen golden bun cross sections with a continuous recessed brown cinnamon spiral, a filled center and a darker baked rim, alternating by 180°. Its 0.32 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no food geometry, transparency or baking simulation.

`wheatears` adds sixteen stylized golden wheat ears, each with five pairs of grooved kernels, ten slender awns and a central rachis, alternating by 180°. The 0.24 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no plant geometry, transparency, growth or wind simulation. Larger texture/preview resolutions help resolve the thin awns and grooves.

`scalemail` adds six columns by eight staggered rows of steel scales, with upper rows covering lower rows, raised central ribs, round rivets and rolled rims. Dark gaps retain opaque steel backing, unlike organic `fishscales` or ring-shaped `chainmail`. The 0.24 m tile retains metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; no scale geometry, transparent holes, collision or protective physics.

`trihexagonaltiles` adds 56 blue hexagonal tiles and 112 ivory triangles in alternating orientations, separated by shared recessed neutral grout; the hexagons sit slightly higher and smoother. The 0.84 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no tile geometry, collision or construction simulation.

`burlwood` adds stylized brown-gold figured wood with warped closed-contour grain, nine offset elliptical eyes and darker, slightly recessed, rougher piths. Seeded grain varies around fixed eye locations, unlike straight wood fibers or small bird's-eye maple figures. The 0.24 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no wood geometry, species identification, ring dating or growth simulation.

`slottedmetal` adds 8×4 staggered vertical round-ended wells in a brushed steel sheet, with machined beveled walls and dark opaque steel floors. Matching axis units preserves circular end caps despite rectangular pitch; this differs from round `perforatedmetal` holes and horizontal wood slots. The 0.4 m tile retains metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; no through-hole geometry, transparency, ventilation or structural physics.

`peppercorns` adds 25 stylized dark-brown dried berries with domed bodies, shallow wrinkled skin and darker recessed, rougher stalk scars, distinct from clove stems/calyxes or coffee-bean slits. The 0.08 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no berry geometry, transparency or food/plant simulation. Its low-contrast skin is intentionally subtle, not a photogrammetric scan.

`castellatednuts` adds 4×4 alternating-orientation steel hex nuts with round central bores, six recessed radial crown slots and six raised segments. The inner ring remains continuous and the slots have opaque steel floors, unlike uncut `hexnuts` or external crown-cap flutes. The 0.08 m tile retains metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; no nut geometry, transparent through-holes, screw threads or fastening physics.

`farfalle` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical golden bow-tie pasta pieces with connected narrow waists, flared wings, converging pleats and scalloped end cuts, distinct from filled ravioli pillows or hollow macaroni. The 0.16 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no pasta geometry, transparency, cooking or soft-body physics.

`cardamompods` adds 4×4 stylized olive-green closed pods alternating diagonally, with pointed tapered ends, domed skins, converging longitudinal ribs and channels, and attached short stalks. This differs from clove stems/calyxes and opened pistachios. The 0.08 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no pod geometry, transparency, opening animation or plant/food simulation.

`brickarches` adds a 4×4 decorative red-brick arch frieze: each semicircle has nine wedge-shaped voussoirs, radial mortar joints and two piers with three bed-jointed courses each. Seeded brick tones fade into common recessed mortar; openings remain dark opaque backing. The 2.4 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no building geometry, transparent doorways, load-bearing or collision physics.

`knottednet` adds a 6×6 tan cord net with bowed sagging spans, raised crossed ties, tightened knot creases and dark recessed mesh centers. Unlike straight metallic wiremesh, diagonal lace or staggered macrame, its strands bow continuously between a square knot lattice. The 0.3 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no net geometry, transparent openings, tension or cloth physics.

`fiddleheads` adds 4×4 alternating mirrored green fern-shoot motifs with tapered one-and-a-half-turn coils, open spiral gaps and attached bent stems, distinct from expanded fern pinnae. Precomputed smooth capsule unions preserve connected stems without angular ownership seams. The 0.24 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no plant geometry, transparency, growth or branch physics. This is a stylized motif, not botanical identification or a photogrammetric scan.

`cowrieshells` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical ivory cowrie undersides, with domed oval shells, dark narrow recessed apertures and nine raised smoother teeth on each opposing lip. Tooth rows do not bridge the aperture; this differs from scallop ribs and nautilus coils. The 0.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no shell geometry, transparent apertures or marine-life simulation.

`cotterpins` adds 4×4 alternating upright/inverted steel split pins, with elliptical loop eyes, two separated shafts and unequal outward-bent tails. Compact ring and capsule unions keep the wire connected while preserving dark opaque eye/shaft gaps. The 0.12 m tile retains metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; no hardware geometry, transparent holes, fastening or bending physics.

`seedstitch` adds a sage-green 12×12 checker of open V-shaped knit stitches and horizontal purl bumps, alternating across both rows and columns. Smooth finite yarn capsules retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.12 m tile; no yarn geometry, transparency or cloth simulation.

`mangosteenslice` adds 4×4 thick purple-red rinds enclosing six rounded ivory pulp lobes, joined at the center and separated by recessed radial gaps; neighboring slices turn by 30°. Smooth compact ellipse unions retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.32 m tile; no fruit geometry, transparent openings or botanical simulation.

`pickettiles` adds a staggered field of teal glazed elongated hexagons: 36 pointed tiles per 0.9 m UV square, nominally 0.1×0.3 m before grout/bevel. Smooth products of six half-planes retain straight sides, tapered tips and recessed neutral grout, with seeded per-tile tones. Opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership remain unchanged; no tile geometry, transparency or construction simulation.

`squarewashers` adds 4×4 closed square steel frames alternating by 45°, with beveled straight outer sides, four retained corners and circular recessed center bores. The 0.16 m tile retains opaque metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership; bores remain dark opaque steel backing. No washer geometry, transparent holes, fastening or load simulation.

`eucalyptusleaf` adds 4×4 blue-green lanceolate leaves tilted alternately by ±45°, with gently curved blades, tapered tips, raised midribs, five pairs of oblique secondary veins and connected short petioles. Compact polynomial blade/stem fields retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.48 m tile; no leaf geometry, transparency, wind or botanical simulation.

`twillweave` adds a 16×16 balanced two-over-two twill: light tan weft crossings and dark brown warp crossings appear in pairs, shifted one yarn per row to form continuous diagonals. Rounded yarn profiles and localized over/under exposure retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.16 m tile; no yarn geometry, transparent gaps or cloth simulation.

`pumpkinseeds` adds 4×4 pale seed hulls tilted alternately by ±45°, with tapered pointed ends, raised perimeter rims and intact lower central faces. Nested compact polynomial masks retain opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.12 m tile; no seed geometry, transparent hulls, cracking or botanical simulation.

`spurgears` adds 4×4 stylized steel gears alternating by 15°, with twelve beveled external block teeth, recessed circular bores and raised annular hubs. Smooth tooth/body unions retain opaque metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership on a 0.2 m tile; bore/gullet gaps remain dark opaque steel backing. No gear geometry, transparent holes, involute-profile certification or transmission simulation.

`diamondcheckertiles` adds 32 square glazed tiles rotated 45°, alternating black and cream along both diagonal axes, with softened bevels, recessed neutral grout and seeded face tones. Quotient-lattice ownership remains periodic across both UV edges; the 1.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership. No tile geometry, transparency or physics.

`appleslice` adds 4×4 apple cross sections alternating by 36°, with thin red-brown peel, pale flesh and five separate recessed seed chambers containing darker raised seeds below the uncut flesh. Its 0.4 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership; distinct from the intact exterior in `appleskin`. No fruit geometry, transparent chambers, cutting, juice or botanical simulation.

`paperclips` adds 4×4 silver steel wire clips alternating by ±45°, with three connected legs, two nested return bends and two open wire ends around recessed slots. Compact capsule/ring unions retain opaque metallic maps/default factor 1, optional height and ownership on a 0.24 m tile; slots remain dark opaque steel backing. No wire geometry, transparency or clamping simulation.

`ivyleaf` adds 4×4 green three-lobed ivy leaves alternating by ±45°, with pointed lobes, two deep notches, three raised palmate veins and connected short petioles. Compact polynomial lobe unions and masked vein projections retain opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.32 m tile. No leaf geometry, transparency, wind animation or botanical simulation.

`ribknit` adds 12×12 rose-brown stitches in alternating vertical columns of raised V-shaped knit stitches and lower horizontal purl bumps. Column ownership stays fixed across rows, unlike `seedstitch`; compact yarn supports preserve periodic opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.12 m tile. No yarn geometry, transparent holes, elasticity or cloth simulation.

`pillowtiles` adds 6×6 ivory glazed rounded-square tiles with continuous convex cushion faces, softened bevels, recessed neutral grout and seeded face tones. Quartic superellipse coverage and a separate smooth quadratic dome retain opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.6 m tile. No tile geometry, displacement or transparency.

`coffeerings` adds 3×3 seeded irregular elliptical coffee cup tide marks on pale paper, with locally faded brown rings and lighter interior staining. Pigment and roughness vary over near-flat paper grain without carved cavities or stain-driven AO. The 0.36 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no paper/cup geometry, liquid transparency, fluid, absorption or drying simulation.

`cashews` adds 4×4 cream kidney-shaped kernels with thick C-shaped bodies, connected rounded asymmetric tips and mouths open to the backing, in alternating ±45° orientations. Smooth crescent/tip unions and separate convex domes retain opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership on a 0.16 m tile. No kernel geometry, transparent holes, subsurface scattering or food physics.

`hinges` adds 4×4 steel hinge faces with paired rounded mounting leaves, six recessed countersunk bores and five convex barrel knuckles. A continuous lower pin remains visible across four joint grooves; seeded brushed grain stays inside the hardware. The 0.4 m tile retains opaque metallic maps/metallic 1, optional height and ownership. No hinge geometry, transparent holes, articulation or rigid-body physics.

`coins` adds 4×4 gold coin faces with raised circular rims around lower fields and connected embossed five-point stars. Smooth overlapping triangular spikes and pentagonal hubs preserve open valleys without internal seams; seeded face tones alternate with 36° star orientations. The 0.12 m tile retains opaque metallic maps/metallic 1, optional height and ownership. No coin geometry, lettering, transparency, currency system or rigid-body physics.

`righttriangletiles` adds 32 blue-grey/cream right-isosceles glazed triangles in 4×4 split squares with alternating diagonal directions. Independent seeded pigments fade into recessed neutral grout; perpendicular diagonal distance keeps straight and diagonal grout/bevel widths equal. The 1 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership. No tile geometry, displacement or transparency.

`cherryblossom` adds 4×4 stylized pink five-petal flowers with shallow split-tip notches, a lower heart and twelve raised pale pollen dots. Independent smooth petal/dome unions preserve connected bodies without overlap spikes; seeded tones alternate with 36° orientations. The 0.24 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership. No flower geometry, transparent holes, growth, wind or subsurface-scattering simulation.

`punchcard` adds 2×4 thin cream paper cards with one clipped upper corner and 20×8 rectangular punch positions. Each column has two distinct seeded punches, leaving intact paper webs; slots return all surface fields to opaque backing rather than transparent cutouts. The 0.44 m tile retains dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership, with about 0.3 mm paper relief. No card geometry, real data encoding/reading or paper physics.

`braidedcord` adds four vertical cotton-colored flat plaits with four periods each. Three differently toned strands follow C2 quartic paths with doubled-phase depth, alternating over/under rather than forming the helices in `rope` or the two-strand knitted cable in `cableknit`. Smooth depth ownership preserves convex crossings and periodic column gaps. The 0.08 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership; no strand geometry, transparent holes, braiding process or cloth physics.

`crossbeddedsandstone` adds four horizontal sandstone beds containing gently curved inclined laminae with alternating dip directions. Seeded dip sets fade before the shared contact bands rather than continuing through bed boundaries, unlike nearly parallel `sandstone` strata and `rammedearth` layers. The 1.2 m tile retains opaque dielectric maps/metallic 0, optional height and ownership, with subtle lamina relief and continuous sandy grain. No rock geometry, displacement, stratigraphic generation, sedimentation or erosion simulation.

`grommetcanvas` adds 4×4 raised convex brass grommet rings on rough brown woven canvas, with dark opaque bore backing and smooth inner/outer bevels. The authored metallic map is 1 only on brass, 0 on cloth/backing and fractional on bevels; default material factor 1 preserves this split rather than making the whole surface metallic. The 0.16 m tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership. No geometry, transparent cutouts or cloth physics.

`fishscaletiles` adds interlocking teal glazed fan mosaics with convex semicircular tops, two concave lower arcs and neutral curved grout. Four columns and eight staggered rows retain seeded glaze tones and coplanar faces rather than overlapping roof shingles or painting waves on square tiles. The 0.4 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height, tint-neutral grout and ownership; no geometry, transparency or tiling simulation.

`hexkeys` adds 4×4 black oxide L-shaped hex keys with seeded quarter-turn poses, unequal arms, tangent round elbows and flat cut ends. Flat crowns and two side facets distinguish the hexagonal rods from round wire clips. The 0.4 m opaque metallic tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership; metallic map and default factor are 1. No tool geometry, mechanical assembly or physics.

`driedfigslice` adds 4×4 alternating tapered dried fig slices with tan wrinkled rind, dark brown flesh and many tiny pale elliptical seeds confined to the interior. Seed positions, axes and tones are deterministic and jittered rather than arranged in pomegranate chambers or a kiwi ring. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no fruit geometry, transparency, food generation or biological simulation.

`creamcrackers` adds 4×4 separate golden baked crackers with four scalloped borders, nine dark recessed docking pits, browned rims and irregular toasted blisters. Unlike continuous wafer pockets, each cracker retains an intact flat baked face between the nine pits. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no food geometry, transparent holes, baking or food physics.

`doorkeys` adds 4×4 flat brass door keys with seeded quarter-turn poses, bored circular bows, continuous necks, asymmetric blades, three rounded bittings, longitudinal grooves and chamfered tips. The 0.32 m opaque metallic tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership; metallic map and default factor are 1. These are stylized surface silhouettes, not key geometry, transparent holes, lock mechanisms or mechanical simulation.

`dodecagontiles` adds a canonical 3.12.12 glazed mosaic: 56 blue-teal dodecagons and 112 pale triangular inserts in seven columns and eight staggered rows, with seeded tones, neutral grout and coplanar faces. Its rectangular canonical period is normalized to square UVs with approximately 1% axis-scale difference, not a physical regular-polygon measurement guarantee. The 0.7 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no geometry, transparency or tiling simulation.

`ballchain` adds four silver ball pull chains with eight solid spherical beads each, narrow straight connectors and two gently meandering periods. Beads hide their lower connector joins instead of adding wire height through the crown; this differs from bored oval seed beads and ring links. The 0.064 m opaque metallic tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic map and default factor 1. No chain geometry, transparency or chain physics.

`succulentrosettes` adds 4×4 gray-green rosettes with three whorls of six fleshy spoon-shaped leaves each. Staggered inner leaves hide the narrower outer bases, rise toward the center and retain convex crowns rather than flat flower petals or parallel artichoke bracts. Seeded orientations and leaf tones vary the 0.64 m opaque dielectric tile. Existing maps, optional height and ownership remain unchanged, with metallic 0; no plant geometry, transparency, subsurface scattering or biological simulation.

`foldingfans` adds 4×4 alternating 140-degree paper fans with twelve radial accordion folds, thirteen exposed wooden ribs, side guards and round pivot caps. The annular paper hides the upper ribs while leaving open-looking gaps over opaque backing near the pivot. The 1 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no fan geometry, transparent holes, opening animation or folding physics.

`togglebuttons` adds 4×4 alternating horizontal/vertical wooden barrel toggles with tapered rounded ends, convex crowns, two recessed cord bores and gently curved longitudinal wood grain. It differs from the four-hole round rimmed `button` preset. The 0.32 m opaque dielectric tile retains existing maps, optional height and ownership, with metallic 0; no button geometry, transparent holes, cords or garment physics.

The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparency, juice, cutting, growth, nutrition or physics simulation.

`tactileswitches` adds four-legged tactile switches with low silver leads, black molded dielectric housings, higher metal covers and still-higher solid black insulated plungers. Coherent compact layers mask buried lead roots and clear cover metal beneath each plunger; only exposed leads and covers are metallic, with default factor 1. The 0.24 m opaque mixed-metal tile retains neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, mechanical pressing, conductivity, switch logic, durability certification or physics simulation.

`paisleybrocade` adds navy woven cloth with rotated curved pointed gold-colored yarn outlines and three raised inset dots. A quadratic bent centerline and varying-width squared cap distinguish the stylized paisley drop from a straight leaf/ellipse. The interior remains lower solid blue cloth, not a transparent opening; supplementary yarn is dielectric, unlike metallic floral `goldbrocade`. The 0.4 m opaque tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no yarn geometry or cloth physics.

`siliconepastrybrushes` adds orange one-piece silicone pastry brushes with long handles, wide heads and seven separated lower bristle prongs. Compact heads cover handle necks and all prong roots; gaps show neutral opaque backing, while the handle-end hanging mark is a solid recessed dimple. This is not the long spine/dense teeth of `haircombs`. The 0.32 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no brush geometry, bristle/liquid/bending simulation, food-contact or heat-resistance certification, or physics.

`graphicgranite` adds polished pink feldspar with stylized pointed quartz intergrowth scripts, using three paired tapered stroke groups per seeded cell. Compact pointed covers intersect near jittered shared roots; smooth product unions change pigment only. Dark quartz and pink feldspar share the same microheight and roughness with AO 1, not carved characters or raised veins. This differs from fern-like `dendriticagate` and folded `gneiss`. The 0.2 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent quartz, crystal growth or physics simulation.

`brasspadlocks` adds closed U brass shackles joined through higher brass bodies and dark low solid keyhole motifs. True constant-width arcs meet straight legs tangentially; bodies cover the roots. Circular key heads and capsule stems form one recessed opaque dielectric motif wholly inside the brass face, clearing metal rather than opening transparency. Only bodies and shackles are metallic, with default factor 1. The 0.24 m opaque mixed-metal tile retains neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent holes, unlocking/locking state, security certification or physics simulation.

`carrotcuts` adds orange root cross sections with pale filled medullae, twelve fine pointed radial vascular pigment rays and thin darker rinds. The core and rays share the flat cut-face height, roughness and AO, not annular beet rings or citrus segment grooves. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent medullae, juice, cutting/growth/nutrition or physics simulation.

`bluepushpins` adds blue plastic pushpins with two broad flanges, a narrow waist and exposed lower silver-colored metallic tapered needles. Coherent plastic replacement hides and demetallizes the needle roots; top flanges stand higher than base flanges and waists. The 0.16 m opaque mixed-metal tile retains default factor 1, neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, piercing, fastening/load certification, transparency or physics simulation.

`oatflakes` adds broad pale flattened oval flakes with subtly irregular rims, low solid central creases and six fine longitudinal fibers. The crease floor remains opaque and the flakes are distinct from slender rice grains, bowed fennel seeds and triangular buckwheat hulls. The 0.06 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent splits, cooking/nutrition or physics simulation.

`tigerfur` adds orange fur with six wavy dark bands and two genuinely connected pointed branches per row. The existing short-fur warp, grain and directional hair records supply unchanged pigment-independent pile height, roughness and AO; only compact tiger pigment is new. The 0.4 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no fur geometry, transparency, animal/hair dynamics or physics simulation.

`grosgrainribbons` adds four blue woven ribbon strips with 64 transverse ribs and three higher continuous selvedge wales per edge. Compact bodies retain neutral opaque gutters; these are not full-surface corduroy pile or twisted ribbon streamers. The 0.06 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent gutters, knotting, cloth dynamics or physics simulation.

`azuritemalachite` adds stylized overlapping three-globule blue azurite clusters with two fine pigment rings per globule in a green malachite matrix. All pigments share the flat polished microheight, roughness and AO 1, not raised spheres or carved rings. The 0.2 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent crystals, mineral growth or physics simulation.

`openendspanners` adds two unequal higher open wrench heads connected by a narrower lower metal shaft. The 0.4 m opaque mixed-metal tile retains default factor 1, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent jaws, fastening/torque behavior, tool certification or physics simulation.

`lentilseeds` adds near-round whole convex seeds with short low opaque side hila. The finite side scar does not split the seed or form a long central oat crease; body coverage retains a nonsingular dome over neutral backing. The 0.04 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent splits, germination, cooking/nutrition or physics simulation.

`blueprintpaper` adds flat blue paper with tiered grids, white rounded plate outlines, circles, crosshairs and finite dimension bars with short end ticks. Unlike grid-only graphpaper, technical drawings change pigment only and do not engrave the paper. The 0.4 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, CAD parsing/export, typesetting or document certification.

`horsechestnutleaf` adds stylized compound foliage with seven pointed lightly serrated leaflets attached by thin individual petiolules to one common petiole, with brighter raised midribs. Inner stalk and outer blade profiles retain seven separated runs, unlike a continuous maple palm or ginkgo fan. The 0.8 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent leaf gaps, botanical growth/physiology or physics simulation.

`binderclips` adds two constant-width silver U-wire handles beneath a black coated trapezoid body with a higher lower lip. Exposed wire stays metallic, while paint, handle cavities and exterior neutral opaque backing remain dielectric. The 0.32 m opaque mixed-metal tile retains default factor 1, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent holes, clamping/spring/load behavior or physics simulation.

`charoite` adds stylized purple polished stone with twisting dense fiber bundles, pale wider bands and sparse dark solid inclusions. Optical pigment and inclusions share one polished plane with tiny grain only, not carved grooves or pits. The 0.2 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent mineral cavities, mineral growth, dynamic chatoyant optics or physics simulation.

`chenilleyarn` adds eight pink puffy yarn cores with 128 rows each of brighter higher short transverse pile and loose pointed fringe beyond both core edges. Individual tufts taper to neutral opaque backing, unlike continuous corduroy cloth or flat grosgrain selvedges. The 0.08 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent pile gaps, yarn topology, cloth/hair or physics simulation.

`ceramicfuses` adds matte white convex ceramic cartridge bodies with two full-width metallic end caps and raised terminal collars, without resistor-like thin leads or transparent glass. Ceramic and exterior neutral opaque backing remain dielectric. The 0.16 m opaque mixed-metal tile retains default factor 1, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, electrical/thermal/fuse-blow simulation, ratings or safety certification.

`hazelnutshells` adds whole pointed-top rounded-base woody shells with longitudinal grain that follows each shell rotation and a low pale rough solid basal attachment scar. Unlike continuous walnut ridges or almond pores, individual silhouettes retain a convex crown and never open to kernels. `yagasuricloth` adds stylized cream arrow-fletching-inspired pigment in opposing columns on continuous plum woven cloth. Three mirrored pointed feather pairs join each shaft; pigment does not emboss or engrave height, roughness or AO. The 96-thread weave retains normal relief at the default 256px resolution. The 0.24 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, real weaving/dye-process/cultural craftsmanship certification or cloth physics.

`balloonwhisks` adds three projected oval metal wire loops joined to a continuous neck and a grip with four higher polished rings. Wire/neck/grip use metallic 1 while cage gaps and exterior neutral opaque backing remain metallic 0; default factor is 1. The 0.8 m opaque mixed-metal tile retains maps, optional height and ownership. These are stylized shaded projections, not exact constant-width wire geometry or transparent openings; no mixing/food-contact certification or physics simulation.

`orthocerasstone` adds stylized pointed straight chambered fossil sections with eight curved septa flush in dark polished stone, distinct from coiled fossil limestone and trilobite bodies. Pale mineral chambers, dark septa and matrix share one polished plane; neither fossil outlines nor septa emboss or engrave height, roughness or AO. The 0.4 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, AO 1, mortar 0, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, holes, paleontological/provenance certification, growth or physics simulation.

`rickracktrim` adds six continuous rose textile strips with eight waves, two raised woven selvedges and diagonal fine fibers. Local tangent-corrected width avoids horizontal-width pinching at sloped sections, without claiming exact global curve distance. The 0.12 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, neutral opaque backing, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, transparent strip gaps, real braiding or cloth physics.

`toroidalinductors` adds twelve higher smoother bare-copper projected turns above a dark dielectric ferrite ring. `pennerigate` adds straight slanted-cut pasta tubes with five higher longitudinal ridges and two low dark rough solid mouths, distinct from existing bent macaroni. The 0.16 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; no geometry, cooking/food or physics simulation.

`lepidolitemica` adds lavender lepidolite-inspired mica with three rising, brighter, smoother nested hexagonal cleavage tiers over a continuous rougher mineral matrix, distinct from expanded wavy vermiculite lamellae. `brassjinglebells` adds raised brass dome faces with a higher smoother crimped seam and a lower darker rough opaque cross slot with a round center and four widened round ends. `nutmeghalves` adds stylized oval nutmeg cut faces with a lower rough dark rind around a nearly flat pale-brown kernel. `coaxialcablecuts` adds coplanar cable cuts with a silver central conductor, pale dielectric, forty isolated shield-strand sections and black jacket. `shippocloth` adds cream interlocking complete-circle pigment over continuous navy96-thread woven cloth. Ink changes color only, never height/roughness/AO; actual default256 maps retain visible two-axis normals under both colors. `driedtamarindpods` adds four rough brown shell bulges with three genuinely narrower lower waists and a short stalk, distinct from bulges hidden inside a continuous pointed green-pea pod. The 0.32 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, neutral opaque backing, maps, optional height and ownership. `conicalsampletubes` adds opaque blue capped sample-tube faces with a tapered lower body, higher smoother closed cap, lower one-sided tether and slightly higher brighter rough frosted oval label. `botryoidalcopper` adds stylized higher brighter smoother positive native-copper nodules over lower darker rougher metallic valleys, opposite to concave hammer dents. `pencilshavings` adds seeded rotated half-annular wood fans with twelve radial corrugations and a darker smoother outer paint rim, distinct from hexagonal pencil cut faces. The 0.16 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; the inner area and open half are not transparent. `papayacuts` adds seeded rotated oval ripe-papaya cuts with a narrow rind, thick higher bright orange flesh and twenty-eight independently raised dark rounded kernels in a lower elongated seed pit. Height ordering is flesh>seed>pit; seeds change roughness/AO as well as color/height, not merely pigment within jelly sacs. The 0.16 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0, neutral backing, maps, optional height and ownership; the pit is not transparent. `sewingthimbles` adds closed round-top silver-colored thimble faces with a narrower top, wider base, twenty-four lower darker rougher blind metallic dimples and a higher smoother wider rolled lower lip. Body, lip and dimples share metallic 1, not dielectric punched holes; default factor 1, complete-body mortar 0 and neutral dielectric backing retain maps, optional height and ownership in the 0.16 m opaque tile. Front-face shading only; no geometry, transparent holes, real interior, sewing/puncture/collision/deformation/physics simulation or protective rating.

`boullebrassinlay` adds Boulle-inspired coplanar brass S-scrolls and oval finials over mottled polished synthetic tortoiseshell-inspired resin, with alternating mirrored repeats. Brass cores are metallic 1, resin and curl voids metallic 0, default factor 1 and mortar 0 throughout. Both share the same polished height and AO, not raised gold badges, inlay steps or engraved trenches; nearly flat normal and white AO previews are intentional. The 0.24 m opaque tile retains maps, optional height and ownership. Stylized synthetic material only; no geometry, real tortoiseshell, transparency/transmission or volume/manufacture/restoration/physics simulation.

`diamondpanelparquet` adds four mitered frame boards around a clipped 45° diamond field with alternating board-local grain and lower darker rougher continuous wood joints. Framed diamond layout only, not historical parquet authenticity; no geometry, real boards/displacement, floor collision/installation/deformation or physics simulation.

`dendriticfrost` adds sixteen seeded rotated raised rough opaque frost crystals with six connected stems and twenty-four explicit 60° sidebranches over a smooth continuous dark-blue face, distinct from printed snowflakes or broad angular noise fans. The 0.12 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0/mortar 0 throughout, maps, optional height and ownership. No geometry, transparency/transmission or crystal-growth/weather/snow-accumulation/melting/physics simulation.

`bullionknots` adds sixteen seeded rotated elongated golden embroidery knots with twelve raised transverse wraps and closed rounded ends on dark-blue woven cloth. The wraps change height, roughness, color and AO above continuous lower yarn valleys, rather than printed stripes or round French knots. Golden yarn is dielectric pigment, not metal; the 0.12 m opaque tile retains metallic 0/mortar 0 throughout yarn and cloth, maps, optional height and ownership. No geometry, real helical yarn topology, transparent cloth holes or needle/thread/cloth/sewing/physics simulation.

`orecchiette` adds sixteen seeded rotated cupped golden semolina ear-pasta faces with continuous high rolled rims and lower darker rougher off-center opaque bowls, unlike raised ravioli filling or transparent holes. `solderlessbreadboard` adds an opaque white plastic signal-bank face with sixteen rows of two five-hole banks (160 rounded-square recesses), intact four-way white bridges and a separate continuous lower central trough. `kyaniteblades` adds sixteen seeded rotated opaque blue kyanite-inspired bundles of three parallel cleaved blades with staggered oblique ends and three raised longitudinal striae per blade over lower darker rougher continuous matrix. The 0.16 m opaque dielectric tile retains metallic 0/mortar 0 across blades and matrix, maps, optional height and ownership. `internaltoothwashers` adds sixteen seeded rotated closed silver-colored outer rims with twelve inward metal teeth alternating six raised and six lowered tips around an opaque star-shaped recess. `driedchillies` adds sixteen seeded rotated quadratic-curved strongly tapering dried red fruits with actual lower darker rougher wrinkle valleys, higher olive-green calyces and connected thin stems. `utilityknifeblades` adds sixteen seeded rotated silver-colored trapezoidal utility blades with two spine indexing notches and a continuous lower smoother brighter honed edge. `windmillparquet` adds a five-board windmill panel with grain following four rotating long staves and a wooden center square. `seamrippers` adds nine seeded asymmetric steel tools with a long curved tapered tine, a shorter blue ball-capped tine, exposed backing U slot and nonmetal navy handle. `flakedarrowheads` adds sixteen seeded stemmed flint-inspired points with two exposed lateral notches, a raised central ridge and five lower darker rougher oblique scars on each front facet beside the ridge. `dandelionseeds` adds nine seeded stylized parachute seeds with sixteen thin off-white pappus fibers, connected lower tan stalks and raised brown seeds. `shojipaperlattice` adds a continuous six-by-six raised brown wood grid around lower white fibrous paper, an opaque shoji-inspired surface rather than open trellis or a palette-only tile variant. Periodic full-core/support profiles are C1; full wood skips paper fields and fully covered vertical grain. All M0/mortar0, 0.9 m tile, 0.004 m relief, unchanged maps/height/Worker/cache/tint/ownership; no transmission, transparency, scattering or joinery geometry.

`nyloncableties` adds nine seeded cream nylon ties with one wider open rounded locking head, a continuous narrow strap, seventeen genuinely raised darker smoother transverse ratchet teeth and a tapered tail. Cached poses/grain and squared rounded-rectangle/window profiles reuse existing conventions; smoothstep taper and nearest-row compact tooth supports remain C1 without neighbor/all-tooth scans or per-sample allocation/hash/square root/trigonometry. One tile spans 0.6 m with 0.004 m relief; maps/height/Worker/cache/tint/ownership stay unchanged. `weavingshuttles` adds nine seeded pointed hollow wooden boat shuttles with longitudinal grain, twelve raised brighter smoother cream yarn windings, two exposed lower metal spindle ends and opaque-backed side clearances. Squared parabolic-width tips, capsule/rounded profiles and nearest-row compact windings stay C1 without allocations/hash/square root/trigonometry/neighbor scans. Wood grain is sampled only on frames and full yarn skips hidden spindle finishing. Product complements retain full-body exact mortar0; only exposed spindle uses metal1, with public factor1 preserving mixed maps and wood/yarn/backing metal0. One tile spans 0.6 m with 0.004 m relief; maps/height/Worker/cache/tint/ownership stay unchanged. No transparency, geometry, weaving/feed/tension physics or craft certification is added.

`tailorstapes` adds four continuous unnumbered cream woven ribbons with dual-edge black graduations and blank central cloth lanes. Each edge has one hundred positions, eight short/one medium/one long per ten. Existing squared warp/weft and compact exposure preserve microrelief beneath nominal 2 μm darker/smoother ink. A cached ten-entry length table selects only the nearest compact tick; strips, tick owners, mark ends, weave parity and UV joins remain C1 without per-sample allocation/hash/square root/trigonometry/neighbor scans. Empty strips/ticks/ink skip later work. All metal values are zero and full cloth mortar0; 0.1 m tile/0.001 m relief, unchanged maps/height/Worker/cache/tint/ownership. No numbers, font assets, measurement API, real calibration, cloth simulation or new geometry is added.

`woodenclothespins` adds nine seeded stylized open-jaw pegs with continuous longitudinal wood grain, a flared central slit and three higher brighter smoother exposed metal spring wraps. Compact half-plane jaws, smoothstep flares and nearest-row squared capsules stay C1 without per-sample allocation/hash/square root/trigonometry/neighbor scans; full wire skips hidden wood grain and empty bodies skip finishing. Product complements retain full-body exact mortar0. Only wire coverage uses metal1, with public factor1 preserving mixed maps and wood/backing metal0. Slit/inter-wrap clearances expose low opaque backing. One tile spans 0.6 m with 0.004 m relief and unchanged maps/height/Worker/cache/tint/ownership; no transparency, geometry, clamping/spring/force physics or product certification is added.

`cinnamonbarkcoils` adds nine seeded open three-and-half-turn bark spirals, with lower darker rougher outer-flank bark and true opaque-backed center/inter-turn clearances rather than a filled pastry or concentric rings. Nearest unwrapped-turn ownership, annular culling and compact ends remain C1 across angle branches and owner changes. `compactcassettefaces` adds nine seeded dark rounded cassette faces with two lower hollow six-spoke plastic reels, a raised blank paper title label, three lower access openings and a visible lower magnetic ribbon. Nearest wheel/axis ownership, squared rounded/circular/capsule profiles and product complements retain C1/full-body exact mortar0. All metal0, opaque-backed cutouts, 0.45 m tile/0.004 m relief and unchanged maps/height/Worker/cache/tint/ownership. The unprinted label is intentional, not a text API; no font assets, transparency, geometry, recording/playback, magnetic/transport physics or audio-module feature is added.

`kintsugiceramic` tiles sixteen seed-jittered dark teal glazed ceramic shards with a continuous coplanar gold-filled fracture network. Ceramic interiors are nonmetallic; full gold seam cores are metallic, with smooth mixed shoulders and material factor 1 so the map remains active. All phases share the same micro-height and AO 0.99: the filled repair is neither an empty crack nor a raised wire. Cached periodic nine-site patches, symmetric pair-plane normalizations, per-shard tones and 128² grain avoid per-point allocation, hashing, square roots and trigonometry. Each point finds its nearest site, then multiplies every competing C1 half-plane rather than selecting a second-nearest seam; full gold cores conceal nearest-owner changes and overlapping triple-junction shoulders remain C1. Jitter 0.1 and support 0.03 leave discarded competitors inactive at patch-owner changes. A 0.32 m tile and 0.0003 m relief reuse four-map/optional-height, Worker/cache/tint and resource ownership contracts. This is an authored repair appearance, not fracture/repair simulation, displaced geometry, gold chemistry, a new layered coating or BRDF-anisotropy feature.

`pleatedfilterpapers` adds nine seeded cream paper filter faces with continuous flat microtextured bases, rising thirty-two-pleat annular walls and phase-matched scalloped rims. Ridge/valley height, color, roughness and AO change together; every paper phase is nonmetallic with exact full-body mortar 0, while the outer scallop gaps reveal lower opaque backing rather than transparent holes. Cached poses and 128² grain, squared radial bounds, compact C1 rise profiles and a rational cos(2θ) followed by four angle doublings produce the 32-fold profile without per-point allocation, hashing, square roots, atan2 or trigonometry. The flat center hides the angular pole; full interior paper skips rim coverage and empty support skips grain/finish. The 0.36 m tile and 0.004 m relief reuse four-map/optional-height, Worker/cache/tint and resource ownership contracts. This is a paper-filter surface appearance, not actual cup geometry, pore-scale paper structure, permeability or filtration/fluid simulation.

`splitcedarshakes` lays twenty-four warm cedar shake faces in four half-staggered courses of six variable-width boards. Longitudinal 96×8 grain, grain-correlated uneven lower edges, thicker tapered butts and two seed-positioned round-ended splits open through each butt distinguish the wood surface from mineral-grit `roofshingles` and straight `shiplap`. Cached piece widths/tones/split anchors, compact C1 half-plane and squared capsule profiles, and a fixed course-center grain slice for the frayed edge avoid per-point allocation, hashing, square roots, trigonometry and neighbor/all-board scans. Empty support skips hidden finish; all wood is nonmetallic with exact full-body mortar 0. Open splits and course gutters reveal lower opaque backing, not transparent holes or a new layer system. No crystal geometry, displacement, transmission or geological simulation is added.

`turnbuckles` adds nine seeded rotated steel eye-eye tensioners: a closed elongated frame, recessed open center, connected lower threaded rods and closed end rings. Openings remain opaque dark nonmetal backing, not transparency. Mirrored thread grooves have lower height, darker pigment and higher roughness; the metal mask uses factor 1. Tile size is 0.24 m with 0.003 m relief and zero mortar. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no geometry, true holes, displacement or mechanical simulation is added.

`rhodochrosite` adds a rose matrix with six wavy broad white laminae and fine companion bands, rather than closed agate rings. Cached 3×3 and 9×3 noise warp a continuous six-period phase; compact squared broad/fine shoulders stay C1. Separate 12² pigment and 128² grain fields retain one polished plane with micro-height variation≤0.24 μm and AO 1. Laminae change pigment/roughness, not carved relief. All pixels remain opaque metallic-zero/mortar-zero, with a 0.18 m tile and 0.00012 m relief. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no crystal geometry, displacement, transmission or geological simulation is added.

`bayleaf` adds nine seeded rotated olive pointed smooth blades, short petioles, raised midribs, seven recurved side-vein pairs and fine marginal veins. Compact blade/stem coverage and smooth squared shoulders stay C1; axis fading removes the absolute-coordinate cusp beneath the midrib. Setup caches four-value poses, seven vein roots and 128² grain. Grain micro-height variation≤6.4 μm stays below 40 μm side veins instead of hiding them. All pixels remain opaque metallic-zero/mortar-zero, with a 0.24 m tile and 0.0016 m relief. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no leaf geometry, transparent cutout, SSS or plant simulation is added.

`gabardine` adds khaki dense warp-faced two-over-one steep twill. It reuses poplin compact yarn coverage, crossing lift and fiber sampling, but advances a three-crossing draft by one warp per pick on 48 warp×24 weft pitches, giving an approximately 63.4° diagonal rather than plain weave or equal-pitch two-over-two twill. Warp radius/core is 0.495/0.44 versus weft 0.25/0.18. Both yarns use base/crown 0.34/0.04 and ±0.1 lift fading before owner changes. Cached 128² grain and 192/96-frequency fibers require no per-point allocation or trig. All pixels remain opaque metallic-zero/mortar-zero, with a 0.018 m tile and 0.0003 m relief. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no yarn geometry, transparent holes or cloth simulation is added.

`oilspotglaze` adds 64 seeded elliptical iron-oxide spots with brown interiors, brighter annular rims and fine crystalline pigment on glossy black-brown ceramic glaze. Seven cached pose values store cos/sin, two offsets, two inverse squared radii and tone. Maximum radius 0.34 plus jitter 0.1 stays below 0.5; compact spot/rim shoulders vanish C1 before owners change, with no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. Cached 128² grain supplies one glaze plane with micro-height variation≤0.24 μm; 64² crystal noise changes pigment only and AO stays 1. Bright brown markings remain dielectric, not metal rings. All pixels remain opaque metallic-zero/mortar-zero, with a 0.16 m tile and 0.00012 m relief. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no crystal geometry, displacement, transmission or kiln simulation is added.

`persimmonslice` adds nine seeded rotated orange cut discs with eight separate radial dark seeds, pale centers and thin darker rinds. Following the existing fruit flush-cut contract, seeds/pulp/rind share one cut plane with grain micro-height variation≤8 μm; seeds change pigment and roughness, not open cavities. Setup caches four-value poses, eight axis pairs, 128² grain and inverse squared constants. Radius 0.38 plus jitter 0.025 stays below 0.5; compact smooth disc/rind/pith/seed shoulders remain C1 without per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. All pixels remain opaque metallic-zero/mortar-zero, with a 0.24 m tile and 0.002 m relief. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no fruit geometry, transparent pulp, SSS or growth simulation is added.

`kitkattiles` adds 24 columns×4 rows of stacked jade narrow finger tiles with 6:1 pitch, genuinely concave longitudinal glaze troughs and equal-UV-width neutral gray grout. Unlike convex wood slats or flat subway tiles, trough centers sit 0.48 mm below both shoulders and fade at each end. Four compact smooth face edges follow subwaytiles; mortar is 1-face. Cached 96 owner tones and 128² grain require no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt; grain micro-height variation≤6 μm. All pixels remain opaque metallic-zero, with a 0.6 m tile and 0.003 m relief. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no geometry, displacement, transparency or tile-layout simulation is added.

`brakediscs` adds nine seeded rotated silver disc faces with a central bore, five mounting holes, two half-step-staggered rings of twelve ventilation holes, raised center hubs and fine concentric wear. Holes and exterior remain opaque dark metallic-zero backing; the steel mask uses factor 1. Squared annular faces and compact hole shoulders follow bearing conventions. Setup caches two-value poses, 29 three-value hole records and 128² grain; no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt is added. Compact periodic wear in squared radius avoids center singularities and is only 4 μm deep, not coarse carved grooves. Mortar stays zero; tile size is 0.72 m with 0.004 m relief. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no geometry, transparent holes, displacement or brake physics is added.

`mulberrypaper` adds warm cream cloudy pulp with 192 randomly oriented long curved bast fibers and occasional tan inclusions, not woven strips or folded paper. Following compact paper-fiber neighborhood sampling, separate light/dark complement unions bound intersecting fibers without additive height spikes. Eight cached values per fiber, three fibers per 8×8 cell and fixed 3×3 lookup require no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. Maximum half-length 0.9, bend≤0.04 and width≤0.03 give support<0.942; omitted cells start at distance 1.15, so fibers vanish C1 before owners change. Squared axial envelopes and side caps taper both tips. Fiber lift≤7 μm and total micro-height variation≤13.6 μm retain AO 1. All pixels remain opaque metallic-zero/mortar-zero, with a 0.16 m tile and 0.0002 m relief. Optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership are unchanged; no fiber geometry, translucent paper, SSS or papermaking simulation is added.

`howlite` adds ivory mineral blocks separated by fine irregular gray matrix veins. Two cached site values per 12×12 cell and paired periodic 4² warp fields distort the network. Normalized overlap of compact eighth-power radial weights in a fixed 3×3 neighborhood replaces nearest-site switching; the gray veins are not carved grooves. Support1.2 vanishes C1 before omitted sites at distance1.3, while global coverage prevents division by zero. No per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. A common polished plane has only ≤0.2 μm grain relief and AO1; matrix veins affect color and roughness only. A 0.24 m tile/0.0001 m relief retains opaque M0/mortar0 and existing options/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparent cracks, displacement or mineral simulation.

`ottoman` adds purple transverse ribs made of paired coarse weft picks bound by fine warps, with recessed inter-rib gaps. It reuses poplin/gabardine compact yarn coverage, fading lift and fiber sampling: 48 warps/24 wefts share one draft across two consecutive picks, reverse on the next pair and alternate on adjacent warps. Each pair shifts inward by 0.1 pitch, giving 0.8 within-pair and 1.2 between-pair spacing. Weft support0.38 and lift half0.36 vanish C1 before the nearest owner boundary at0.4. Lift0.18 puts over-yarn above under-yarn at crossings; elevated coarse weft base/crown forms the transverse ribs. A 0.024 m tile/0.0004 m relief retains opaque M0/mortar0 and existing options/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparent yarn gaps, anisotropy or cloth simulation.

`shackles` adds nine seeded rotated silver U-bow shackles with rounded tubing, two enlarged end bosses, one raised cross-pin and a drilled flattened pin tab. It reuses isolated hardware pose caching, compact unions and normalized height to avoid double thickness at joints. A continuous nearest-curve distance joins the upper semicircle to two straight legs, using one necessary hypot per point and no per-point allocation, hash or trig. All supports including jitter remain inside tile owners; bow, bosses, pin, tab and hole are C1. The center and tab hole are opaque M0 dark backing, with metal face masks multiplied by factor1 and mortar0. A 0.6 m tile/0.004 m relief retains existing options/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparent holes, displacement, screw operation or load-bearing physics.

`watercaltrops` adds nine seeded rotated black-brown rounded shells with two symmetric upward-curved tapered horns, fine longitudinal wrinkles and a low stem scar. It reuses rambutanskin compact curved tapered supports and isolated pose caching, but joins paired thick horns to a central shell. Smooth centerline bending, finite tip width and C1 root/tip fades combine with bounded union/normalized relief to avoid root height spikes. Conservative support radius<0.465 remains below0.5 including jitter; no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. A 0.24 m tile/0.003 m relief retains opaque M0/mortar0 and existing height/tint/repeats/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparent backing, displacement or plant-growth simulation.

`fluorite` adds nine seeded rotated purple crystal faces with nested nearly square growth-color zones in a pale mineral matrix, not circular agate rings or raised square tiles. It reuses isolated crystal poses and a common polished mineral plane with six cached values per crystal. Eighth-power polynomial contours avoid max-axis derivative cusps; four polynomial zoning periods remain C1 at phase wraps. Maximum half-extent0.335 gives support radius<0.435, below0.5 including jitter; no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. Zones affect color/roughness only, with ≤0.2 μm grain variation, AO1 and opaque M0/mortar0 on one polished plane. A 0.06 m tile/0.0001 m relief retains existing options/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparency/refraction, fluorescence, displacement or crystal-growth simulation.

`wenge` adds dark straight wood grain, pale longitudinal parenchyma bands and 192 elongated open vessel pores, not recolored generic grain or raised cloth ribs. It reuses compact wood-pore and cached longitudinal-noise sampling: four cached values per 24×8 pore, half-width0.035–0.055 and half-length0.15–0.35 vanish C1 inside their owners. Three cached noise fields need no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. Vessel pores are genuinely32 μm lower, rougher and more occluded; pale bands affect pigment only, with ≤1.2 μm grain variation. A 0.12 m tile/0.0004 m relief retains opaque M0/mortar0 and existing options/height/tint/repeats/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparent holes, displacement, anisotropy or wood-growth simulation.

`parchment` adds cream cloudy writing skin, fine longitudinal scraped color marks and 256 randomly oriented shallow residual follicles, not coarse pebbled leather or pulp fibers. It reuses compact elliptical pores and cached noise with six values per 16×16 follicle.
