Maximum support0.08 vanishes before the nearest owner boundary at0.2; no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. Scraping affects pigment without deep grooves; follicles are2.4 μm lower, cloudy skin variation≤3 μm plus grain≤0.4 μm retains AO≥0.96. A 0.04 m tile/0.0002 m relief retains opaque M0/mortar0 and existing options/height/tint/repeats/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparency/transmission, displacement, text or parchment-making simulation.

`cavalrytwill` adds olive paired broad/narrow steep diagonal wales. It reuses poplin/gabardine/ottoman compact yarn coverage, lift and fiber sampling: 72 warps/36 wefts form an approximately63.4° slope. A six-step warp-face110100 draft creates two unequal-width wales, advancing one warp per row; this is a surface pattern, not certification of a historical weaving specification. Warp/weft radii0.495/0.25 and cores0.44/0.18 retain C1 lift fading before ownership boundaries, with no per-point allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. A 0.024 m tile/0.0003 m relief retains opaque M0/mortar0 and existing options/height/tint/repeats/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparent yarn gaps, anisotropy or cloth simulation.

`screwhooks` adds nine silver open ring hooks connected to tapered threaded shanks. Compact C1 annulus and thread-phase masks leave the open mouths, ring cavities and background opaque nonmetal recesses; steel uses metallic factor1. Approximately0.00016 UV-height thread grooves darken pigment/occlusion and raise roughness. A positive tip-distance width factor avoids terminal floating-point cancellation/division by zero; normalized overlaps avoid doubled height. The0.36 m reference tile/0.004 UV relief reuses existing options, Worker, cache and ownership without per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. No geometry, transparent openings, screw mating or physics simulation is added.

`nigellaseeds` adds25 randomly rotated charcoal seeds with elongated three-sided angular coats, rather than round grains or tall-ribbed buckwheat hulls. Products of compact C1 halfplanes use aspect0.9/1.15: support≤0.368 plus jitter0.015 stays inside the0.5 owner. Five cached values per seed and128² grain/256² pore fields produce0.0009375 UV-height recessed pits (22.5μm only at the0.024 m reference scale, not measured displacement) that darken pigment/occlusion and raise roughness without piling particles at edges. Opaque metallic0/mortar0 maps use a0.024 m reference tile/0.0375 UV relief and existing options, Worker, cache and ownership; no per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry, square roots, geometry, transparent holes or biological simulation.

`phyllite` adds 48 fine wavy foliation ridges and satin mica flecks to gray-green rock, not slate terraces or broad gneiss bands. Cached 4² warp and 128² grain/mica fields drive compact C1 periodic ridges without per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. Mica changes pigment and lowers roughness, without extra height or metallic response. A 0.18 m reference tile uses 0.018 UV relief; ridge contrast is 0.00117 UV (about 210.6μm only at that reference scale, not measured displacement). Opaque metallic0/mortar0 maps retain options, Worker, cache and ownership, without geometry, displacement, anisotropy, interference colors or geological simulation.

`shantung` adds warm ivory 64-warp/32-weft 1/1 plain weave with 96 irregular raised weft slubs. It reuses the compact yarn/lift path of poplin, gabardine, Ottoman and cavalry twill; each of three segments per weft caches its center, inverse squared half-length and strength. C1 slubs thicken and widen the weft radius from0.22 up to0.37 (core0.15 up to0.28), staying inside the0.5 owner. Warp-over crossings occlude the slubs; lift fades before ownership changes. A 0.024 m reference tile/0.018 UV relief keeps opaque metallic0/mortar0 maps and existing options, Worker, cache and ownership. No per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry, square roots, geometry, transparent yarn gaps, anisotropy or cloth simulation.

`hempcrete` adds a pale porous mineral binder with 144 embedded brown fibrous hemp shivs, rather than overlapping OSB flakes. Seven cached values per shiv drive compact x⁸+y⁴ rounded strips, with 128² grain/96² pore fields. Maximum support about0.391 plus jitter0.03 stays within the0.5 owner; no per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. Shiv coats stay nearly flush without piled height or binder pores drilling through them. Pores recess by at most0.0036 UV. A 0.24 m reference tile/0.015 UV relief corresponds to at most0.864 mm pore depth only at that reference scale, not measured displacement. Opaque metallic0/mortar0 maps retain options, Worker, cache and ownership without geometry, transparent holes, physics or structural/thermal certification.

`utilityblades` adds nine silver trapezoid blade faces with two open mounting notches in the short back edge and only one honed longer lower edge, not double-edge razors. Four compact C1 linear halfplanes and two notch masks use five cached values per blade; support about0.404 plus jitter0.008 stays inside the0.5 owner without per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. Steel uses metallic factor1 while notches/background are opaque metallic0 recesses with mortar0. A 0.24 m reference tile/0.004 UV relief gives a0.00052 UV hone height contrast (124.8μm only at that reference scale, not measured displacement). Existing options, Worker, cache and ownership remain; no geometry, transparent cuts, cutting or mechanical simulation.

`chrysocolla` adds 36 turquoise clusters containing 108 joined rounded nodules over a dark green matrix, not metallic botryoidal copper or polished growth bands. Eight cached values per cluster and 128² grain/64² pigment fields drive three compact C1 circular faces; normalized dome averaging retains saddles without adding overlapping heights. Support at most 0.38 plus jitter 0.025 stays inside the 0.5 owner without per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. A 0.12 m reference tile/0.012 UV relief retains opaque metallic factor 0/mortar 0 maps, options, Worker, cache and ownership without geometry, displacement, transmission, metallic optics or chemical/geological simulation.

`sichuanpepperhusks` adds 49 rough reddish-brown empty pericarps with compact C1 squared-distance ring walls, continuously warped irregular contours and an outward split; opposite attachment scars are lower, darker and rougher, unlike whole peppercorns or transparent holes. Six cached pose values per husk and 128² grain/40² wrinkle fields keep support at most 0.385 plus jitter 0.03 inside the 0.5 owner without per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. A 0.05 m reference tile/0.015 UV relief gives pore depth 0.000825 UV and scar depth 0.0024 UV (41.25μm and 120μm by reference scale only, not measured displacement). Opaque metallic factor 0/mortar 0 maps retain options, Worker, cache and ownership without geometry, transparent cutouts, plant growth or chemical simulation.

`huckaback` adds 40×40 warp/weft yarns with a 10×10 draft and 32 diagonally alternating pairs of five-pick warp floats (64 long floats), not waffle pits or five disconnected crossing beads. It reuses the formal cloth sampler; this preset still allocates 600 Float64 values/4800 bytes for whole equal-draft-run offsets, squared half-spans and inverse shoulders. Long floats stay continuous across intermediate owners and fade C1 at run ends; Lyocell and Hopsack each reuse the same whole-run profile builder with 96 values, while the other pre-existing members of this cloth family still allocate no whole-run profile buffer. A 128² grain field and 160×160 fiber periods need no per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. A 0.032 m reference tile/0.022 UV relief gives one-sided lift 0.00308 UV (98.56μm by reference scale only, not measured displacement). Opaque metallic factor 0/mortar 0 maps retain options, Worker, cache and ownership without yarn geometry, pile loops, transmission, water absorption or anisotropic optics.

`anorthosite` adds a polished cut dominated by pale gray plagioclase grains, sparse dark accessories and grain-local parallel twin-albedo lamellae, reusing the formal gabbro grain sampler rather than a mafic matrix or raised mineral tiles. Six cached values for each of 100 sites and a 128² grain field drive normalized sixteenth-power compact weights over a 3×3 neighborhood. Radius 1.2 stays below omitted-site distance 1.3; own-site squared distance at most 0.98 keeps weight positive without per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. A 0.2 m reference tile/0.004 UV relief limits micrograin height span to 0.000008 UV (1.6μm by reference scale only, not measured displacement); twins and grain boundaries share one flat cut plane. Opaque metallic factor 0/AO 1/mortar 0 maps retain options, Worker, cache and ownership without geometry, transmission, Schiller/birefringent optics, geological simulation or certified mineral proportions.

`cubebberries` adds 64 dark brown wrinkled pepper berries with connected narrow woody pedicels, not stalkless peppercorns or detached tails. A compact C1 ellipse covers the capsule pedicel root with bounded coverage; at full berry coverage the berry owns neck height rather than adding or averaging both complete layer heights. Six cached pose values per berry and 128² grain/64² wrinkle fields keep per-axis support at most 0.468×0.98 plus jitter 0.025 = 0.48364 inside the 0.5 owner without per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry or square roots. A 0.08 m reference tile/0.04 UV relief gives wrinkle height 0.0022 UV/pore depth 0.0012 UV (176μm/96μm by reference scale only, not measured displacement). The berry is rougher than its woody tail; opaque metallic factor 0/mortar 0 maps retain options, Worker, cache and ownership without geometry, transmission, botanical or chemical simulation.

`seraphinite` provides a procedural polished deep-green face with pale curved mineral feather bands: 16 bundles vary in orientation, length, width and frequency and overlap through a periodic 3×3 neighborhood rather than being clipped into owner-local leaf stickers. Nine cached values per bundle use 144 Float64 values / 1152 bytes, with 8² matrix / 12² warp / 128² grain fields and no per-sample allocation, hash, trig or sqrt. It reuses the existing feather-axis fade and mineral-neighborhood patterns: C1 axis fading removes the absolute-distance cusp, squared-distance fine veins vanish before phase wraps, and maximum support 1.1 stays below the nearest omitted site at 1.3. The 0.16 m reference tile and 0.006 UV relief bound cut-face micrograin height variation to 0.000012 UV (1.92 μm at the reference scale, not displacement or a device measurement). Mineral feathers affect albedo, not raised leaf geometry. All maps remain opaque M0 / AO1 / mortar0 with unchanged options, Worker, cache and ownership; no geometry, transmission, directional chatoyancy, Schiller or anisotropic optical simulation is added.

`hexsocketheads` provides 25 seed-rotated round steel bolt heads with blind, flat-bottomed six-flat hex sockets. It reuses the existing round-head support and hex half-plane patterns: three paired C1 half-plane profiles multiply to retain six flats, six extended corners and smooth bevels without a max-distance corner crease. Five cached pose values per head use 125 Float64 values / 1000 bytes, a 128² grain field and a fixed hex constant evaluated at initialization; no per-sample allocation, hash, trig or sqrt is added. Cap support 0.38 plus jitter 0.025 is 0.405, below owner half-width 0.5; socket corner radius about 0.207846 stays inside the full top-face radius 0.33. The 0.18 m reference tile and 0.03 UV relief give nominal recess depth 0.0126 UV and bound flat-floor micrograin variation to 0.00006 UV (2.268 mm and 10.8 μm at the reference scale, not displacement or device measurements). The blind floor remains M1 above neutral M0 backing, blending between top and floor rather than cutting through the backing. All pixels are opaque; mortar is one minus cap coverage, and default `createMaterial()` metallic factor 1 preserves the regional metal map. Options, Worker, cache and ownership stay unchanged; no geometry, shaft, screw thread, physical perforation or load certification is added.

`datepits` provides 25 elongated tan date stones with raised cheeks and a finite curved longitudinal furrow. It reuses cached seed poses and closed squared-distance support: a finite polynomial bow offsets the ellipse, and the groove fades C1 before both tips without per-sample allocation, hash, trig, sqrt or division by a vanishing tip width. The furrow is not a coffee-bean split or a through-hole. Five cached values per pit use 125 Float64 values / 1000 bytes and a 128² grain field; conservative rotated support about 0.431937 plus per-axis jitter 0.025 remains below owner half-width 0.5. The 0.08 m reference tile and 0.025 UV relief give nominal furrow depth 0.00475 UV and micrograin height span 0.0003 UV (380 μm and 24 μm at the reference scale, not displacement or device measurements). The darker, rougher, lower-AO groove floor remains above the background and both tips stay closed. All pixels are opaque M0 / mortar0 with unchanged options, Worker, cache and ownership; no geometry, botanical, food or chemical simulation is added.

`sapele` provides a reddish-brown quartersawn ribbon-figure approximation with broad light/dark longitudinal bands and fine shallow open vessels, directly reusing the production wenge sampler rather than adding a duplicate branch. Four cached values for each of 320 pores use 1280 Float64 values / 10240 bytes, with 3×4 warp / 64×4 fiber / 128² grain fields. Seven broad C1 squared-distance bands and compact elongated pore supports remain UV-periodic: maximum x support 0.044 plus site maximum 0.75 and maximum y support 0.26 plus site maximum 0.6 both stay below owner edge 1. No per-sample allocation, hash, trig or sqrt is added. The 0.3 m reference tile and 0.002 UV relief give pore depth 0.00016 UV and flat-face micrograin height span 0.000006 UV (48 μm and 1.8 μm at the reference scale, not displacement or device measurements). Broad wood ribbons affect albedo, not raised ribs; fine vessels are genuine shallow depressions with higher roughness and lower AO. All pixels are opaque M0 / mortar0, with unchanged options, Worker, cache and ownership and no geometry, tree growth, anisotropic or chatoyant optical simulation; the preset represents fixed ribbon color rather than certified moving reflections.

Added `chambray`, a blue-dyed-warp / white-weft balanced 1/1 plain weave with 32×32 equal-width yarns and alternating exposure at every crossing, not denim twill or a float draft. It reuses the production poplin sampler with equal 0.42 supports / 0.3 cores, 0.34 bases / 0.04 crowns and C1 crossing lifts; only chambray selects warp tone 0.18 and weft tone 0.92, leaving previous cloth calculations unchanged. It uses the shared 128² grain / 128-directional fiber profiles, no extra pose or draft cache and no new per-sample allocation. A 0.016 m reference tile and 0.012 UV relief give 0.00168 UV crossing lift (26.88μm when converted using the reference scale, not displacement or a device measurement). Opaque nonmetal / mortar0; options, Worker, cache and ownership contracts are unchanged. Equal-height yarns are not poplin ribs; cloth dynamics and anisotropic optics are not implemented.

Added `nephrite`, an opaque polished-nephrite approximation with muted mottled greens and fine interlocking mineral color. A 6² cloud, two 4² warp lattices, 96×8 / 8×96 axial and 64×8 integer-diagonal fiber lattices use a 0.07 UV warp and C1 threshold blends; 128² grain retains a flat micro-polish. Mineral color does not form raised yarns, separated coarse grains or recessed cracks: AO1, opaque nonmetal / mortar0. Translucency, subsurface scattering, jade toughness and anisotropic optics are not simulated. Seeded lattices are cached once, with no per-sample allocation / hash / trig / sqrt; integer diagonal coordinates and periodic warps preserve signed UV tiling. A 0.16 m reference tile / 0.003 UV relief gives a 0.000006 UV micrograin height span (0.96μm when converted using the reference scale, not geometry, displacement or a device measurement). Existing options, Worker, cache and ownership contracts are unchanged.

Added `diorite`, a polished salt-and-pepper coarse-stone approximation with 256 jittered mineral sites, abundant pale plagioclase-like color and substantial dark mafic color. The 0.35 pale threshold assigns seeded pigment classes, not geological volume fractions. It directly reuses the production gabbro / anorthosite sampler: normalized compact cap¹⁶ weights in a 3×3 neighborhood, six cached values per site (1536 Float64 values / 12288 bytes) and 128² grain. Radius1.2 vanishes before omitted sites can approach within1.3; the own site has squared distance at most0.98, keeping the denominator positive. Grain-local ten-cycle twin lamellae affect albedo only, not the polished cut plane. A 0.16 m reference tile / 0.004 UV relief gives a 0.000008 UV micrograin span (1.28μm at the reference scale, not geometry, displacement or a device measurement). Opaque nonmetal / AO1 / mortar0, no per-sample allocation / hash / trig / sqrt; options, Worker, cache and ownership remain unchanged. Mineral composition and anisotropic optics are not simulated.

Added `carawayseeds`, 64 narrow brown crescent-shaped caraway fruits with five paler converging longitudinal ridges. Bend0.16 exceeds halfWidth0.065; finite polynomial taper closes the pointed tips without dividing by a vanishing width. It reuses the production fennelseeds sampler, with five cached pose values per fruit (320 Float64 values / 2560 bytes) and 96² grain, no per-sample allocation / hash / trig / sqrt. Conservative rotated support sqrt((0.16+0.065)²+0.38²)+0.025 is about0.4666, below the owner half-width0.5, preserving seeded signed UV tiling. A 0.04 m reference tile / 0.02 UV relief gives nominal length0.0038 m and rib lift0.0011 UV (44μm at the reference scale, not geometry, displacement or a device measurement). Opaque nonmetal / mortar0, unchanged options, Worker, cache and ownership. These fruits are commonly called seeds; plant growth and transparent gaps are not simulated.

Added `springpinends`, 36 randomly oriented slotted hollow steel tube end faces: one flat C-shaped wall around a low opaque bore with a single narrow slot continuously connecting the bore to the exterior. It is not a twin-lug retaining circlip or a blind socket. Existing cached-pose and compact squared-annulus / C1-slot patterns blend inner and outer bevels with the slot by products, avoiding angular seams or nearest-edge creases. Five values per end use 180 Float64 values / 1440 bytes plus 128² grain; no per-sample allocation / hash / trig / sqrt. Outer radius 0.36 plus jitter 0.025 remains below owner half-width 0.5. A 6×6 layout preserves visible wall samples at the supported 32² minimum map size, unlike the initially aliased 8×8 draft. A 0.064 m reference tile / 0.03 UV relief gives a 0.00006 UV face micrograin span (3.84μm at the reference scale, not displacement or a device measurement). Metal-1 walls and metal-0 backing blend continuously with mortar 1-face; the facade metallic factor is 1. Everything remains opaque: bores and slots are not transparent geometry. Press-fit deformation and elasticity are not simulated; options, Worker, cache and ownership remain unchanged.

Added `rhyolite`, a pink-brown fine-groundmass flow-banded stone approximation with 14 periodic curved pigment bands and sparse pale phenocrysts. It directly reuses the production porphyry sampler and polished cut-plane contract: 256 potential rotated crystal sites, six values per site (1536 Float64 values / 12288 bytes) and 96² grain. Only rhyolite allocates a 3² flow-warp lattice, with 0.6 phase warp and squared-distance C1 shoulders; squared radius and inverse shoulder are computed once per sampler. Maximum crystal support sqrt(0.14²+0.06²), about 0.1524, remains below the nearest owner-boundary distance 0.35; absent crystals leave no ghost mask. CrystalChance 0.25 is a seeded procedural assignment, not a geological fraction. Both matrix bands and brighter, smoother crystals remain flush rather than raising the cut plane. A 0.12 m reference tile / 0.0005 UV relief gives a 0.0000075 UV micrograin span (0.9μm at the reference scale, not geometry, displacement or a device measurement). Opaque nonmetal / mortar0, no per-sample allocation / hash / trig / sqrt. Existing porphyry allocates no flow lattice and retains its original calculations; options, Worker, cache and ownership are unchanged. Lava dynamics, mineral composition and optical certification are not implemented.

Added `tonkabeans`, 64 randomly rotated, slightly curved, closed elongated dark-brown to near-black seeds with irregular meandering longitudinal recessed drying-fold approximations and fine skin grain. These are not single-furrow date pits or stalked round cubeb berries. Existing isolated owner-cell pose, squared elliptical body and C1 shoulder patterns use five values per seed (320 Float64 values / 2560 bytes), cached 40×6 wrinkle and 3×6 warp lattices and 128² grain. Conservative support sqrt(0.36²+(0.16+0.02)²)+0.015, about 0.4175, stays below the owner half-width 0.5. Folds are simultaneously darker, lower, rougher and more occluded rather than painted-on grooves; both ends remain closed, with no cut kernel. A 0.20 m reference tile / 0.0275 UV relief gives an 18 mm nominal length and 8 mm width; wrinkleDepth 0.12 is 0.0033 UV (0.66 mm at the reference scale, not geometry, displacement or a device measurement), with a 27.5μm reference micrograin span. Opaque nonmetal / mortar0, no per-sample allocation / hash / trig / sqrt. Existing options, Worker, cache and ownership are unchanged; drying dynamics, interiors and chemical or culinary uses are not implemented.

Added `pentalobescrewheads`, 36 randomly oriented flat steel screw heads with five rounded connected blind-socket lobes and a flat steel floor, without a center security post; not the existing six-lobed starsocketbolts. Existing isolated owner-cell cached-pose, compact squared-profile and C1 product-union patterns use 180 Float64 pose values / 1440 bytes, ten Float64 lobe-center offsets / 80 bytes and 128² grain. All sin/cos runs only during sampler creation; local axes rotate only after a pixel hits a head, with no per-sample allocation / hash / trig / sqrt. Head support 0.37 + jitter 0.025 = 0.395 stays below the owner half-width 0.5; socket support 0.19 is inside the flat head core 0.35. The central floor connects all five lobes without nearest-arc creases. A 0.048 m reference tile / 0.03 UV relief gives a 5.92 mm nominal head diameter, 0.012 UV socket depth (0.576 mm at the reference scale) and 2.88μm reference micrograin span: not geometry, displacement, device measurements or P2/P5/P6 size and tool-fit certification. Steel heads and blind floors are M1 above M0 backing, mortar1-face, with facade metallic factor1. Opaque, not a transparent through-hole; options, Worker, cache and ownership are unchanged, with no fastening mechanics, tool or product-specific capability.

Added `padauk`, selecting warm red-orange straight African-padauk face grain with coarser, sparser longitudinal open vessels and 18 subtle low-contrast pigment bands, rather than seven broad reddish-brown sapele ribbons or dark wenge. It directly reuses their production sampler: 128 potential four-value pore sites (512 Float64 values / 4096 bytes), selected with seeded poreChance 0.65; unoccupied sites use zero inverse length and leave their whole owner pore-free. Longitudinal centers span 0.3–0.7 to avoid uniformly punched rows, with cached 3×4 warp, 64×4 fiber and 128² grain. Maximum half-width 0.055 is below the shortest site-to-x-boundary distance 0.25; maximum half-length 0.27 stays below the y-boundary distance 0.3. New preset bandContrast 0.09 is selected once per sampler; the two existing woods retain 0.3 and their original calculations. No extra noise field or per-sample allocation / hash / trig / sqrt. Bands remain coplanar pigment, while actual vessel recesses are darker, lower, rougher and more occluded. A 0.16 m reference tile / 0.002 UV relief gives poreDepth 0.12 = 0.00024 UV (38.4μm at the reference scale, not geometry, displacement or a device measurement), with a 0.96μm reference micrograin span. Opaque nonmetal / mortar0; options, Worker, cache and ownership are unchanged. Natural aging, UV fluorescence and wood mechanics are not implemented; procedural pore counts are not botanical measurements.

`cottonflannel` approximates solid brushed cotton flannel over a plain-woven ground; it does not define all flannel as cotton or plain weave. It directly reuses the balanced 32×32 one-over-one yarn support/lift core. Continuous short nap conceals 94% of the ground color and leaves only 4% of its height, bridging the former weave gaps rather than exposing open mesh, leather pores, or upright velvet pile. Only this kind adds cached 5×5 brush and 96×32 nap fields (3,097 Float64 values / 24,776 B); a small periodic warp creates directional short-grain variation without hard edges, reusing the existing 128×128 grain field with no per-point allocation, hashing, trigonometry, or square roots. Its 0.024 m reference tile and 0.01 UV relief give the main nap field a theoretical 0.001 UV height span (24μm at that reference scale), with a 4.8μm reference cloud span—not measured fiber length, geometry displacement, or a microscopic scan. The surface stays opaque, nonmetallic, mortar-free, highly rough, and softly occluded even over ground gaps, preserving existing options, Worker, cache, and ownership contracts without individual hair geometry, view-dependent fuzz BRDF, or cloth simulation.

`pinolith` approximates grey-included pale pointed magnesite lenses in a polished dark matrix, not carved rice or simulated mineral growth. A C1 intersection of offset ellipses, variable axes/offsets and periodic cloud warp creates varied kernels: 500 nine-value crystal poses occupy 4,500 Float64 values / 36,000 B. Each wrapped 3×3 neighborhood has a precomputed canonical opacity order in 4,500 Uint16 entries / 9,000 B, so an upper crystal hides buried outlines instead of tracing every black rim like transparent leaves; point sampling does not sort, allocate, hash, use trigonometry, or take square roots. Cached conservative per-crystal radii stay below 0.903 while omitted owners start at distance 1.1, with cached 32² cloud and 128² grain fields. A 0.1 m reference tile and 0.001 UV relief keep crystals and rims on one cut plane; only grain has a theoretical 0.000003 UV height span (0.3μm at the reference scale), not geometry displacement, a microscopic scan, or measured crystal dimensions. It remains opaque, nonmetallic, mortar-free and AO1, preserving options, Worker, cache and ownership without refraction, actual cleavage, or rock physics.

`tripointscrewheads` approximates 36 independently seeded rotated round steel screw heads, each with three straight round-ended blind channels meeting at the center at 120 degrees, not offset Tri-Wing slots or a center post. It directly shares the pentalobe head/backing core; squared-segment distances with bounded C1 product union join all three channels into one flat floor. The 180 Float64 head-pose values / 1,440 B and six Float64 unit-axis values / 48 B are prepared once with cached 128² grain. The surface sampler needs no per-point allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots. The maximum channel support radius 0.3 is inside the full head radius 0.35, and head radius plus jitter 0.395 stays inside the owner half-width 0.5. Both head and blind floor are M1 over M0 low backing, with facade metallic factor 1 and mortar equal to 1 minus face rather than the socket mask. Opaque maps and existing options/Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged. The 0.048 m reference tile and 0.03 UV relief give a 0.012 UV recess depth, equivalent to 0.576 mm at that reference scale; the theoretical grain span is 0.00006 UV, equivalent to 2.88 μm. This is shading/normal mapping only, not threads or driver geometry, dimensional Y00 fit, standards certification, torque or tamper-resistance performance.

`septarian` approximates a polished interior crop with warm grey-brown host polygons, brown linings and pale cream calcite-filled fractures, not carved open gaps or a complete round concretion. Its 36 periodic site triples occupy 108 Float64 values / 864 B. Preparation clips 48 candidate half-planes from a 7×7 neighborhood with reusable 52-vertex scratch buffers and retains only active normalized Voronoi facets. The three measured seeds each have 216 facets / 648 Float64 values; that measured count is not a new all-seed contract. Point sampling uses 5×5 candidates with a covering-radius cull: the radius is below 1.344, omitted owners start at 2.05, and omitted neighboring sites start at 3.1, beyond twice that radius. Cached 3² cloud, 12² fine fracture warp and 128² grain fields keep a conservative warp-perturbation Jacobian infinity-norm bound of 0.63 below 1. Variable-width pale fill and brown linings use C1 products of actual facet shoulders rather than nearest-plane switches, with no per-point surface-sampler allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots. The 0.12 m reference tile / 0.001 UV relief keeps all mineral phases coplanar; only grain spans a theoretical 0.000003 UV, equivalent to 0.36 μm at that reference scale. Opaque M0/mortar0/AO1 maps keep all mineral pigments tintable with existing options/Worker/cache/ownership; no geometry, transparent cavities, mineral-growth simulation or universal composition certification is added.

`squaresocketheads` has 25 independently seeded rotated round steel heads with four-flat blind square recesses, directly sharing the established hexsocketheads head/backing core. Two paired absolute-half-plane shoulders multiply to keep corners C1, not max-distance creases, six-flat drives or round holes; the old hex retains its left-associated three-mask multiplication order. The 125 Float64 pose values / 1,000 B and cached 128² grain are prepared once without an extra square-axis table or per-point surface-sampler allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots. The square support radius about 0.25456 stays within the full head radius 0.33, and head radius plus jitter 0.405 stays within the owner half-width 0.5. Head and nearly planar blind floor are M1 over M0 low backing, with facade metallic factor 1, mortar equal to 1 minus face rather than the socket mask, and opaque maps. A 0.06 m reference tile / 0.03 UV relief gives a nominal 0.0126 UV head-to-floor depth, equivalent to 0.756 mm at that reference scale; the floor-grain theoretical span is 0.00006 UV, equivalent to 3.6 μm. Existing options/Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged. This is shading/normal mapping only, not certified Robertson or driver-size fit, physical taper, threads or cam-out performance.

`bronzite` approximates a selected polished dark-brown stone face with differently oriented fine bronze striations lighting only in localized flecks. Dark grain and bright flecks share one level cut—not globally aligned engraving, blue-grey Pietersite, metallic ore or raised gold leaf. It reuses the irregular five-half-plane fragment leaf and old Pietersite arithmetic order; only the new kind adds a 96² C1 fleck gate, sampled once per point outside the neighbor loop. This is fixed pigment resembling one illumination snapshot, not view-dependent schiller/chatoyancy or an optical model. Four hundred 23-value fragment owners plus 5×7 cloud, 128² grain and 96² flecks retain 34,835 Float64 values / 278,680 bytes of tables, not total RAM. Seeded poses, trigonometry and support are cached in the constructor; points allocate nothing and use no hashes, trigonometry or square roots. The 3×3 neighborhood and circle cull have a conservative support bound about 1.224216 below the nearest omitted owner distance 1.25; five multiplied C1 shoulders and periodic fields preserve signed continuity. The authored 0.12 m nominal tile and 0.0008 UV relief are references, not metre-valued geometry displacement. Pigment never engraves height; AO one, metallic/mortar zero, opaque channels and near-flat normals agree. Optional height, tint/Worker/cache/repeats and ownership remain unchanged. No photograph scan, real geometry, dynamic sheen, transmission or mineral/species, locality, treatment, hardness, value or healing certification is supplied.

`pietersite` is a stylized polished cut of blue-grey and golden-brown cemented fibrous angular fragments, not globally parallel tiger-eye bands, metallic pigment or excavated cracks. Each of 36 irregular five-half-plane clasts has its own fiber direction, curved bands/fine fibers and blue/gold phase; 23 pose values each total 828 Float64 values / 6,624 B. Cached 5×7 cloud and 128² grain contain 16,419 values. The 3×3 owner neighborhood and circle cull use a conservative support bound 1.045164 below the nearest omitted owner distance 1.15. Five C1 shoulder products avoid nearest-plane corner creases without per-point surface-sampler allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots; needsDetail bypass remains. One authored blue-grey-to-golden ramp keeps all mineral pigment tintable, with opaque M0/mortar0/AO1. All phases share a polished plane, with only a theoretical 0.000003 UV grain-height span; the 0.06 m reference tile / 0.001 UV relief gives 0.18 μm at that reference scale, not displacement geometry or measured fiber dimensions. Existing options/Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged; view-dependent chatoyancy, anisotropy, translucency, locality authentication and geological simulation are not certified.

`castorbeans` has 16 independently seeded rotated/jittered glossy mottled oval seed coats, each with one connected paler, rougher apical caruncle—not wrinkled tonka beans, grooved date pits, paired caruncles or transparent seeds. The 80 Float64 pose values / 640 B and 16×9 spots, 5×7 warp and 128² grain (16,563 values) are prepared once. Existing oval-seed squared caps, C1 shoulders and ordered compositing keep the caruncle connected to the testa. A conservative rotated support bound 0.29+0.09 plus jitter 0.025 gives 0.405, below the owner half-width 0.5. Dark testa mottling changes pigment only, not carved height; coat and caruncle have smooth convex domes, opaque M0/mortar0. No per-point surface-sampler allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots; needsDetail bypass remains. The 0.064 m reference tile / 0.03 UV relief gives a 9.6 mm coat long axis and 2.88 mm caruncle width at that reference scale; the theoretical 0.000045 UV grain-height span corresponds to 2.88 μm. These are reference dimensions, not botanical measurement or displacement geometry. Existing options/tint/Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged, without growth, capsules, medicinal or edible functionality.

`zebrawood` is a pale golden-brown wood face with dark, varying-width gently wavy longitudinal streaks and sparse open vessels on both light and dark regions—not zebra fur, end-grain rings, carved stripes or a recolored wenge alias. It reuses existing hardwood seeded elliptical-vessel/C1-shoulder and cached-noise conventions, adding smoothly varying dark-streak widths. The streaks change pigment only; only vessels are shallow depressions, with opaque M0/mortar0 throughout. The 512 Float64 vessel-pose values / 4,096 B and 3×5 warp, 18×4 width, 64×4 fiber and 128² grain (16,727 values) are cached once. No per-point allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots; needsDetail bypass remains. At the 0.16 m reference tile / 0.002 UV relief, nominal vessel depth 0.0002 UV corresponds to 32 μm and theoretical grain-height span 0.000006 UV to 0.96 μm. These are reference dimensions, not anatomical measurement or displacement geometry. Existing options/tint/Worker/cache/ownership stay unchanged, without growth, species identification, anisotropy or dynamic chatoyancy.

`twopinscrewheads` has 25 independently seeded rotated/jittered round steel heads, each with two opposed disjoint circular blind recesses, flat metal floors and an intact central steel bridge—not a connected slot, three holes, a center pin or punched-through backing. It falls through to the existing hex/square head/backing/blind-floor core, adding two disjoint squared-circle C1 shoulders; the original four-/six-flat product order is unchanged. The 125 Float64 pose values / 1,000 B and 128² grain are initialized once. No per-point surface-sampler allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots; needsDetail bypass remains. Maximum hole support 0.222 is below full head radius 0.32; center separation 0.31 exceeds twice hole radius 0.134, leaving a 0.176 central bridge. Head radius plus jitter 0.395 is below owner half-width 0.5. Head and blind floors are M1, lower backing M0, mortar is 1−face and every map is opaque; the public facade uses metallic factor 1. At the 0.06 m reference tile / 0.03 UV relief, nominal depth 0.0129 UV corresponds to 0.774 mm and theoretical floor-grain span 0.00006 UV to 3.6 μm. These are reference dimensions, not measured mechanical dimensions, displacement geometry, screw threads or certified driver fit. Existing options/tint/Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged.

`redberyl` 近似淡色顆粒流紋岩上的覆盆子紅綠柱石，選沿晶軸看向平坦六角底面的表面視角，不是假裝重建照片中的側向高柱；不是Quartz尖錐、圓斑、六瓣花、方片、印刷或凹洞。緊湊leaf沿用既有cached noise、seeded pose與C1平方半平面肩慣例，三組相隔60°的平方strip相乘，保留六直邊及平坦凸頂；種子決定旋轉、偏移、半徑、頂高與疏密。4×4 owners的8欄pose加8² cloud及128² grain共16,576 Float64／132,608B tables，非總RAM；support平方及肩倒數在建構預算，保守owner margin約0.0556 cell、不搜尋鄰格，逐點無配置／seed hash／trig／sqrt。晶面較光滑、顆粒母岩較粗糙，M0／opaque／淺AO差異，無假黑洞；保留signed C1及既有height圖每圖正規化。Nominal tile0.18m，從root `await ProceduralMaterial.create('redberyl', { size: 512, seed: 7, heightMap: true })`使用既有Worker／cache／ownership與destroy。這是五maps外觀，不增晶柱mesh、透光／折射／多色性／散射，不認證礦種、產地／天然染色／硬度／稀有價值或療效，不打包照片。

`cottondrill` 近似暖卡其色3/1經面棉斜紋：同色經緯、連續三緯經浮長及單向逐緯推進的斜紋，不是1/1平織、2/2籃織、來回人字、藍白Ticking或畫上斜線。直接共用既有profiledWeave whole-float核心，只在建構選新preset與同色yarn tone；144經／96緯及4×4 draft保留整數週期，平方C1 cover／圓冠與完整浮長肩保留穿越owner邊界的凸起，不逐緯夾成珠。4×4×6 float profiles加128² grain共16,480 Float64／131,840B tables，非總RAM；逐點無配置／seed hash／trig／sqrt，無新增nap／slub cache。Nominal tile0.08m、default metallic0、五maps opaque，細縫是低介電底而非透明孔；淺normal、霧面roughness及密實AO，height圖仍依既有每圖min/max正規化，不能推論實際布厚。從root `await ProceduralMaterial.create('cottondrill', { size: 512, seed: 7, heightMap: true })`使用，沿用Worker／cache／ownership與destroy契約。不模擬布料、透光或纖維幾何，不認證棉含量、GSM／支數／實體密度、耐用／安全或洗護。

`bluelaceagate` 近似淡藍Blue Lace Agate磨光面：平齊藍底上有粗細不等、交替成對的白色細蕾絲波紋；不是鈷藍Lapis、灰石、同寬印刷條紋、凸白脊或凹槽。直接共用既有Rhodochrosite平齊色帶核心，不增第二個sampler；只在建構時替新種類選48×3細warp grid，原種類仍9×3、原算術次序及preset值不變，局部calcite名稱改中性的paleBand。9整數色帶週期搭配大／細平滑warp、兩種不重疊平方肩，既有C1 cache／週期wrap維持signed連續；新色帶不加入height或AO陰溝。3²／48×3／12²／128²四表共16,681 Float64／133,448B，非總RAM；逐點無配置／seed hash／trig／sqrt。名目tile0.12m、default metallic0，五maps opaque、normal接近平面；可選height仍沿既有每圖min/max正規化，不能由0–255色階推論宏觀凹凸尺度。Tint／repeats／native Worker／cache所有權不變。只近似靜態外觀，不認證礦種／天然或染色／產地／尺寸／硬度／寶石價值，不模擬半透明邊、散射、晶體光學或療效。

`linchpins` 近似銀色圓環扣Linch Pins：閉合圓線保險環、較粗實心銷柄及凸起冷鐓頭，兩側環內留暗色介電間隙；不是開口R型髮夾銷、雙腳開尾銷、平墊圈或禁止符號印刷。新小leaf沿用periodic cached noise、平方環帶／capsule及smooth cover慣例，八次superellipse收圓方頭；依序疊環、柄、頭的真height冠，不把三種表面壓成同高平片。偶數4×4交替斜向排列保留signed週期；建構時計算inverse尺寸與保守support，預設最少留0.117 cell margin，不搜鄰格，空支撐跳過geometry、零cover跳過冠計算。96² grain共9216 Float64／73,728B tables，非總RAM；逐點無配置／seed hash／trig／sqrt。名目tile0.2m、default metallic1，但metallic map只覆蓋實物、間隙為M0；五maps皆opaque，不是alpha挖空或真穿孔mesh。可選height／tint／repeats及native Worker／cache所有權不變。只近似靜態閉合外觀，不認證DIN尺寸、鍍層化學／耐蝕、承載／防脫／安全，不模擬扣合、彈簧、碰撞或轉動。

`silknoil` 近似天然奶油白、霧面的Silk Noil絹紡短纖布：細平織交替紗冠與雙向稀疏細小真凸纖維結，不是亮面繅絲Shantung、satin、暗凹孔、棕色印點或大顆粒。「Raw silk」有時是Noil商業稱呼，不表示未清洗去膠的sericin原絲。新小leaf重用periodic cached noise與平方compact support；交替紗項及斜率在owner歸零，128偶數紗列維持signed週期。48² nep owner只在建構快取admission、位置、方向、inverse尺寸及奶油至淡褐色調；平方橢圓冠真增height，smooth cover改色素／roughness／AO。最大預設支撐留0.08 cell margin、不搜鄰格。48²×6 nep表加128² grain，共30,208 Float64／241,664B tables，非總RAM；逐點無配置／seed hash／trig／sqrt。名目tile0.12m、default metallic0，五maps／可選height／tint／repeats及native Worker／cache所有權契約不變。只近似靜態外觀，不認證混紡／sericin／產地／洗護／密度／momme／垂墜／強度或安全，不模擬布料動態或真實短纖。

`bloodwood` 新增所選鮮紅Bloodwood（Brosimum rubescens／Satine）縱切面，細緻、略游移的直紋與稀疏細短真凹導管；不是橘色Padauk、紫心木、灰木、螢光紅漆、密集焦痕孔、端木或凸起燈芯絨色帶。既有疏孔木材kernel只加三處新kind admission，不改共享運算：compact squared孔支撐、constructor-only seed、共平面色帶、256×10細纖維與128² grain。48×16×4孔表加3×4 warp及fiber／grain共22,028 Float64／176,224B tables，非總RAM；最大支撐至少保留X0.185／Y0.155 owner margin。逐點無配置／seed hash／trig／sqrt／鄰格搜尋。名目tile0.12m，孔內降低height、增加roughness與輕微AO；具光澤感的紅色表面仍是介電，default metallic0。只近似選定紅料，不涵蓋所有figure，不認證樹種／產地／色彩、view-dependent chatoyancy、解剖尺寸、日照老化、木塵安全或力學性質。

`cavansite` 近似稀疏、亮藍、真凸起的短針狀放射晶簇，落在占多數的暖白粗粒礦物基底；不是磨光藍圓珠、平印花、Fluorite方晶色帶或Pentagonite長刺星形。只參考館藏標本的藍晶簇／淺底組合，opaque五maps不模擬真實晶體光學、化學、透明／螢光、礦種鑑定、產地或校準尺寸。新小leaf重用periodic cached noise與smooth compact support，以平方徑向冠及36根constructor快取、略不規則短放射針的complement product合成；徑向body邊界的色素／高度／AO皆C1。每owner的admission、旋轉、縮放與jitter只在建構時計算，預設最大支撐至少留0.155 cell margin、不搜相鄰owner。4²×6 site表、36×4 needle表、128² grain及32² host共17,648 Float64／141,184B tables，非總RAM；逐點無配置／seed hash／trig／sqrt。名目tile0.16m，藍晶簇真凸起且比基底光滑，實物含釩不代表金屬表面，default metallic0。

`matelasse` 近似暖象牙白的織成絎縫布：連續凸起菱形接結網、略蓬鬆內片及細緻交替紗冠；不是 `quilting` 的下凹虛線車縫、cathedral-window 折片、充氣軟墊格或印花菱線。參考真實菱形 Matelasse 床罩，只選 cotton-like 外觀，不認證纖維、織機結構、產地、尺寸、洗護或耐磨。獨立小 leaf 重用既有 periodic lattice 與 smooth support：雙斜向緊支撐凸肋以 bounded smooth union 合成，不用 nearest-rib 切換或交點加倍；平方內片支撐在 owner 平坦接合，交替紗冠項及其斜率在紗線 owner 歸零。128 偶數紗列與六次斜向重複維持 signed UV；唯一不可變 128² grain 表共16,384個 Float64／131,072B tables，非總RAM，逐點不配置、不 seed hash、不三角／sqrt、不掃鄰格。名目tile0.24m，default metallic multiplier0；五張一般 PBR maps 僅表達靜態視覺凹凸，不模擬布料動態、填棉或真實纖維結構。七項 consumer cases涵蓋凸接結／淺內片、相鄰經緯交替、C1肩線／owner、actual512 opaque象牙白介電maps及signed週期；整合前已跑十六個各自獨立負向與合法加寬凸肋控制。

`yellowheart` adds a selected straight-grained golden Yellowheart (Euxylophora paraensis / Pau Amarello) face with fine, low-contrast longitudinal grain and sparse short open-vessel traces. It is not orange Padauk, purple wood, neon paint, dense ring-porous stripes, raised ribbon grain, endgrain or a true satinwood identification. The existing Padauk/Iroko/Purpleheart/Sapele/Wenge kernel is reused unchanged apart from admitting the new fine-grain preset: constructor-only sparse vessel admission, compact squared ellipses, periodic warped pigment bands, 224×6 fine fibers, 128² grain and genuine shallow pore recesses. The 40×12×4 pore cache, 3×4 warp and fiber/grain lattices total 19,660 Float64 values / 157,280 B of tables, not total RAM. Maximum vessel supports leave at least 0.17/0.11 owner margins, preserving C1 joins; color grain stays on one satin plane while the sparse vessel interiors lower height, raise roughness and occlude slightly. Sampling adds no allocation, hashing, trig, square roots or neighbor scan. The nominal tile is 0.12 m. Five maps, optional height/tint/repeats, native Worker/cache, dielectric default and explicit metallic override, isolated textures and destroy contracts remain unchanged. This is a static visual approximation of selected golden stock, not species/provenance certification, calibrated color, anatomical dimensions, sunlight aging, dust safety or mechanical wood properties.

`rclips` adds a bright spring-wire R-clip approximation with a single round pull loop, one long straight insertion leg, an inward-necked bowed spring leg and a flared short tip. The eye, spring clearances and free-end gaps stay connected to the dark recessed background; the result is not a closed washer, flat split cotter or two parallel U-legs. It reuses the paperclip capsule/half-ring kernel and complement union, caching 13 five-value segments, one five-value bend and the existing 96² grain lattice: 9,286 Float64 values / 74,288 B of tables, not total RAM. A second smooth cover rounds only the R-clip wire crown, with metal following the wire mask and the empty bed remaining dielectric; the existing paperclip branch stays unchanged. Squared distances are reused between face and crown profiles. Sampling adds no allocation, hashing, trig or square roots; the maximum 0.42605 local support stays strictly inside a half-cell, and smooth capsule projections, overlapped arc shoulders, seed grain and signed UV repeats remain C1. The tile is 0.12 m and alternating ±45° clip placement is deterministic; seed varies micrograin, not the manufactured outline. Five maps, optional height/tint/repeats, native Worker/cache, explicit metallic override, isolated textures and destroy contracts remain unchanged. This is a height-field visual approximation, not a certified DIN part, dimensional drawing, material grade, spring-force, fatigue or retention simulation.

`ticking` adds a navy-and-cream mattress-ticking approximation: a broad dyed center stripe is flanked by two fine lines on a dense 2/1 warp-faced twill. The stripes follow the warp yarns, while cream weft crossings remain visible; they are neither printed solid bars nor a plaid, brushed nap, open gauze or embossed stripe relief. It reuses the existing woven-yarn kernel and whole-run float profiles, so a two-pick warp float stays raised through its intermediate owner boundary rather than turning into beads. The 3×3 draft uses 54 cached Float64 profile values plus the existing 128² grain lattice: 16,438 values / 131,504 B of tables, not total RAM; the immutable stripe draft is ordinary authoring data. Narrow yarn supports and squared smooth float profiles preserve C1 joins at yarn owners, shoulders, fibers and signed UV seams. Stripe pigment does not drive height, roughness or occlusion. Seed work stays in the constructor; sampling adds no allocations, hashes, trig or square roots. The five-map, height/tint/repeats, Worker/cache, isolated texture ownership and destroy contracts remain unchanged. The nominal tile is 0.048 m; the visual preset makes no feather-proof, textile-density, wear, fire-safety or manufacturer certification claim, and existing woven presets remain unchanged.

`sodalite` adds an opaque polished blue-to-blue-violet approximation with fine granular matrix variation, narrow irregular white calcite-like veinlets and occasional pale patches. It is not metallic pyrite, raised white ribbons, open cracks, a transparent orange-yellow specimen, fluorescence or photochromic hackmanite. A new leaf reuses the existing periodic cached-noise and smooth squared-cover primitives: two domain-warped carrier scales (11² and 43²), 6² cloud, 128² grain and two 4² warp tables total 18,422 Float64 values / 147,376 B of tables, not total RAM. Squared distance from the carrier level forms smoothly feathered filled veins, joined to pale cloud patches by a complement product; there are no nearest-site or angular seams. Seed hashing is paid only during construction; point sampling has no allocation, hash, trig, square root or neighbor scan. Pigment and modestly rougher white veins remain on one flat micrograin plane with AO 1, metallic 0 and mortar 0. The 0.12 m nominal tile and 0.0002 UV relief are authored, not calibrated mineral dimensions or optical properties. The existing CPU maps, optional height, facade controls, cache, native Worker and cleanup contracts remain unchanged.

`dalmatianstone` adds a polished creamy feldspar/quartz-like matrix with variable-size dark green-black aggregates, not raised pebbles, pores, ringed orbs or white snowflakes. The name follows the GIA paper’s recommendation of Dalmatian stone rather than the trade misnomer Dalmatian jasper; this is an authored appearance, not a chemical or provenance identification. One seeded candidate per 10×10 cell is variably admitted, rotated, sized and curved; the 128² cached grain also smoothly perturbs its squared contour, and neighboring spots can join across owner boundaries. A fixed periodic 3×3 neighborhood avoids clipped edge aggregates. With contour multiplier at least 0.6, maximum support is below 0.393 cell units, far inside the nearest omitted-site distance 1.15. The 800 pose values plus 128² grain and 12² matrix caches total 17,328 Float64 values / 138,624 B of tables, not total RAM. All pose hashing/trigonometry/inverse radii are constructor work; point sampling allocates nothing and uses no hashes, trig or square roots. Smooth covers affect pigment and slightly rougher dark inclusions only: one polished plane retains shallow micrograin height, AO 1, metallic 0 and mortar 0. The nominal 0.06 m tile and 0.002 UV relief are authored values, not calibrated specimen dimensions. CPU maps, optional height, facade controls, cache, native Worker and cleanup contracts remain unchanged.

`purpleheart` adds a static exposed eggplant-purple Purpleheart face with mostly straight medium grain and sparse, closed-ended longitudinal vessels. It represents an authored exposed-color state, not freshly cut gray/brown stock, UV/oxidation animation, fluorescence, wood identification, or calibrated physical properties. It shares the existing Padauk/Iroko/Sapele/Wenge vessel kernel, selecting the fine sparse profile at construction only; each occupied vessel has a dark recessed floor, slightly rougher walls and local AO, while all long-grain bands remain flat pigment rather than proud ribbons. The 30×10 candidate sites store 1,200 inverse-ellipse/position values, with 3×4 warp, 192×6 fiber and 128² micrograin: 18,748 Float64 values / 149,984 B of tables, not total RAM. Candidate centers and support stay inside their owner (horizontal center 0.25–0.75 with radius at most 0.09; vertical center 0.35–0.65 with radius at most 0.28), so periodic single-owner evaluation and smooth squared covers remain C1. Point sampling adds no allocation, hash, trig or square root; the existing point kernel and arithmetic order for all four sibling kinds are unchanged. Metallic and mortar stay 0. The nominal 0.12 m tile and 0.003 UV relief are authored dimensions; CPU maps, optional height, controls/cache/native Worker and cleanup follow the same contract.

`blackpalm` adds a satin-finished longitudinal Black Palm approximation: dense narrow dark fibrovascular bundles run mostly along V through tan/brown ground tissue, unlike the dotted cut bundles and recessed vessels of `palmendgrain`. There are no annual rings, knots, board seams, dark holes, or pigment-carved relief. It reuses the existing Tigerwood/Snakewood compact curved-spindle pigment kernel unchanged; seeded positions, inverse squared widths/lengths, small slopes and bends are cached at construction. The 80×12 owners with two candidate bundles each use 11,520 pose values plus 96×6 wood, 128×8 fiber and 128² micrograin caches, totaling 29,504 Float64 values / 236,032 B of tables, not total RAM. Including jitter, support is at most 0.758 owner units across and 1.38 along, below the 1.5 bound for the existing periodic 3×3 neighborhood. Smooth squared covers overlap as pigment on one fine-grained face; point sampling adds no allocation, hash, trig or square root. Metallic stays 0, AO stays 1, mortar stays 0, and roughness is satin; only the shared fine grain contributes shallow height. The 0.096 m nominal tile and 0.004 UV relief are authored choices, not calibrated fiber dimensions, wood grading, mechanical properties or a new shading model. The CPU maps, optional height, facade/cache/Worker controls and existing cleanup contract remain unchanged.

`eclips` adds stainless E retaining clips with three inward contact lugs, two broad wing windows joined to the shaft opening, and a flared side mouth—not the existing C clips with plier eyelets. Sixteen slightly rotated, jittered and scaled poses use 80 cached values plus 96² grain: 9,296 Float64 values / 74,368 B of tables, not total RAM. Compact squared-radius covers and smooth half-planes keep every clip within 0.35 cell units per axis, including jitter, below the 0.5 owner boundary. The central lug or empty bore protects the mirrored pocket's absolute-x join. Sampling has no allocation, hash, trigonometry, square root or neighbor search; empty backing skips grain. Metal face, height, roughness and AO share the same cover. Authored metallic maps are 1 on steel and 0 on opaque backing; the default material factor is 1. The nominal 0.064 m tile and 0.004 UV relief are authored, not dimensional certification. Existing maps, optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and ownership remain unchanged; no mesh holes, transparency, shaft assembly, spring mechanics or load-rating simulation is added.

`snowflakeobsidian` adds an authored polished black face with flush gray radiating inclusions, not the existing Obsidian fracture scars, raised flowers or smooth painted disks. Thirty-six seeded clusters each use 24 compact radial blades and a variable 0.4–1.1 scale; the scaled support plus jitter is at most 0.4657 cell units, below the 0.5 owner boundary. It reuses the Chrysanthemum Stone kernel with constructor-selected pigment/roughness profiles and scaled cached bases. The 3,564 geometry values plus 96² grain total 12,780 Float64 values / 102,240 B of tables, not total RAM. Sampling has no allocation, hash, trigonometry or square root. Only micrograin shapes height; the gray inclusions are slightly rougher than the black matrix. The authored 0.12 m nominal tile and 0.001 UV relief are not specimen measurements. Opaque dielectric maps (metallic 0), optional height, tint, repeats, Worker/cache and caller ownership remain unchanged; no fracture/crystal geometry, transparency, mineral identification or calibrated optical simulation is added.

`f05opticalfaces` adds four 2×2 uncapped JIS-F05-type optical receiver mating-face schematics: black matte rectangular molded-resin faces with two long side recesses, raised six-sided guides chamfered at two corners of one end, two rounded lateral bosses, dark keyed mouths and round low alignment wells. All faces are opaque dielectric; there is no shutter, LED/glow, metal contact or transparent/refractive lens. This related connector-family appearance variation is not new rendering technology or a photodiode/optical/data/audio/electrical/fit/EMC/safety simulation or certification. The catalog then contained 880 kinds (879→880).

Appearance evidence: page 5 of the actually read/rendered six-page primary [Toshiba TORX1350A(F) datasheet, 2020-03-12](<https://toshiba.semicon-storage.com/info/TORX1350A(F)_datasheet_en_20200312.pdf?did=35629&prodName=TORX1350A(F)>) shows the horizontal JIS-F05 receiver front, 13×8.5 mm—not its 13.5 mm depth or 3.5 mm protruding pins. The [primary product page](<https://toshiba.semicon-storage.com/ap-en/semiconductor/product/isolators-solid-state-relays/detail.TORX1350A(F).html>) specifies molded resin, not exact resin chemistry; the part is Not Recommended for New Design (NRND). The viewed [2014 distributor family photograph](https://rutronik-tec.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/10/Toshiba21.jpg), from its [announcement](https://rutronik-tec.com/toshiba-extends-toslink-family-with-new-energy-conserving-fibre-optic-transmission-modules/), supports approximate black pre-A TOxX1350 family appearance only, not calibrated A-model front optics. Shape, palette, roughness, layering, 0.04 m nominal tile and 0.0038 UV relief are authored approximations, not CAD/tolerance/fit/thickness or optical calibration. No source image/PDF is bundled.

The independent 56-field preset/kernel uses two rounded-rectangle and two circle squared-distance C1 profiles, two three-factor C1 six-plane masks, a product union of guide collar/ears and 19 cached channel deltas. Four four-column poses (128 B) plus 96² Float64 grain (73,728 B) retain 73,856 B typed payload, not total RAM or FPS. The owner-coordinate support bound is 0.4023056142 < 0.5. Sampling has no per-point allocation, hash, trigonometry, square root or side/slot loops. Default metallic 0 and stored metallic-roughness B=0 apply everywhere; explicit metallic 1 changes only the factor, never the stored map. Existing opaque four maps, optional height, tint/repeats, synchronous/native module Worker pool/cache paths and preset-owned texture/caller cleanup remain unchanged, as do the previous 879 kinds; no dependency, alias or API entry point is added. Prefer 512; 32 is only a coarse preview. No cap, solder/support pins, rear/PCB details, mesh/transparent holes, collision or optical propagation is modeled.

`minidin4femalefaces` adds four 2×2 four-contact mini-DIN female-face schematics, bringing the then-current catalog from 880 to 881 kinds: near-square black PBT carriers/circular bezels, continuous thin tin-plated brass earth rims, dark annular cavities and black circular inserts. Each insert has an OPEN top notch and two lower side steps; four plastic lead-ins enclose vertically elongated upper slots and horizontally elongated lower slots, with a separate CLOSED lower rectangular key opening. Only the earth rim is metallic. No visible silver internal contact annuli, outer shielding can, rear pins, switches or video/signal/electrical simulation are represented.

Primary appearance sources: the [Same Sky MD-40S product page](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/interconnect/connectors/din-connectors/md-40s), page 2 of the actually read/viewed five-page [MD-S datasheet (printed 2024-09-12)](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/resource/md-s.pdf), and the [manufacturer photograph](https://cdn.sameskydevices.com/products/image/getproductimage/976?typecode=m&width=300). The separate MD-40S four-contact front and shared housing are used—not the generic large nine-contact drawing or PCB footprints as a four-contact front. The top notch belongs to the PBT insert, not the continuous metal rim. Black PBT/tin-plated brass roles are source-grounded; proportions, color, roughness, AO, layer heights, the 0.04 m nominal tile and 0.0042 UV relief are authored approximations, not CAD/tolerance/fit/thickness/plating-optics measurements or electrical/video/EMC/safety certification. No source PDF or photograph is bundled.

The independent 87-field preset/kernel uses six rounded-rectangle geometries (shared slot radius inverse), four squared-circle C1 profiles, two smooth half-plane step gates and 32 cached C/H/R/AO deltas. Four four-column Float64 poses (128 B) plus 96² grain (73,728 B) retain 73,856 B typed-array payload, not total RAM or FPS; the single-owner support bound is 0.4189705149024927 < 0.5. Sampling has no per-query allocation, hash, trigonometry, square root or slot loop. Default metallic 1 exposes only the thin earth rim; all black plastic, floors and cavities have M0. `createMaterial({ metallic: 0 })` changes only the factor, not the stored metallic mask. Existing opaque four maps, optional height, tint/repeats and Worker/cache APIs apply: the preset owns textures; callers manage borrowing materials and caller-owned pools/caches, removing borrowing materials before destroying the preset. No dependency, alias or API entry point is added; the previous 880 algorithms/order are preserved. Prefer 512; 32 is only a coarse preview. No mesh, transparent bores or physical contacts are modeled.

`dcbarreljackfaces` adds four 2×2 DC barrel-jack front illustrations, bringing the then-current catalog from 881 to 882 kinds. Each tall black PBT rounded rectangle (9:11 aspect) has an upward-offset dark circular mouth, a SOLID recessed nickel-plated copper center pin and a SEPARATE lower silver-plated copper-alloy terminal-2 strip. The strip has flat top/bottom edges; only its ends are circle-clipped. Only these two contacts have M1; all PBT, backing and bore floors have M0. Depth order is backing < bore < lower strip < solid pin < carrier face: both contacts remain recessed below the carrier. There is no hollow center, outer metal annulus or added raised circular bezel.

Primary appearance sources are the [Same Sky PJ-102A product page](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/interconnect/connectors/dc-power-connectors/jacks/pj-102a), page 2 of the actually read/viewed three-page [PJ-102A datasheet (printed 03/11/2025, rev 1.07)](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/resource/pj-102a.pdf), and the [manufacturer front photograph](https://cdn.sameskydevices.com/products/image/getproductimage/690?typecode=m&width=300). The drawing shows a 9 × 11 mm front, Ø6.40 mm opening, Ø2 mm pin and mouth center 6.5 mm above the bottom. The catalog's 2.0 × 6.5 mm designation and the 5.5 mm mating-plug diameter are not the drawing's Ø6.40 mm opening. Shape, color, roughness, AO, layer heights, the 0.04 m nominal tile and 0.0042 UV relief are authored approximations—not CAD, fit/tolerance, electrical, polarity, switch, safety or connector simulation/certification. Rear solder legs, PCB geometry and real through-holes are omitted; floors are opaque stand-ins. No source PDF or image is bundled.

The independent 47-field preset (45 numeric fields and two palettes) uses five C1 profile factors: one rounded carrier, two squared-circle profiles for bore/solid pin, and two multiplied smooth spring-axis gates with bore clipping; sixteen C/H/R/AO deltas are cached. Four four-column Float64 poses (128 B) plus 96² Float64 grain (73,728 B) retain 73,856 B typed-array payload, excluding JS object/closure overhead—not total RAM, FPS or a performance certification. The single-owner support bound is 0.4383801121065569 < 0.5. Sampling has no per-query allocation, loop, hash, trigonometry or square root; cached grain is skipped in the full bore core, and the second fractional wrap before owner indexing is retained for tiny negative UVs. Default metallic 1 exposes only the solid pin and separate lower strip; `createMaterial({ metallic: 0 })` changes only the factor, not the stored metallic mask. Existing opaque four maps, optional height, tint/repeats and Worker/cache APIs apply: the preset owns textures; callers remove borrowing materials before destroying it and manage caller-owned pools/caches. No dependency, alias or API entry point is added; the previous 881 algorithms/order remain unchanged. Prefer 512; 32 is a coarse preview.

`stfiberadapterfaces` adds four 2×2 ST simplex adapter front schematics, bringing the current catalog from 882 to 883 kinds: six-flat plated-metal hex bases, concentric raised round collars and raised circular barrel lips. Each lip has ONE open top axial keyway and TWO opposed side bayonet studs around a deep dark opaque circular cavity. The keyway lowers ONLY the lip to the continuous metal collar; it never cuts the collar/hex base through to the background. The high lip is an open C-shape. All visible hardware, including the keyway floor, has M1; cavity/backing have M0. Depth is backing < bore < nut < collar/key floor < studs < lip. No white ceramic ring, center contact annulus or pin is drawn.

Primary appearance sources are the [SENKO ST product page](https://www.senko.com/product/st-adapter-2/), the actually read five-page [DS-ST-000006-RevA-EN datasheet](https://www.senko.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/DS-ST-000006-RevA-EN.pdf) (page 1 photograph and page 3 drawing viewed), and the primary [ST-Adapter.png](https://www.senko.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/ST-Adapter.png). This uses 216-101 simplex, not 316 duplex. Only plated metal is supported; no nickel/alloy chemistry is inferred. An internal zirconia split sleeve is specified but not clearly visible as a white ring in the primary view, so it is not drawn. The PDF metadata creation date 2024-11-15 is not a printed date. The 29.3 mm dimension is axial depth; the double-D 9.6 × 7.8 mm panel cutout is reference only, not the front shape. Proportions, color, roughness, AO, layer heights, the 0.04 m nominal tile and 0.0042 UV relief are authored for a simplified front illustration, not CAD. Threads, individual knurl teeth, washers, caps, rear parts, cable/ferrule and PCB are omitted; no optical/electrical/mating/safety simulation or certification, browser/FPS certification, or bundled source assets.

The independent 54-field preset (52 numeric fields and two palettes) uses 13 C1 profile factors: six nut support planes, three squared-circle profiles, two key-axis gates and two stud-axis gates, with 20 constructor-cached C/H/R/AO deltas. Products of smooth profiles and role replacement avoid nonzero hard-min/max joins. Four four-column Float64 poses (128 B) plus 96² Float64 grain (73,728 B) retain 73,856 B typed-array payload, excluding JS object/closure overhead—not total RAM or FPS. The rotation-independent owner radius plus jitter is 0.35841016151377547 < 0.5. Poses/grain are constructor-cached; queries have no allocation, loop, hash, trigonometry or square root, skipping zero support and unused grain in nonmetal bore/background. The second fractional wrap before owner indexing remains necessary for tiny negative UVs. Default metallic is 1; `createMaterial({ metallic: 0 })` changes only the factor, not the stored metallic mask. Existing opaque four maps, optional height, tint/repeats and Worker/cache APIs apply: the preset owns textures, callers manage borrowed materials and their own pool/cache, removing borrowed materials before disposing the preset. No new dependencies, helpers, exports, API or palette expansion. These are source/implementation descriptions, not claims of full formal tests, browser or built-distribution verification.

`to220packagefaces` adds four 2×2 front-oriented exposed-tab TO-220 package schematics, bringing the catalog from 884 to 885 kinds (886 gallery choices including plain). Each has a neutral-metal tab with TWO clipped upper corners and ONE centered dark opaque recessed mounting hole, a matte-black rectangular body masking the lower tab and lead roots, and THREE uniformly spaced parallel straight metal leads with broader root shoulders and narrower tails. Full tab/lead surfaces have M1; body, hole floor and backing have M0, with C1 boundary blends. These are surface roles, not electrical isolation. Authored normalized heights are backing .02 < hole .06 < tab .30 < lead .44 < body .68; the hole is a real height depression, not black paint or a transparent opening.

Appearance follows STMicroelectronics' [STP55NF06 exposed-tab TO-220 type A, May 2012, Doc ID7544 Rev11](https://www.st.com/resource/en/datasheet/stb55nf06.pdf#page=13): the original PDF cover artwork on page 1 and Figure 23 / 0015988_typeA_Rev_S on page 13 were actually viewed, with the dimension table read on page 12. This is NOT TO-220FP or the D²PAK variant. Drawing dimensions are references only, not measurements of this authored artwork or exact dimensioned CAD. Chemistry, plating and precise optical properties are not inferred. Printed markings, molded small circles/recesses and rear details are omitted; no source artwork is shipped. It does not simulate heat, circuits, electrical/RF behavior, safety or FPS; the source's tab-to-pin-2 connection is not modeled.

Defaults are a 0.08 m nominal tile, 0.0042 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 1. Explicit `createMaterial({ metallic: 0 })` changes only the scalar factor, never the stored M mask. Artist fields remain in the existing immutable `src/data` preset. Constructor-cached four-column Float64 poses (128 B) and 96² Float64 grain (73,728 B) retain 73,856 B typed-array payload, excluding JS object/closure overhead—not total RAM or a performance certification. The sampler reuses C1 profiles and convex layer replacement; queries have no allocation, loops, hash, trigonometry, square root or neighbor search. Existing periodic opaque maps and texture ownership remain unchanged. Prefer 512 to show the hole and three narrow lead tails; 32 is only a coarse preview and need not resolve every lead.

`mudcloth` adds hand-painted mud-cloth band schematics, bringing the current catalog from 886 to 887 kinds (888 gallery choices including plain). Eight horizontal bands repeat four authored motif families in turn: zigzag, dot rows (alternating sizes), diamonds with small center dots, and double-rail ladders with rungs. Every band boundary carries a continuous light separator line, and periodic noise wobbles the coordinates like hand painting; each band also has its own phase and stroke width. A dark brown-black dyed ground and cream undyed-cotton strokes use one scalar color ramp (ground about 0.10, strokes about 0.84, plus slight dye pooling and per-thread tone variation) over a 64×64-thread-per-tile plain weave whose alternating over/under parabolic thread faces generate height and AO. Metallic and mortar are 0.

The look is inspired by West African mud-dyed cloth (bògòlanfini): light motifs on a dark dyed ground. Motif geometry, proportions, colors and texture are authored; they are not an exact copy of any particular people's, workshop's or traditional pattern and do not claim its cultural meaning. No external artwork is cited or shipped.

Defaults are a 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.0008 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 0. Existing periodic opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache APIs and texture ownership are reused without new APIs or dependencies. Prefer 512 to resolve threads and fine strokes; 32 is a coarse preview. This description does not claim browser or three-backend verification.

`turingpattern` adds Turing-style labyrinth/spot coat-pattern schematics, bringing the current catalog from 887 to 888 kinds (889 gallery choices including plain). Three integer wave vectors (13, 12.5 and 13.0 cycles per tile) are summed as cosines into a near-hexagonal spot lattice, a periodic noise warp bends the coordinates, and a slowly varying threshold moves between small round spots (high threshold) and labyrinth networks (low threshold). Soft-edged patches are dark brown on a pale camel ground, with slightly darker, slightly raised centers. Metallic and mortar are 0.

This is an analytic three-wave approximation, not a reaction–diffusion simulation, and it does not try to reproduce the markings of any particular animal; every parameter and color is authored, and no external artwork is cited or shipped. Each sampled point costs three cosines and four periodic-noise reads, with no loops or allocations.

Defaults are a 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0007 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 0. Existing periodic opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache APIs and texture ownership are reused without new APIs or dependencies. Prefer 256 or larger for spot detail; 32 is a coarse preview. This description does not claim three-backend or cross-browser verification.

`bayerdither` adds ordered Bayer-dither pixel tile mosaics, bringing the current catalog from 888 to 889 kinds (890 gallery choices including plain). Each tile holds 64×64 square blocks; every block reads one slowly varying brightness field at its center and compares it with an 8×8 Bayer threshold matrix to become lit or dark. Lit blocks are cream-white, higher and glossier; dark blocks are deep blue-black, lower and rougher; a dark recessed groove with beveled edges separates blocks, and each block has slight tone jitter. In gentle regions of the field the result shows classic ordered-dither textures, collapsing to a checkerboard near 50% brightness. The Bayer matrix is built in the constructor with the recursion B(2n)=[[4B,4B+2],[4B+3,4B+1]]. Metallic and mortar are 0.

This is an authored pixel-art-style surface, not taken from any image or game asset, and not a tool implementation of image dithering. Defaults are a 0.32 m nominal tile, 0.0036 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 0. Existing periodic opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache APIs and texture ownership are reused without new APIs or dependencies. Prefer 512 or larger so each block spans at least 8 px; 32 is a coarse preview. This description does not claim three-backend or cross-browser verification.

`patchworkquilt` adds patchwork quilt-top schematics, bringing the current catalog from 889 to 890 kinds (891 gallery choices including plain). Each tile holds 4×4 square patches that each pick one of four hash-selected designs: plain (with small printed dots), diagonal half-square triangles, concentric square rings, or three strips, using two of five tone levels. Seams between and within patches are dark recessed grooves; each patch puffs toward its center like padded batting, with a dashed running stitch 0.07 of a patch inside the border (6 dashes per side, duty about 0.62; slightly recessed, lighter and rougher). A 96×96-thread-per-tile plain weave sits underneath. Metallic and mortar are 0.

This is an authored, simplified quilt-top: tones, designs and proportions are not an exact copy of any traditional block (such as log cabin or nine patch); no external artwork is cited or shipped. Defaults are a 0.8 m nominal tile, 0.0042 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 0. Existing periodic opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache APIs and texture ownership are reused without new APIs or dependencies. Prefer 512 or larger to resolve the dashed stitches and dots; 32 is a coarse preview. This description does not claim three-backend or cross-browser verification.

`amberresin` adds polished amber-resin schematics, bringing the current catalog from 890 to 891 kinds (892 gallery choices including plain). Stretched flow bands (honey gold to deep amber) come from periodic noise with 28 rows along v but only 4 columns along u, overlaid with two grid-keyed features: round gas bubbles (about 55% of the 6×6 cells, radius 0.022–0.05 UV, lighter inside with a thin dark rim that lowers only AO) and "sun spangle" discs (about 40% of the 4×4 cells, radius 0.07–0.10 UV, slightly lighter, with 9 radial stress cracks fading in from the nucleus; the cracks recess the height and add roughness). Overall roughness is about 0.14 (polished). Metallic and mortar are 0.

This is an authored opaque-map approximation: amber's translucency, refraction and insect inclusions are not simulated and no specific locality or specimen is modeled; no external artwork is cited or shipped. Every feature center stays inside its owning cell so the full radius never crosses a cell edge. Defaults are a 0.2 m nominal tile, 0.0006 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 0. Existing periodic opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache APIs and texture ownership are reused without new APIs or dependencies. Prefer 512 or larger to resolve the thin bubble rims; 32 is a coarse preview. This description does not claim three-backend or cross-browser verification.

`ammonitestone` adds ammonite-fossil limestone schematics, bringing the current catalog from 891 to 892 kinds (893 gallery choices including plain). Each of the 2×2 cells holds one disc-shaped ammonite with its own jittered position, scale and starting phase. The shell is a logarithmic spiral: the fractional part of whorl = ln(ρ/r₀)/ln(g) − θ/2π is the whorl cross-section coordinate, with integer values forming the recessed, AO-dark sutures and the middle a rounded tube with a central keel; 18 ribs per whorl twist falcoidally and fade in from the inner whorls to avoid aliasing. The matrix is gray-brown limestone (slow mottling plus fine grain); the shell is cream and smoother (roughness 0.55 against 0.88). The growth ratio g is 2.3, the outer shell radius is 0.4 of a cell, giving about 3.1 whorls. Metallic and mortar are 0.

This is an authored schematic fossil, not a reconstruction of any genus or specimen: the complex foliated septal sutures, aperture and diagenetic mineral differences are not modeled, and no external artwork is cited or shipped. Each fossil's radius plus its position jitter stays below half a cell, so a point only ever belongs to its own cell. Defaults are a 0.5 m nominal tile, 0.0026 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 0. Existing periodic opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache APIs and texture ownership are reused without new APIs or dependencies. Prefer 512 or larger to resolve the ribs; 32 is a coarse preview. This description does not claim three-backend or cross-browser verification.

`octopussuckers` adds octopus-arm sucker schematics, bringing the current catalog from 892 to 893 kinds (894 gallery choices including plain). Four columns by four rows of suckers, with odd rows shifted by half a column pitch, each with its own jittered position and radius (92–100% of 0.11 UV). Every sucker has three layers: a raised toroidal rim (crest at about 79% of the radius), a cup recessed down from the skin level (12 radial ridges windowed to vanish at the rim foot and the central orifice, so every channel is C1 at the foot), and a small, deep, dark orifice (clearly lower AO). The skin is wet pinkish gray with slow wrinkles and small dark speckles. Metallic and mortar are 0.

This is an authored schematic: the sucker count, row pitch, speckles and colors are not measurements of any species, and no external artwork is cited or shipped. Each sucker radius is below half the column pitch and half the row pitch, so a point only belongs to its own row and nearest sucker. Defaults are a 0.4 m nominal tile, 0.0034 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 0. Existing periodic opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache APIs and texture ownership are reused without new APIs or dependencies. Prefer 512 or larger to resolve the cup ridges; 32 is a coarse preview. This description does not claim three-backend or cross-browser verification.

`expandedpolystyrene` adds expanded-polystyrene bead schematics, bringing the current catalog from 893 to 894 kinds (895 gallery choices including plain). Each tile holds 16×16 beads placed like a periodic Voronoi diagram (one site per cell, at least 0.2 of a cell from the cell edge). The three nearest sites separate three roles: the domed bead face owned by F1 (height rising toward the bead center, each bead with its own slight tone), dark-gray fused seams where F2−F1 is below the seam width, and dark voids at three-bead junctions where F3−F1 is small. Bead faces are white with roughness 0.78, seams 0.9, and voids are darkest with the lowest AO. Metallic and mortar are 0.

This is an authored schematic: bead arrangement, size, seam width and colors are not measurements of any product or density grade, and no external artwork is cited or shipped. Because every site stays inside its own cell, the 3×3 neighborhood contains the three nearest sites and the texture is periodic on both axes. Defaults are a 0.12 m nominal tile, 0.0022 UV-relative relief (not metres of displacement) and metallic factor 0. Existing periodic opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache APIs and texture ownership are reused without new APIs or dependencies. Prefer 512 or larger to resolve the voids; 32 is a coarse preview. This description does not claim three-backend or cross-browser verification.

`asphaltshingle` adds asphalt three-tab roofing-shingle schematics, bringing the current catalog from 894 to 895 kinds (896 gallery choices including plain). Each tile has 12 courses of 6 tabs; the tab pattern of every course is shifted right by a whole number of half tabs, which keeps exactly one repeat per tile. Tabs are separated by narrow slots that run up from the lower course edge (only through the lower 58%, exposing a dark underlayment; the upper 42% is covered by the course above and is not slotted).
