Girdle switches share a fully covered zero-slope ring where both finite septal gates vanish; nearest-axis ties lie outside compact rib support. Squared-radius annuli, smooth finite septa and short spines use product unions rather than max/clamped-sum creases. Sampling compares at most nine cached axes and evaluates one current-girdle septum or marginal spine, with no allocation, hash, square root, trigonometry or neighboring-cell scan; empty backing skips grain and generic detail noise is bypassed. The 0.00024 m nominal tile and 0.0022 UV relief are authored; relief is not metres. Existing opaque M0 four maps/optional min–max height, tint/repeats, sync/native Worker/cache and caller ownership remain unchanged; no geometric holes, transparency, 3D shells or automatic parallax are introduced.

`bgasolderballs` adds schematic unmounted BGA undersides: four raised dark square dielectric carriers each hold a complete, uniformly pitched 13 × 13 field of equal-sized silver metallic rounded domes, 676 balls in total. Balls rise brighter and smoother above the carrier with occluded contact bands; seeds change whole-package placement/orientation and microfinish, not manufacturing-grid regularity. This is distinct from the two external lead rows of `dualinlinechips`, annular `ballbearingfaces`, connected `ballchain` and dielectric `lensarray`. The actually read [TI SSZA002B PBGA document](https://www.ti.com/lit/pdf/ssza002), pages 2 and 5, describes laminate/solder-ball construction and potentially fully populated underside arrays. The actually viewed [Pentium MMX underside photograph](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Kl_Intel_Pentium_MMX_embedded_BGA_Bottom.jpg) instead shows green substrate, regular silver balls and a central black ball-free region; it is not a direct full-grid specimen. The charcoal palette and complete grid are authored, with no copied image asset or specific TI/Intel part, pinout, JEDEC dimensions, solder alloy, assembly/reflow/reliability or electrical certification. Reused cached-noise and compact C1 owner conventions retain 128 B of four poses plus 128² grain, 131,200 B of typed tables, not total RAM or FPS. Default per-axis owner support is 0.34√2 + 0.012 = 0.492833 < 0.5; ball radius 0.0125 is below half the 0.04 pitch. Nearest row/column lookup evaluates only one ball, never scans the array. Owner floors are computed once per axis; sampling allocates nothing and performs no hash, square root, trigonometry or neighbor search. Paired signed half-plane products preserve C1 carrier corners, ball supports vanish before nearest-grid owner boundaries, and continuous grain does not jump with grid ownership. Maps retain metallic 1 on balls and 0 on carriers/backing; default factor 1 preserves mixed metal, while explicit 0 changes only the factor. The 0.05 m nominal tile and 0.012 UV relief are authored, not metre-valued depth or dimensional calibration. Inventory grows from 862 to 863; existing opaque four maps/optional min–max height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and caller ownership remain unchanged, without real ball meshes, underfill/routing, solder flow, transparency or additional browser/Canvas2D certification.

An independent 768² raw-surface fixture finds carriers by dielectric-height separation and balls by eight-connected metallic > 0.5 masks, without pose tables, seed phases or nearest-grid equations. Observed nearest-center distances and a fourth angular harmonic recover pitch/orthogonal axes; independently clustered projections verify every row/column population and unique cell. Local peak refinement and twelve-direction probes compare tops, midprofiles, contacts and carriers. Outer probes use min(1.5 × observed ball radius, observed nearest pitch / 2), avoiding a neighboring ball in valid dense 17 × 17 grids. All three seeds yield four carriers/676 balls/no orphans; every carrier has 13 × 13 levels, 169 unique cells and 13 balls per row/column. Area CV ≤0.023033, pitch CV ≤0.002442, axis-pitch ratio 0.999833–1.000218, fourth-harmonic strength ≥0.999379, lattice residual ≤0.014079 pitch and centering error ≤0.000217 equivalent carrier radii. Raw normalized ball rise/pigment contrast/finish contrast/carrier elevation are ≥0.549037/0.691606/0.543080/0.220260; center-to-midprofile drop ≥0.207058, midprofile elevation ≥0.318149 and contact-AO contrast ≥0.219584. Peaks retain M1 and carrier rings M0. Actual seeded metal-footprint changes are 0.116089/0.112793. C1 residual ≤0.002491 and signed-UV periodic error ≤1.49e-14; 32/512 maps are opaque with positive Z and XYZ squared-length error ≤0.012888. The 0.001 tilt² threshold exceeds flat encoded quantization. At 512, M0/M1/transition counts are 240518/17067/4559; 32 is coarse preview, not individual-ball certification. Forty-five valid variants each pass fifteen cases, including even 12 and 11/15/17 grids, one/nine carriers, independent AO, no grain height, C2 compact cubic domes, hemisphere profiles with C1 contact blending and quintic shoulders. Twenty-two executable faults are rejected, none accepted; only AssertionErrors count, covering missing-center/perimeter-only arrays, staggered/nonuniform/nonorthogonal lattices, unequal diameters, flat pads/recesses, missing carriers/rise/pigment/finish/contact, wrong metal regions, hard/linear shoulders, finish-only seeds and owner clipping.

`pigskinleather` adds warm-brown matte skin with 64 irregular triangular follicle triplets: each group contains three separate rounded pits whose centers are lower, darker, rougher and occluded. Seeds vary group placement/orientation/radius and individual pore size/ellipse orientation. The arrangement is triangular, not each individual pore. It is distinct from existing Voronoi-pebbled `leather`, single raised quill collars in `ostrichleather`, hairy `cowhide` patches and the equal-sized single staggered perforations of `perforatedleather`. References actually read are [Etherington and Roberts' conservation dictionary, pigskin](https://cool.culturalheritage.org/don/dt/dt2578.html), [Kawanishi Kaban's ∴ identification paragraph](https://shop.kawanishikaban.com/en/blogs/%E7%B4%A0%E6%9D%90%E3%81%AB%E3%81%A4%E3%81%84%E3%81%A6/material-pigskin), and the [Leather Repair Company pigskin entry](https://prorestorers.co.uk/knowledgebase/pig-leather-skin/). Two 300px close-ups from the latter were viewed, but are not used to certify every triplet visually. The dictionary's roughly triangular groups of three inform an authored motif, not authenticity, breed, tanning, Nappa grading or pore-density certification. Existing cached-noise, compact C1 cavity and single-owner table patterns are reused: three bounded elliptical pits over continuous two-scale grain, not a new general leather API. The 192 × 5 center/quadratic coefficients plus 64² + 128² grain values retain 171,520 typed bytes, excluding JS objects, output maps and total RAM. Sampling visits exactly three pores without allocation, hashing, square roots, trigonometry or neighbor scanning. Default per-axis support is bounded by 0.426782 < 0.5, while within-triplet pore-center distance ≤0.250337 remains below the inter-triplet lower bound 0.272686. The nominal 0.028 m tile and relief 0.0038 UV units are authored, not measured pore depth. Opaque dielectric maps do not reconstruct through-dermis follicles, the flesh side, wrinkle networks, breathability or transmission.

An independent fixture samples max(768, 96 × cells) points per axis, finds eight-connected AO < 0.8 pore regions, then groups mutual two-nearest-neighbor triples. It reads no pose/quadratic tables and reproduces neither seed phases nor pore equations. A local 3 × 3 search refines each observed centroid toward minimum AO; twelve-direction outer-ring medians compare raw height/color/roughness/AO without assuming a fixed color/height ratio. Actual built ESM yields 192 pores/64 triplets for all three seeds, with no ungrouped regions; minimum/maximum side ratio is ≥0.940672 and twice triangle area/maximum side² ≥0.808043. Pore-area CV is 0.27443–0.29612, group-radius CV 0.08203–0.09529, within-cell position SD ≥0.12408 and threefold orientation concentration ≤0.097585. Recess depth/color/roughness/AO contrasts are respectively ≥0.327798/0.469495/0.207435/0.629999, with metallic and mortar both zero; seed footprint changes are 0.017761/0.018494. C1 residual is ≤0.00004552 and signed-UV error ≤5.56e-16. Both 32/512 outputs remain opaque/M0/positive-Z with XYZ squared-length error ≤0.009935. Size 32 is coarse preview, not per-pore certification; the tilt² threshold 0.0001 exceeds the quantized flat [128,128,255] XY floor, and a trivial <3 bound is not used as normal-length evidence. The matrix passes 44 legal variants × 15 cases, including independent pigment/depth/AO, density changes, no grain relief, C2 cubic/C1 smoothstep shoulders and independent height/pigment profiles; all 21 executable faults are rejected, counting only AssertionErrors.

`trianglesocketheads` adds 25 silver-gray round steel heads with straight-sided TA triangular blind recesses, softly beveled corners, closed flat steel floors and unbroken surrounding head faces, above rough dielectric gray backing. Seeds vary placement, orientation, grain and pigment, not head size. This is not the radial three-channel Y of `tripointscrewheads`, a square/hex socket or curved-sided TP3. The actually read [Mingxing TA pan-head triangle-recess product page](http://www.mx-fastener.com/pan-head-triangle-shaped-recess-ta-screw.html) and two viewed product photographs distinguish straight-sided TA from TP3 and list carbon steel/galvanized construction; the authored silver-gray finish does not certify alloy, plating chemistry, DIN/ISO dimensions, torque or tamper-resistance. No photographs are copied into the repository. The existing two-pin/square/hex shared sampler gains three smoothed signed half-plane products, not paired absolute planes or a minimum intersection; existing floor metalness, circular shoulders and old-path evaluation order remain intact. Constructor storage is 25 × 5 pose values plus 128² grain values, totaling 132,072 retained typed bytes, not total RAM or an FPS claim. The new sampling branch adds no allocation, hashing, square roots, trigonometry or neighbor search. The owner bound is 0.38 + √2 × 0.025 < 0.5, and the triangular circumradius 2 × 0.135 stays inside fullRadius 0.33. The nominal 0.06 m tile and relief 0.03 are authored parameters; relief uses UV units, not metres or measured recess depth. Maps do not create threads, through-holes or pan-head geometry.

An independent fixture finds heads and recesses from 512² metallic masks and eight-connected regions, without reading pose tables or reproducing triangular half-plane equations. Each observed circular-head centroid emits 720 first-exit rays: all three seeds yield 25 heads/25 sockets, three radial corner peaks and no lost exits. Corner-gap error is ≤0.5°, maximum/minimum radius 1.95953–1.97365, and normalized threefold-symmetry/flat-fit residuals ≤0.014202/0.000375 relative to observed mean socket radius; aperture-centroid offset is ≤0.006978 head radii. Observed within-cell position SD is ≥0.013273 and threefold orientation concentration ≤0.241191. Multi-angle, multi-radius floor probes retain metallic 1/mortar 0, normalized recess depth ≥0.418507, floor elevation above measured backing ≥0.230026 and an overall floor-height range <0.001968; face/floor raw color contrast is ≥0.398937, AO contrast 0.44 and floor roughness increment ≥0.042397. Actual built ESM C1 residual is ≤0.000074385 and signed-UV period error ≤2.301e-14. Both 32/512 encoded normal maps remain positive-Z with XYZ squared-length error ≤0.012826 and every map opaque; the 512 metal mask contains 144014 M0/90428 M1/27702 transition pixels. Size 32 is coarse preview, not per-socket outline certification. The final matrix passes 32 legal variants × 15 cases and rejects 20 executable faults, with zero accepted and only AssertionErrors counted: four/six-sided or curved drives, painted/raised/backing-height floors, missing pigment/AO/finish, fixed stamps, nonmetal/grout floors, hard/linear/minimum-corner creases and owner clipping.

`diatomvalves` depicts four seeded ivory, lanceolate external valve motifs: apically elongated lineolae form paired transapical striae around an expanded, flush central clear area, while two raphes remain separated centrally and bend slightly toward the same side near their tips. The axial sternum rises slightly; pores and fissures are lower, darker, rougher and occluded, not eight chiton plates, guard-cell rings or deep internal-valve chambers. References actually read include the [Diatoms of North America Navicula guide](https://diatoms.org/genera/navicula/guide), [N. tripunctata external/internal SEM metadata](https://diatoms.org/species/navicula_tripunctata/images), and the morphology section/caption of [Yılmaz et al. (2024), Fig.3](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11019260/). Both tripunctata SEM views and Fig.3 were actually viewed: A–D are external, E–H internal; internal grooves/helictoglossae are not copied onto the outer face. This is an authored naviculoid motif, not a species reconstruction, SEM gold coating, live cell or complete paired frustule. The 0.00012 m tile, 2² layout, ivory palette and exaggerated normal-authoring relief 0.0022 are not calibrated pore density, shell thickness or reflectance. Existing cached poses, C1 compact support and grain support bounded nearest-row/column lookup of one lineola; construction retains four poses, 24 row bounds and 96² grain, totaling 160 + 768 + 73,728 = 74,656 typed bytes, excluding JS/output maps and without claiming an fps gain. Whole aperture supports stay outside the central clear area instead of per-pixel clipping into split fragments. Default radius plus jitter is 0.47070 < 0.5 cell; row/column switches occur outside aperture support. Sampling allocates nothing and uses no hash, sqrt, trig, neighboring-cell or per-pore scan. Fully opaque M0 cavities retain solid backing; no geometry, transparency/transmission, full girdle/internal valve, photonics, locomotion or biological model is added.

Diagnostics use max(768, 384 × cells) samples per axis and eight-connected regions. All three default 768² seeds yield four footprints, each with two raphes and 84 lineolae in 22 transapical groups: observed results, not taxonomic pore counts or pinned fixture constants. Centroids/covariances recover axes and four first-exit contours measure dimensions; bilateral local color contrast identifies cavities without reading pose/row tables, copying the shape equation or assuming a fixed color/height-drop ratio. Median lineola axis ratio is ≥2.3239, same-side row-spacing CV ≤0.03686, with no central lineolae. The observed raphe gap is 0.08164–0.08379 of valve length, and same-sided tip deflection ≥0.01498 of width. Median raw color/normalized height/roughness/AO cavity gaps are ≥0.61967/0.29991/0.27971/0.52. Footprint radius CV ≥0.05494, position SD ≥0.01670, orientation concentration ≤0.3541 and seed footprint changes 0.10254/0.10767 distinguish geometry from pigment noise. C1 residual ≤0.014712 and signed-UV difference ≤1.95e-14. Fifty-two legal variants pass 15 cases each, including independent pore pigment/depth, shorter bodies, different rows, quintic shoulders and elliptic outlines. Twenty-four executable faults are rejected, none accepted: circular/transverse holes, missing clear center, fused raphes, opposite bends, absent depth/finish/AO, one-sided pores, irregular rows, fixed stamps, actual owner clipping, hard/linear shoulders and metallic valves. Actual 32/512 RGBA remain M0, opaque and positive-Z with XYZ unit error ≤0.010058; 32 is coarse preview, not pore/raphe or species certification.

`chitonshells` adds nine elongated oval dorsal motifs, each with eight raised valves (two terminal caps and six intermediate plates), seven directionally asymmetric lap shoulders and a darker, rougher mantle girdle around the shell core. Exposed faces rise in the lap direction beneath a low rounded midline ridge; a continuous lower, darker, occluded shell underface remains at the seams, rather than merely incising seven symmetric lines into one oval. [UCMP Polyplacophora](https://ucmp.berkeley.edu/taxa/inverts/mollusca/polyplacophora.php), including the actually viewed Lined Chiton photograph/anatomy diagram, informs the eight dorsal plates and girdle. The actually viewed dorsal/side SEM in [Connors et al. (2019), Fig. 1a–b](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6904579/) distinguishes the eight primary plates from the peripheral small scales. This preset only grains the girdle; it does not reconstruct that paper's hundreds of scales or μ-CT geometry. The warm-brown palette, 0.12 m tile and 3² layout are authored illustration, not grayscale-SEM color recovery, a particular species or calibrated biological scale. Existing compact squared ellipse shoulders and C1 corner-ramp conventions form rounded-absolute chevrons. One owner and one active course suffice, without scanning eight plates per pixel. The constructor precomputes hashes, rotation/scale bases, plate tones and 96² grain; poses/tones 864 B + grain 73,728 B retain 74,592 B of typed storage, excluding JS/output maps and not proving higher fps. Default maximal rotated radius plus jitter is 0.47335 < 0.5. Curved coordinates can extend beyond the last interval inside the tail cap: retain its last valve rather than read the next owner's tone or undefined. The field hot path has no allocation, hashing, square roots or trigonometry; shoulders and ramps remain C1. This fully opaque dielectric 2D relief does not create hidden overlap volumes, eight articulated meshes, rolling motion, shell-eye optics, transmission/SSS, shell-layer/muscle anatomy or biomechanics.

Diagnostics use max(512, 256 × cells) samples per axis: all three default seeds at 768² have nine four-connected footprints and 72 raised faces, with exactly eight principal-axis face intervals per body, not separate meshes. Footprint centroids/covariances recover axes; observed valleys and half-height crossings measure seven consistent lap directions, long/short shoulder ratio ≥3.3881 and forward height rise ≥0.06946 on the six middle plates. Valleys lie ≥0.3910 below adjacent peaks yet ≥0.1542 above the girdle upper bound, with an AO gap of about 0.21. Independently tracked lateral valleys recover rounded-chevron shift ≥0.3020 pitch and bilateral difference ≤0.00280. All 32 radial probes per body retain a girdle, with relative width ≥0.1796 and plate/girdle roughness gap ≥0.3752. Footprint radius CV ≥0.03553, position SD ≥0.02148 and orientation concentration ≤0.4280; changed-seed footprint classifications differ by 0.11401/0.11646. C1 residual ≤0.000383 and signed UV difference ≤2.45e-15. RGBA checks use the actual 32/512 grids; phase segmentation is only performed at 512 using sampled raw heights, not the denser diagnostic grid or min–max height bytes. At 512, plate/girdle luma gap is 77.423, girdle/ground 20.145 and finish gap 0.37619. Forty-three legal variants pass 15 cases each; all 29 executable faults are rejected, including seven/nine valves, symmetric incisions, absent lap ramps, missing underface/girdle, sharp chevrons and tail-cap tone overrun. Both 32/512 maps are opaque M0 with positive Z and XYZ unit error ≤0.012027; 32 is coarse preview, not a per-valve certificate. Actual forced WebGPU/WebGL2 and strict-CSP dist native Worker/cache/borrowed-pool reuse/cleanup are exercised; no new Canvas2D, cross-browser or biological-material certification.

`leafstomata` depicts 25 schematic pairs of kidney-shaped guard cells with seeded size, position, orientation and pore-width variation around dark, recessed, occluded opaque pore floors. The two brighter, smoother cell crests meet at slightly lower polar contacts that remain above the leaf ground; this is not an undivided shell rim, toothed cowrie aperture or single coffee-bean groove. The actually read [Nadeau and Sack (2002), Stomatal Development in Arabidopsis](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC3243354/), including its viewed Fig. 1 TEM/cryo-SEM, and [Jaafar and Anderson (2024), stomatal cell-wall review](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11232514/), including its viewed Fig. 1 schematic, inform kidney-shaped eudicot guard cells joined at their poles rather than grass dumbbells. These are a reference chapter/review and their cited images, not experiments reproduced by the engine. The 0.00025 m tile, 5² layout and green palette are authored illustration, not colors measured from grayscale SEM, calibrated stomatal density or a species reconstruction. Existing compact squared shoulders and cached grain support C1 outer/pore shoulders, radial crests and polar contacts; the full pore skips the undefined radial coordinate. The constructor precomputes hashes, rotation/scale bases, inverse pore widths and 96² grain/12² skin noise. The field hot path has no allocation, hashing, square roots or trigonometry. Poses 1,200 B + grain 73,728 B + skin 1,152 B retain 76,080 B of typed storage, excluding JS containers/output maps and not proving higher fps. Default rotated support plus jitter has conservative half-width 0.448 < 0.5, requiring only its owner. Rough green ground abstracts the epidermis; it does not reconstruct pavement-cell topology, the developmental one-cell-spacing rule, organelles, geometric air passages, transmission/SSS, live opening, transpiration or biomechanics.

At three seeds the actual 512² height field has 25 hole-filled outer footprints, 25 four-connected recessed pores and 50 eight-connected cell crests. Eight-connectivity handles diagonal crest pixels; the polar-connected cells are not 50 separate meshes. Observed pore centroids/covariances independently recover principal axes: aspect ≥2.873, paired-crest midpoint offset ≤0.01213 cell and axial alignment error ≤0.11297. All 800 radial profiles per seed retain a closed raised enclosure; polar-profile peaks stand ≥0.32014 above the ground upper bound, with side-profile peaks ≥0.07780 higher than the polar peaks. Footprint radius CV ≥0.04282, relative pore-width CV ≥0.10597, position SD ≥0.04067 and orientation concentration ≤0.25534; changed-seed footprint classifications differ by 0.15845/0.16064, pore classifications by 0.04663/0.04932. C1 residual ≤0.000881 and signed UV difference ≤9.92e-16. Actual sampler heights classify the 512 RGBA phases: guard/ground luma gap 59.917, ground/pore 24.298, smoother-guard finish gap 0.31196 and guard/pore AO gap 0.64115. Optional height follows the shared min–max normalization, not raw height/relief values. Forty legal variants pass 15 cases each; all 25 executable faults are rejected. Both 32/512 maps are opaque M0 with positive Z and XYZ unit error ≤0.011043; 32 is coarse preview, not a per-pore/cell certificate. Actual forced WebGPU/WebGL2 and strict-CSP dist native Worker/cache/borrowed-pool reuse/cleanup are exercised; no new Canvas2D, cross-browser, biological or medical certification.

`watermelontourmaline` adds 16 separate, approximately rounded-triangular polished slices: pink cores, pale-gray transition zones and green rinds on one shared plane above rougher warm-gray backing. It is not fruit-rind striping, recolored cubic fluorite or engraved inlay. [GIA Tourmaline Quality Factors](https://www.gia.edu/tourmaline-quality-factor) describes a pink core/thin green skin and thin slices; its actually viewed typical specimen photo informs the palette. [GIA Micro-Features of Tourmaline (2024)](https://www.gia.edu/gems-gemology/summer-2024-micro-features-tourmaline) describes trigonal structure, triangular cross sections and color zoning. The 0.08 m tile, 4² layout and idealized threefold geometry are authored, not specimen/chemical/dimensional reconstruction. Existing fluorite/chiastolite planar-pigment principles and the fixed-gray neutral-pigment path are reused. Body, core and pale-zone boundaries use three compact-circle C1 products, not nearest-arc seams or a new public geometry/color API. Construction precomputes radius-normalized rotation bases, core/pale inverses and squares, hash, 96² grain and 8² cloud. The field hot path allocates nothing, uses no hash/sqrt/trig and skips inner layers/grain outside a slice. Pose 1,152 B + grain 73,728 B + cloud 512 B = 75,392 B retained typed storage, excluding JS containers/output maps and not implying an fps gain. Default support 0.34 + jitter 0.07 = 0.41 < 0.5 cell needs only the current owner. This is a front-lit opaque PBR palette approximation, without gem transmission/refraction, pleochroism, cat’s-eye/iris effect, inclusion optics, physical slice mesh or provenance/quality certification. No source photo is copied as an asset.

At three seeds the actual 512² sampler has 16 separate periodic four-connected slices. Height-weighted centroids and 96 first-exit rays independently measure normalized third Fourier coefficient ≥0.06576, second/fourth ≤0.00002564 and 120° difference ≤0.000514, without copying circles or private poses. All 1,536 rays per seed encounter pink, pale and green in order; sampled face height/relief span ≤0.001929, roughness span ≤0.01350 and backing/face roughness gap ≥0.59602. Radius CV ≥0.04427, position SD ≥0.03291, orientation concentration ≤0.23489; seed outline-classification change is 0.08276/0.07959. C1 residual ≤0.0001588, signed-UV difference ≤1.12e-16. Actual 512 RGBA pink/green/pale fractions are approximately 0.08293/0.07513/0.01218; pale luma exceeds both pigments by 46.305, phase mean heights differ by <0.618 byte and roughness by <0.00158. All 16 observed centers are red and all 1,536 rind probes green. Thirty-six legal variants each pass 15 cases; 24 executable faults are rejected. Swapping the palette leaves both pigment totals present but fails all 16 core-position checks. Actual 32/512 maps are M0, opaque, positive-Z with XYZ unit error ≤0.010735; 32 is only a coarse preview, not certification of every fine gray band or outline. Forced WebGPU/WebGL2 and strict-CSP dist native worker/cache/borrowed-pool reuse/cleanup were exercised, without broader Canvas2D/browser or gem-optics certification.

`cuttlebone` adds a warm-white, rough dielectric wall–septa section over dark, recessed, occluded opaque backing. The actually read [Yang et al. (2020) wall–septa study and Fig. 1](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7519314/), including its SEM/reconstruction images, provide the visual reference: cuttlebone is an internal shell, not vertebrate bone. The authored 0.0024 m nominal tile has six uneven layers and 24 staggered, oppositely bending, variable-amplitude quintic supports per layer, joined to septa by C1 product union—not a white recoloring of the continuous transverse medium in `cardboardedge`. This is a schematic 2D section; real 3D walls form a branching open labyrinth, so 144 2D windows do not mean 144 sealed chambers or the paper’s 93 vol% porosity. It does not reconstruct micro-CT, the 3D waviness gradient/growth direction, geometric holes, transmission/SSS, buoyancy, strength or biological dimensions. Nominal size and curves are authored choices, not copied measurements. Construction precomputes hash, layer bounds/inverse scales and 96² grain. The field hot path allocates no arrays/objects and uses no hash, sqrt or trig. Wall/layer arrays retain 2,456 B plus 73,728 B grain, totaling 76,184 B typed storage, excluding JS containers/output maps and not implying an fps gain. Default conservative support half-span is 0.45675 < 0.5 column; interface offset 0.12 < 0.5 needs at most one neighboring-row correction. Squared local tangent distance limits wall thinning; flat septa conceal row ownership changes.

At three seeds the actual 512² sampler has 144 periodic four-connected low regions and six layers, layer-height CV 0.07198–0.08107. Low-pixel fraction 0.88644–0.89196 describes only this 2D mask, not volume porosity. Every support has opposite-side lobes and a larger upper lobe; both bend directions occur, placement SD ≥0.09744 and amplitude CV ≥0.19434. Differentiating observed height-profile centroids and projecting FWHM onto the local normal gives within-wall max/min ≤1.0084 (bound 1.03), not certified global Euclidean distance. Chalk exceeds backing by ≥0.8570 color, ≥0.6525 height/relief and ≥0.7670 AO; backing roughness exceeds chalk by ≥0.1143. C1 residual ≤0.001774 and signed-UV difference ≤5.30e-14; actual 32/512 RGBA is M0, opaque, positive-Z with XYZ unit error ≤0.01252. Thirty legal variants each pass 15 cases; 21 valid faults are rejected, including max-union joints, linear shoulders, lost tangent correction, missing row correction and powder-only seed changes. Size 32 is a coarse preview, not certification of 144 windows. Forced WebGPU/WebGL2 gallery and strict-CSP dist native worker/cache/borrowed-pool reuse/cleanup were exercised; no broader Canvas2D, browser, geometry or biological-material certification is added.

`pallasite` adds an opaque polished-slice approximation: olive-to-honey dielectric crystals in a connected silver-gray metallic matrix, nearly coplanar phases and fine darker, slightly recessed, rougher intramineral fissures—not raised pebbles or cement grout. The actually read [NASA Mittlefehldt/Rumble abstract](https://ntrs.nasa.gov/api/citations/20060020744/downloads/20060020744.pdf) describes magnesian olivine mixed with iron-nickel, and [ASU’s Buseck Center](https://meteorites.asu.edu/stony-iron-meteorites/pallasites) describes greenish olivine; the [Esquel slice photo](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:FilePath/Esquel.jpg) was viewed for crystal/reflective-metal morphology, not copied. This front-lit opaque authored PBR model does not provide the photo’s backlit transmission, refraction, gem quality, calibrated iron-nickel optical constants, etched Widmanstätten structure or origin/composition certification. The 0.16 m tile has 8² jittered periodic Voronoi grains, varying erosion and C1 half-plane products. A private `buildPeriodicVoronoiPlanes` directly reuses `septarian`’s clipping; its old inline copy is removed without adding a public geometry API. Clipping, hashing, square roots and trigonometry run only during construction; pixels inspect a 3² owner patch and surviving facets without allocating arrays/objects or rebuilding polygons, stopping at the first disjoint positive support. Across three sampled seeds each tile has 384 unit-plane triplets, four to eight facets per grain and mean six; retained typed storage including 96² grain is 88,064 B and shared construction scratch 1,608 B, excluding JS containers/output maps—not FPS. Seed changes crystal placement, size, tone and fissures. Grains M0/matrix M1 use the existing neutral-matrix pigment path; default metallic factor 1 preserves the mask, explicit 0 makes both dielectric. Four opaque maps, optional height, tint/repeats, sync/Worker/cache and ownership remain; no dependency, entry point, mesh, Canvas2D or cross-browser certification is added.

At three seeds the actual sampler’s 512² periodic four-connected mask has 64 separate grains, area CV 0.20349–0.22898 and maximum bbox aspect ≤1.582. Full-metal fraction is approximately 0.34326–0.35850, full-mineral 0.53615–0.54996. Mineral roughness exceeds metal by ≥0.09164, height/relief span ≤0.013985 (polished bound 0.04), AO 0.96–0.99. Comparing darkest/brightest pixels within each observed full-mineral component finds dark features in all 64 grains both more recessed and rougher, against an 80% bound, without private sites/planes. Seed classification changes approximately 0.33936/0.33960 of probes, color difference ≥0.6649; C1 residual ≤0.0005113 (bound 0.04), signed UV error ≤3.55e-14. Actual 512 RGBA has 64 mineral/one metal periodic four-connected components, M>250 fraction 0.35268, M<5 fraction 0.55801, mineral/matrix G−B gap 68.524, roughness gap 0.09162 and AO 245–252. At 32/512 XY² and XYZ length error are approximately 0.00003076, with positive Z and opaque pixels. The sampled polished preset’s 8-bit normals are nearly planar; tiny float fissure height is not presented as visible bump, and 32 does not certify 64 grains. Twenty-two legal variants pass all fifteen cases and twenty real faults are rejected, including geometry-frozen-but-still-colored seeds, reversed M phases, raised stones/deep grooves, dark fissures without recess/roughness, missing neighboring owners, nearest-edge/linear-shoulder creases and lost neutral metal pigment.

`gosperinlay` adds one slightly raised, smoother brass-toned metallic line over a rough navy dielectric backing. The [Gosper curve](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gosper_curve) page and its raw wikitext supply the 60° turtle, axiom A and both A/B productions. Three offline expansions give 343 equal links and 344 distinct triangular-lattice vertices, with two endpoints, no branches and no closing edge: a finite order-three pattern, not the limiting space-filling set or a Gosper-island tessellation. This differs from `moorecurve`’s 64-cell orthogonal closed groove, `boullebrassinlay`’s S-shaped foliate scrolls and `kintsugiceramic`’s crack network. Its 0.5 m preset stores a 343-character direction word. Construction builds four-value segments and 16² local bbox buckets; pixels do not interpret an L-system, allocate arrays/objects, scan all 343 links or use sqrt/trig/hash. It reuses `vogelspiral`’s in-place prefix-end indexing and `fiddleheads`’ product-union rounded joins: 801 references, 257 offsets, at most eleven candidates and a whole-tile mean of 3.12890625 per bin; typed storage including the 96² Float64 grain is 86,820 B, excluding JS strings/objects/output maps. These are structural counts, not FPS measurements. Squared capsule distance and C1 cubic shoulders retain round caps and flat cores. Seed affects grain finish only, not path placement or metal classification. Line M1/backing M0 needs a default material metallic factor of 1; explicit 0 makes both dielectric. Four opaque PBR maps, optional height and existing tint/repeats, sync/Worker/cache and ownership remain. No new entry point, dependency, mesh, alloy/conductivity/manufacturing simulation, Canvas2D or untested-browser certification.

An independent fixture recursively builds the public A/B grammar while production reads an offline direction word; it does not import private poses. Across three seeds the actual sampler yields 343 links and 344 reachable vertices from 949 candidate neighbor pairs: two degree-one endpoints, 342 degree-two interiors, no 606 shortcut links, no gaps at five interior probes per link and no metal in 686 lateral backing probes. Thirteen outward-half-plane first-exit rays per endpoint recover a half-height radius of approximately 0.00567186 and zero spread against a 1.5% bound. Core variation is ≤0.005958 (grain bound 0.0061), line/backing M1/M0, roughness gap ≥0.54555, color gap ≥0.82231 and AO gap 0.33. Seed changes color by ≥0.02365 and normalized height by ≤0.005742, leaving the metal mask identical. C1 residual is ≤0.0009104 (h=1e-10, bound 0.04), signed UV error ≤3.06e-16. At 512 the actual maps have M>250 fraction 0.13271, backing 0.82576, transition 0.044922, roughness gap 0.55991, luma gap 126 and height/metal mismatch at most three bytes (bound four). Outward normals on the raised line have dot ≥0.39413 (bound 0.08), XYZ length error ≤0.01240 and positive Z; line and backing each form one four-connected component. Size32 checks PBR contrast, registration and quantized normals, not 343-link/endpoint topology. Sixteen legal variants pass all fifteen cases; seventeen real faults are rejected, including max-union/linear-shoulder C1 residuals 1286.53/1155.58, an endpoint-only half-plane cutoff with centers retained (cap spread about 1), unclipped elliptical caps (0.0246952 spread), bbox-lost shoulders (9.968e9 residual), wrong direction/missing final link, overwide backing fill, lost roughness/relief, all-metal/all-dielectric output, unseeded grain and a seed-dependent radius change. Two separate RGBA source faults are also rejected by the same fixture: swapped X/Y preserves length but produces outward normal dot −0.54105, while forced Z255 stays forward-facing but produces XYZ length error 0.313756.

`vogelspiral` is a finite golden-angle panel of 97 equal flat glazed disks with rounded shoulders above rough gray backing, in teal/sage/ivory families of 33/32/32. The actually read [Vogel phyllotaxis section of Fermat’s spiral](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fermat%27s_spiral#The_golden_ratio_and_the_golden_angle) gives r=c√n and θ=n×137.508°. This authored variant explicitly uses r=0.044√(n+0.5), θ=nπ(3−√5), n=0–96: the half-step radial offset avoids central crowding and is not the original n=0 center. These are discrete equal disks, not a continuous spiral, tangent growing-radius Doyle spirals, a plant simulation or certified Fibonacci arm counts. The nominal 0.5 m tile has UV radius 0.033 (nominally 16.5 mm); minimum center distance 0.06802551 exceeds diameter 0.066, with outer support radius 0.46523142 and clear margins, not an installation certification. Squared-radius shoulders are C1; seeds vary tone/grain only. As with existing bounded neighboring-shape queries, an 8 × 8 CSR table stores 224 references, at most 8/average 3.5 candidates per bucket instead of 97 per-pixel tests. Fixed storage is 291+96² Float64 entries and 65+224 Uint16 entries: 76,634 B excluding JS objects/output maps. Trig/sqrt/hashes run only at initialization; sampling allocates nothing and bypasses generic detail. Periodic signed UVs, opaque dielectric M0, four maps plus optional height and existing ownership remain unchanged.

Across three seeds the actual sampler’s 1024² connected-face raster has 97 disks, without reading private pose tables. Sorting observed centers by r² recovers golden divergence with error ≤0.00151271 rad (limit 0.006) and affine-r² residual ≤0.023898 annuli (limit 0.06); equal-radius spread is ≤0.32044%, eccentricity ≤0.004744. Thirteen first-exit rays give radius range ≤0.50264% (limit 1.5%); all 97 centers are filled, core-height variation ≤0.005125 within the 0.0061 grain bound, and closest-disk midpoints are flat grout, rejecting annuli and domes. Actual 512 height maps also have 97 faces; 32 does not certify all 97/complete circle geometry. C1 residual ≤0.00014433 (h=1e-10, limit 0.04), signed-UV error ≤6.11e-16. Nineteen legal variants pass all fifteen cases, including rotation, reflection, radial offset and 4/12 bins. Seventeen faults are rejected: non-affine-r² residual 2.32301, ellipses with expanded cache bounds have 5.0948% roundness error, annuli have zero filled centers, domes have core variation 0.06735, shoulder-omitting cache bounds cause a C1 jump near 8.96e9, linear shoulders 343.17. Seeds change glaze/grain, not whole-panel rotation; candidate counts are not an FPS claim.

`langtonsant` is an authored 1,024-step Langton-ant snapshot: 122 glossy dark logical-black cells on a rough pale-warm 32 × 32 tiled board, with a raised cream triangle marking the final cell/heading. The actually read [MathWorld Langton’s Ant](https://mathworld.wolfram.com/LangtonsAnt.html) convention turns left 90° on white, right 90° on black, flips the departed cell and advances one unit; do not mix it with the mirrored convention. Starting at (16,16) facing up on an all-white unbounded plane, this offline trajectory ends at (8,8) facing down, with 122 black/226 visited cells. Its entire x=5–24, y=6–22 trajectory stays inside the 32-cell frame. Runtime stores only 32 row bitmaps and the final direction vector, not a simulator or copied image. The nominal 0.32 m tile has 0.01 m cells. Rounded square shoulders and a product of three half-plane ramps keep the compass triangle C1; logical white is rendered pale warm rather than pure white. It is not a QR code, interactive automaton or proof of a 104-step highway/infinite evolution. Each sampler retains one 96² Float64 grain lattice (73,728 B typed storage, excluding JS data/output maps), with O(1) bitmap lookup, allocation-free sampling, no repeated hashes and generic detail bypassed. Periodic signed UVs, opaque dielectric M0, four maps plus optional height and existing ownership remain unchanged.

Tests copy no bitmap and do not compare against the same forward algorithm: actual sampler/512 RGBA cells, the exposed (.34,.34) compass-cell corner and the triangle’s wide-back/narrow-front heading reverse through 1,024 steps to the unique initially white north-facing origin. At 512 there are 123 half-height components (122 black squares plus a triangle on a white cell). At 32 the binary cell-center snapshot survives, but a one-pixel compass cannot reveal its heading/covered bit: the unique inverse among two bit/four heading candidates is checked, not complete shoulders/triangle geometry. Across three seeds axis-span mismatch is ≤0.01977% (limit 1%), normalized C1 residual ≤0.0002472 (h=1e-10, limit 0.04), signed-UV error ≤6.67e-16. Fifteen legal variants pass all fifteen cases; seventeen faults are rejected, including a wrong bit, wrong marker cell/heading, mirrored turn convention, reversed bits/transposition, circular/stretched cells, linear shoulders, ignored seed, seed-shifted geometry and metallic ink. Compass-only linear shoulders measure 659.38; stretched-cell span mismatch is 14.8268%. Seeds vary finish, never the board.

`pentominotiles` packs each of the twelve free pentominoes once into an authored 6 × 10 rectangle, then repeats it 5 × 3 into 30 × 30 cells and 180 glazed pieces. A pentomino is five edge-connected equal squares, with rotations/reflections identified; the actually read [Pentomino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pentomino) reference explains the twelve free shapes, 63 fixed orientations and 6 × 10 packing. The layout was solved and checked offline, not copied from an image; A–L are private authored IDs, not conventional letter names. Navy, slate and ivory families have 60 pieces each above rough gray grout. The nominal 0.6 m tile has 0.02 m cells. Smooth neighboring-cell weights merge by whole-piece ID, removing fake internal grid seams; competing IDs tie only inside flat grout, preserving C1 rounded shoulders at concavities and four-cell junctions. This weighted rounding is not an exact geometric offset, installation specification, interactive puzzle or general solver. Initialization retains 900 Uint16 owners, 180 Float64 tones and a 96² Float64 grain lattice: 76,968 B of fixed typed-array storage, excluding JS objects/temporaries/output maps. Sampling allocates nothing, rebuilds no hashes/solver and bypasses generic detail. Periodic signed UVs, opaque dielectric M0, four maps plus optional height and existing ownership remain unchanged.

At seeds 0/7/0xffffffff actual height samples at cell centers/shared-edge midpoints decode 180 edge-connected five-cell pieces, with all twelve D4-distinct free shapes exactly once per 6 × 10 block. All 735 internal joins remain seam-free; 1,065 external joins are recessed rough grout. Tests copy no ID matrix and pin no pose: horizontal/vertical layout mirrors are accepted. The actual 512 RGBA height map also has 180 half-height connected faces; 32 certifies maps/coarse colors, not the complete twelve-shape topology. At h=1e-10 the worst C1 slope residual is 0.00037748 (limit 0.04), signed-UV error ≤4.45e-16; seeds change finish rather than move the boundary field (normalized-height difference ≤0.00573476 within the 0.006 grain bound). Fifteen legal variants pass all fifteen cases; fourteen visible faults are rejected, including single-cell tiles, fake internal seams, missing/defective shapes, invisible relief, rough glaze, linear non-C1 ramps, seed-shifted geometry and metallic faces. Existing 850 presets/tiles remain unchanged: all 142,800 channels of 23,800 samples and all 300 RGBA buffers of 60 related-material sets are identical. This is not an FPS claim.

`pythagorastree` adds four finite glazed-square trees, each with 31 squares (root plus four generations, counts 1/2/4/8/16), fading from red-brown trunks to ivory leaf squares over rough grout. Unlike existing `pythagoreantiles`, it is not a plane-filling 2:1 two-size tiling with five large/five small squares. [Wikipedia Pythagoras tree](<https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pythagoras_tree_(fractal)>) and [MathWorld](https://mathworld.wolfram.com/PythagorasTree.html) describe child squares on the legs of an outward isosceles right triangle: child side √2/2 of the parent, child nominal areas summing to parent area. Infinite trees can overlap; area with multiplicity is not generally union area. Exact dyadic-coordinate separating axes verify this authored finite 31-square construction has no interior overlap, 46 nominal corner/edge contacts and root-equivalent area in each generation. Its finite bounds are 4.5L × 3.25L, not the infinite tree's 6L × 4L. Four motifs, scale 0.2, centerY 1.625, grout 0.002, shoulder 0.003, roughness 0.3, UV relief 0.012, palette 100,32,30 / 240,221,180 and nominal tile 0.4 m are authored settings. Thirty-one seven-value geometric records, 124 tones and 96² grain total 76,456 B of Float64 tables, plus JS scalars/initialization recursion, not total RAM. Each point examines one owner's 31 squares with squared-radius rejection and two separable squared C1 profiles, without per-point allocation, hashing, trigonometry, square roots or neighboring-owner scans. Empty grout skips grain; generic detail is skipped. Four opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and existing factory/Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged. No infinite fractal, actual tree, complete mesh, fractal-dimension or tile/structural/installation certification is supplied.

At seeds 0/7/0xffffffff the actual sampler's 1024² half-height raster has 124 squares, 31 per motif. Connected-face fourth moments recover orientation and fourfold strength 0.42466–0.43112 (a circular-clipping fault measures 0.0162). First-exit rays along observed axes recover nominal sides through the squared shoulder, without reading private pose tables. Axis-span mismatch is ≤0.02661% (limit 3%); five size-family and per-generation area errors are ≤0.03374% (limit 3%), root-side error ≤0.00606%. Orientations are 0/45° modulo 90°, with about 1.3e-16 rad floating error. Every observed parent finds two children, position error ≤0.001305 (limit 0.003), child-area sum error ≤0.03098%; all 31 faces are visited. Five generation-tone means differ by ≥0.15871, grout/glaze roughness gap ≥0.59487 and AO gap ≥0.47207, metallic violations zero. Seed-dependent centroid/side drift is ≤8.63e-6, maximum tone changes 0.07460/0.07123. Six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 is 0.00056843 < 0.04 and signed-UV error ≤2.221e-16, covering grid, owner boundaries, four-direction shoulder/corner/offset probes and curvature refinement. At 512 grout fraction is 0.82901, roughness gap 0.59629, luma range 134.76, AO 128–250, max tilt² 0.88043 and four signed normal fractions ≥0.02368. At 32 these are 0.82031, 0.60667, 133.04, 128–250 and 0.029558, not full fine-generation resolution. Fifteen legal variants pass seventeen cases each and fifteen faults are rejected. All 142,632 sampled channel values of 849 old kinds and 60 sets / 300 buffers from ten related kinds remain identical. Full gate and renderer/CSP evidence is in ACCEPTANCE.

`ichinghexagrams` adds an 8 × 8 sheet of six-line relief figures: flat charcoal-green bars with rounded shoulders on rough cream backing, connected yang lines and equally split yin lines with open centers. The sheet has 192 yang and 192 yin lines, hence 576 disconnected bars. The actually read [Hexagram (I Ching)](<https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hexagram_(I_Ching)>) introduction describes six stacked lines, solid yang/broken yin, counting from the bottom, and multiple sequences. This preset uses authored row-major binary order 0–63, least-significant bit at the bottom, not King Wen, Unicode codepoint order or certified Fu Xi arrangement. It differs from seven-segment digits, EAN-13 bars, Braille domes and Rule30 evolving cells. Line pitch 0.1, halfWidth 0.36, halfGap 0.08, halfHeight 0.032, shoulder 0.012, roughness 0.3, UV relief 0.003, palette 28,34,30 / 226,216,178 and nominal tile 0.32 m (8 × 8 authored 0.04 m glyph cells) are authored settings. Sixty-four tones and 96² grain total 74,240 B of Float64 tables, plus JS scalars/initialization scratch, not total RAM. Each point selects one glyph's nearest row and multiplies separable C1 profiles using cached squared radii and inverse bands, without per-point allocation, hashing, trigonometry, square roots or neighboring-glyph scans. Generic detail is skipped. Four opaque dielectric maps, metallic/mortar 0, optional height and existing factory/Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged. No font, interactive text, transparent holes, meshes, divination or historical/cultural certification is supplied.

At seeds 0/7/0xffffffff the actual sampler decodes all 64 figures bottom-up to distinct values 0–63, with 192 solid and 192 broken lines. Solid centers and both broken-line ends are ≥0.7600 relief units high; broken centers are 0.12. Each 1024² half-height raster has 576 bars and six rows per glyph, one connected bar per yang row and two per yin row. Observed widths/heights differ from the squared profile's closed-form half-contour prediction by ≤0.006492 (limit 0.01640625 glyph cell); row/symmetry position error is ≤0.002344 and the yin center gap is 0.171875. Tone and normalized-height differences from backing are each ≥0.6400, roughness difference ≥0.5701; top/backing AO exceeds the observed half-height rim by ≥0.29994/0.37994. Metallic/mortar violations are zero. Seeds preserve glyphs, positions and dimensions with zero measured raster drift; maximum per-glyph tone changes span 0.06151–0.07181. Six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 is 0.00058398 < 0.04; signed-UV measured error is zero over 4096 points, covering grid, bisected actual bar shoulders and curvature refinement. At 512 low-height backing fraction is 0.78223, roughness gap 0.53459, luma range 143.6, AO 158–255, max tilt² 0.28718 and four signed normal fractions ≥0.02514. At 32 these are 0.75, 0.41363, 100.9, 168–255 and 0.00076894: partial strokes remain, not all 64 decodable figures or narrow shoulders. Fifteen legal variants pass fifteen cases each, and fifteen true faults are rejected. All 142,464 sampled channel values of 848 old kinds remain identical, as do 60 sets / 300 buffers from ten related kinds. Full gate and renderer/CSP evidence is in ACCEPTANCE.

`apollonianmosaic` adds a finite two-round Descartes-reflection glazed-circle mosaic: dark teal, sage and ivory disks recursively fill tangent gaps around three equal initial circles, with nineteen disks in each of four periodic motifs. Unlike equal staggered `pennyround`, concentric sector `circularpavers` or square-hole Sierpinski patterns, the disks form an unequal tangent-circle packing. [Apollonian gasket](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollonian_gasket) supplies recursive tangency; [Descartes's theorem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Descartes%27s_theorem) supplies signed bends and the complex center equation. From the quadratic roots, the authored implementation derives b′ = 2Σ(other b) − b and (bz)′ = 2Σ(other bz) − bz, starting with a negative enclosing circle and three equal inner circles. Reflections cannot immediately backtrack; the quad is restored after recursion instead of copied per branch. R 0.47, initial radius R(2√3−3), 2 × 2 motifs, depth 2, grout 0.0015, shoulder 0.003, roughness 0.3, relief 0.012, palette 28,68,62 / 236,218,170 and nominal tile 0.24 m are authored settings; the enclosing circle is not drawn as a tile. Nineteen four-value disk records, 76 tones and 96² grain total 74,944 B of Float64 tables, plus three JS tone numbers and initialization scratch, not total RAM. Sampling reads nineteen disks of one owner using cached squared radii and inverse shoulder bands for C1 bevels, without per-point allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots. Empty grout skips grain; generic detail is skipped. Four opaque dielectric maps, optional height and existing Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged. This is a finite motif of flat bevelled disks, not an infinite fractal, domed mesh, transparency, certified fractal dimension or real mosaic installation.

At seeds 0/7/0xffffffff the actual sampler's 1024² half-height raster has 76 connected disks, nineteen per owner. Recovering nominal radii from area accounts for the squared bevel's half-height contour rather than simply adding grout back. Seeds change tone/grain, not nominal geometry; raster centroid/radius drift is ≤0.00005 and maximum tone changes are 0.08843/0.08418. Six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 is 0.00032641 < 0.04; signed-UV error ≤3.886e-16, covering grid, owner boundaries, observed circumferences, shoulder probes and curvature refinement. At 512 the maps have grout fraction 0.41203, roughness gap 0.60009, luma range 131.68, AO 128–250, max tilt² 0.88892 and four signed normal fractions ≥0.03075. At 32 large colored circles remain, not small circles or bevels (grout fraction 0.42969, tilt² 0.029558). Fifteen legal variants pass seventeen cases each and fifteen faults are rejected. All 142,296 sampled channel values of 847 old kinds remain identical, as do 60 sets / 300 buffers from ten distinct related kinds. Full gate and renderer/CSP evidence is in ACCEPTANCE.

`prismaticpentagonaltiles` adds the V3.3.3.4.4 dual tiling: alternating up/down house-shaped blue-gray, sage and ivory tiles, with two adjacent 90° base corners and three 120° roof corners. Unlike the separated right angles of `cairotiles`, six-petal 60° tips of `floretpentagonaltiles` or square/triangle faces of `elongatedtriangulartiles`, these are mirror-symmetric houses. [Wikipedia's Prismatic pentagonal tiling section](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elongated_triangular_tiling#Prismatic_pentagonal_tiling) supplies the dual relationship, three 120° plus two 90° angles, cmm symmetry and parallel lines bisecting stretched hexagons, but no coordinates. Authored regular-face centers form the pentagon (−1/2,−1/2), (1/2,−1/2), (1/2,√3/6), (0,√3/3), (−1/2,√3/6), with inradius 0.5, base 1, sides (3+√3)/6 and roof edges 1/√3. Four alternating houses form a 1 × (2+√3) cell; 15 × 4 cells contain 240 faces. Eight positions, ten normals, 240 tones and 96² grain total 75,792 B of Float64 tables, plus three JS tone numbers, not total RAM. Sampling checks 3 × 3 periodic owners with squared-radius 0.5 rejection, without per-point allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots; generic detail is skipped. Palette 30,58,76 / 224,236,222, roughness 0.3, relief 0.012, grout 0.025, shoulder 0.018 and the nominal 0.6 m tile are authored settings, not real tile, glaze or installation certification. Opaque dielectric maps, optional height and existing Worker/cache/ownership are unchanged.

At seeds 0/7/0xffffffff the 768² half-height raster has 240 five-corner faces, with inset-area error ≤1.559% and grout-fraction error 0.002877. It observes actual connected-face boundaries using a convex hull, shoulder-aware simplification and straight-side fits, without requesting five vertices. Corner/edge-direction error is ≤1.437° (limit 6°), and the two 90° corners are adjacent on the perimeter. Base/roof ratio √3 and side/roof ratio (1+√3)/2 have error ≤3.161% (limit 6%). Periodic clustering groups 1,200 corners into 360 vertices: 120 with four base corners and 240 with three roof corners. Three tones each have 80 faces, mean-tone gaps ≥0.3350, grout/glaze roughness gap ≥0.5730, AO gap 0.45482 and no metallic samples. Seeds retain positions while changing tone by up to 0.09451/0.09890. Six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 is 0.0029721 < 0.04; signed-UV error ≤4.997e-16. Coverage includes a grid, four band joins, horizontal/vertical/roof-edge families, offsets and curvature refinement. At 512 the maps have grout fraction 0.12879, roughness gap 0.57556, luma range 148.81, AO 128–250 and max tilt² 0.88892. The 32-map fraction 0.0625 and tilt² 0.029558 are undersampled narrow grout, not a physical area measurement. Fifteen legal variants pass seventeen cases each and fifteen faults are rejected. All 142,128 sampled channel values of 846 old kinds remain identical, as do 60 sets / 300 buffers from ten related kinds. Full gate and renderer/CSP evidence is in ACCEPTANCE.

`floretpentagonaltiles` adds the chiral V3.3.3.3.6 dual tiling: forest, olive and ivory glazed pentagonal petals, six around each flower center, each with one 60° and four 120° corners and two long edges twice the three short edges. Unlike the right-angled `cairotiles`, four-corner `deltoidaltrihexagonaltiles` or triangle/hexagon faces of `snubtrihexagonaltiles`, every face is a five-corner petal. [Wikipedia's 6-fold pentille section](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snub_trihexagonal_tiling#Floret_pentagonal_tiling) supplies the six petals, angles, chirality, p6 symmetry and dual relationship; coordinates and edge ratios are authored derivations from adjacent regular-polygon centers. The base pentagon is (0,0), (3/7,−√3/21), (4/7,√3/21), (1/2,√3/6), (2/7,4√3/21), rotated six ways on a 7 × 8 triangular lattice, with inradius 1/(2√7). Ninety edge-plane values, 336 seeded tones and 96² grain total 77,136 B of Float64 tables, plus three JS tone numbers and initialization vertices, not total RAM. Sampling checks 3 × 3 owners with a √(1/3) radius rejection, without allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots; generic detail is skipped. Palette 36,66,42 / 236,218,150, roughness 0.3, relief 0.012, grout 0.012, shoulder 0.008 and the nominal 0.84 m tile are authored settings. Four opaque dielectric maps, optional height and existing Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged; no real tile dimensions, glaze or installation certification is claimed.

`snubtrihexagonaltiles` adds a chiral 3.3.3.3.6 tiling: indigo regular hexagons, ivory side triangles and tan middle triangles in rough grout, with four equilateral triangles and one regular hexagon at every vertex. Unlike `trihexagonaltiles` (3.6.3.6) or `snubsquaretiles` (triangles and squares), it has no reflection symmetry. [Wikipedia Snub trihexagonal tiling](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snub_trihexagonal_tiling) supplies the vertex configuration, snub operation, missing reflection symmetry and floret pentagonal dual; its retrieved text has no coordinates, so the unit triangular lattice, side 1/√7, turn atan(√3/5), 7 × 8 points and chosen hand are authored constructions. Initialization enumerates equilateral triangles between neighboring hexagon corners: one hexagon and eight triangles per cell. The 198 face-geometry values, 504 tones and 96² grain total 79,344 B of Float64 tables, plus nine JS tone numbers and initialization scratch, not total RAM. Sampling queries a periodic 3 × 3 lattice and precomputed edge normals without per-point allocation, hashing, trigonometry or square roots; generic detail is skipped. Palette 24,60,86 / 244,226,170, roughness 0.3, relief 0.012, grout 0.012 and shoulder 0.008 are authored approximations. Four opaque dielectric maps, metallic 0, optional height and existing Worker/cache/ownership remain unchanged; no real tile, glaze or installation certification is claimed.

At seeds 0/7/0xffffffff the 768² half-height raster has 504 faces: 56 hexagons and 448 triangles. All 336 vertices join one hexagon and four triangles, with no stray corners. Inset-area error is ≤0.134% for hexagons and ≤0.887% for triangles; grout-fraction error ≤0.001601. Measured hexagon turn error is ≤3.312° (4° raster-corner tolerance), side-triangle centroid-angle error ≤0.099° and lattice-neighbor distance error ≤0.000349. There are 336 ivory and 112 tan faces; seeds change only face tones and grain. Six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 covers a grid, lattice lines and twelve offset directions: worst 0.0030842 < 0.04; signed-UV period error ≤7.11e-15. At 512 the maps have grout fraction 0.19855, roughness gap 0.57049, luma range 156.93, AO 128–250 and maximum tilt² 0.88892; at 32 these are 0.21875, 0.54052, 155.07, 128–250 and 0.029558. Fourteen legal variants pass all seventeen cases and fifteen faults are rejected, including the mirrored hand (a valid tiling that violates this preset's chosen-hand contract). Grout 0.016 and a 9 × 5 lattice failed raster corner classification and are not certified legal variants. All 141,792 sampled channel values of 844 old kinds remain identical, as do 54 sets / 270 map buffers from nine related kinds. Full-suite and renderer/CSP evidence is recorded in ACCEPTANCE.

`deltoidaltrihexagonaltiles` adds an authored deltoidal trihexagonal (V3.4.6.4) tiling: the hexagon of points nearest to each point of a triangular lattice is cut into six kites by six spokes to its side midpoints, and the kites around a point take burgundy, rose and ivory glaze in turn, so the three kites of every lattice triangle also differ; the kites sit in bevelled glaze over rougher gray grout, and every straight edge lies on one of six parallel line families 30° apart. It is not `kisrhombilletiles` (30-60-90 triangles, V4.6.12), `trihexagonaltiles` (hexagons and triangles, 3.6.3.6), `rhombille` (three lozenges per hexagon), its dual `rhombitrihexagonaltiles` (3.4.6.4) or the other existing tile presets, and a search of README/PLAN/DESIGN/CURRENT and the preset catalog for 3.4.6.4 duals, deltoidal, tetrakis/triakis/floret and prismatic pentagonal found only the `kisrhombilletiles` entry that lists this tiling among related tilings; no preset implemented it. The actually read deltoidal section of the Wikipedia Rhombitrihexagonal tiling page (the redirect target of Deltoidal trihexagonal tiling) supplies: the faces are kites, the type is a dual semiregular (face-transitive) tiling, the face configuration is V3.4.6.4, the symmetry group is p6m [6,3] (*632) with rotation group p6, the dual is the rhombitrihexagonal tiling, Conway called it the tetrille, it relates to the trihexagonal tiling by dividing the triangles and hexagons into central triangles and merging neighboring triangles into kites, and face-transitive variations can distort the kites (p31m). The fetched text gives no coordinates, angles or edge lengths, so the construction on a triangular lattice of pitch 1 (hexagon apothem 0.5 cut by spokes through the side midpoints, so lattice points have degree 6, side midpoints degree 4 and lattice-triangle centers degree 3), the kite angles 60°, 90°, 120° and 90° (360° divided by those vertex degrees) with long and short sides in the ratio √3, and every other number are authored: the tones 0.1, 0.48 and 0.82 plus up to 0.12 per kite, the 102,34,44 / 238,228,204 palette, glaze roughness 0.3 against grout 0.92, relief 0.012, grout half-width 0.014 and bevel shoulder 0.01 in lattice units, and a 7 × 8 lattice (7 columns and 4 row pairs, 1% off square) per UV tile of 0.84 m (an authored lattice pitch of 0.12 m). No real tile dimensions, glaze or installation specification is claimed, and no image is copied or bundled. The independent kernel stores 336 per-kite tones, 14 spoke components and a 96² grain, 76,528 B of tables, not total RAM; each point looks at nine lattice candidates and the six kites of the nearest one, so no square root, trigonometry, hash or allocation runs per point; generic detail is skipped and mortar is the grout. Seeds change only the per-kite tone and grain while the geometry stays fixed; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency or displacement.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: a 768 × 768 raster at the half-height contour is labelled into connected kite faces and each face's corners are read from its radial extent peaks. Every seed has 336 faces (6 kites for each of the 7 × 8 = 56 lattice points), each with 4 corners and an area within 1.07% of the predicted inset kite (allowed 5%; the inset is the grout plus half the shoulder, and the kite is tangential so it shrinks to a similar kite), and a grout fraction within 0.00079 of the prediction (allowed 0.012). The interior angles of all 336 kites are within 3.57° of 60°, 90°, 90° and 120° (allowed 6°), the edge directions within 3.58° of multiples of 30° (allowed 6°) and the long-to-short side ratio within 4.16% of √3 (allowed 10%). Around each of the 56 lattice points exactly six kite centroids lie within 0.5 pitch, equally distant within 2.15% (allowed 4%), spaced 60° within 1.29° (allowed 4°) on the axes 30° off the spokes within 1.25° (allowed 4°), with consecutive kites in different tone classes and kites opposite each other in the same class. The three tone classes hold 112 kites each, with class means 0.343 and 0.375 apart (above 0.25), grout rougher than glaze by 0.5753 (above 0.4), glaze brighter in occlusion by 0.4564 (above 0.3) and no metallic pixels; seeds 7 and 0xffffffff keep every kite at the same place (centroid gap below 0.5 px) and move kite tone by up to 0.1147/0.1159 (above 0.01). Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, the lattice column and row lines, sweeps along all six 30°-apart line families through every lattice point at offsets of 0, 0.25 and 0.5 pitch, and curvature refinement: worst 0.0016687 (below 0.04) and signed-UV period error at most 3.6e-14 (below 5e-10). The 512 map has grout fraction 0.18589, grout minus glaze roughness 0.57038, luma range 152.28, AO 128-250, maximum tilt² 0.87957 (0.1-3) and all four signed normal directions at 0.1108-0.1214 (above 0.02); the 32 map has grout fraction 0.21875, roughness gap 0.59999, luma range 150.7 and tilt² 0.029558 (below 0.5). A regression control accepted 15 legal variants (12 preset variants: cobalt and forest glaze palettes, glaze roughness 0.2 and 0.4, relief 0.008 and 0.016, grout 0.01 and 0.02, bevel shoulder 0.006 and 0.014, and 5 × 3 and 9 × 5 lattices; plus three source variants: quintic bevel ramps, milder per-kite tone variation and shifted tone levels) and rejected 13 genuine faults (4 preset faults: no grout, grout of 0.12, glaze as rough as the grout, unresolved relief; 9 source faults: two tones only, an oversized hexagon apothem of 0.56, spokes rotated 30 degrees, no grout along the spokes, linear bevel creases, metallic glaze, seed ignored by the tone, seed shifting the lattice, a square lattice without the row offset). Two faults were rejected only marginally and are recorded as such: the oversized apothem of 0.56 by an area deviation of 5.33% against the 5% bound, and the missing grout along the spokes by 5.97% against 5%; the other faults failed by large margins (for example 8 faces instead of 336 without grout and 596.9 against 0.04 for the creased bevel). Compatibility: all 843 old kinds × four seeds × seven points (23,604 samples, 141,624 channel comparisons) and ten related kinds (`kisrhombilletiles`, `rhombitrihexagonaltiles`, `trihexagonaltiles`, `truncatedtrihexagonaltiles`, `snubsquaretiles`, `elongatedtriangulartiles`, `rhombille`, `hexagontiles`, `cairotiles`, `soroban`) × sizes 32/512 × three seeds (60 map sets, 300 buffers) are identical to the previous build; presets and tile sizes of the old kinds are unchanged. Incidents recorded honestly: the first version alternated only two tones around each lattice point, which made the three kites of every lattice triangle one color and hid the kites (seen in the prototype before any test), so it now takes three tones in turn; and the first fixture used not.toBe, which the nested control harness does not support (the first control run stopped at its first legal variant), so the comparison was rewritten and the whole control run repeated.

`soroban` adds an authored soroban tile: a seven-rod section of a Japanese abacus, biconical wooden beads on seven bamboo-like rods between a frame bar above and below and a reckoning bar, the beads showing 4567890 and the reckoning bar carrying a dot on every third rod. It is not `seedbeads` (loose seed beads) or any other existing preset, and a search of README/PLAN/DESIGN/CURRENT and the preset catalog for abacus, soroban, suanpan/reckoning and bicone/biconical found no abacus preset. The actually read Wikipedia Soroban page supplies the structure: an odd number of rods and never fewer than seven (standard models have thirteen), one five-bead (go-dama) above and four one-beads (ichi-dama) below a reckoning bar on each rod, a bi-quinary coded decimal where beads moved towards the reckoning bar are on (the five-bead moves down, the one-beads move up), the unit-rod dot on every third rod, usually biconal (double-cone) beads of wood and rods of wood, metal, rattan or bamboo, and the caption of its modern soroban whose right side shows 1234567890 with lower beads as ones and upper beads as fives. The page gives no dimensions, bead proportions, colors or frame layout, so the seven-rod section showing the last seven digits 4567890 of that caption, the dots on the first, fourth and seventh rod counted from the right, the frame bars 0.52 and the reckoning bar 0.5 thick in units of the rod pitch, the bead slot of 0.78 with beads 0.9 wide and 0.78 tall (a double-cone silhouette with a rounded equator ridge, height 0.7 rising to 1.0 relief units at the centre), the rods 0.1 wide, the dots of radius 0.11, the 48,28,18 / 228,198,142 palette, tones (board 0.6 + 0.08 × grain, beads 0.3-0.4, frame 0.1, reckoning bar 0.16, rods 0.88, dots 0.95), roughness 0.8 on the board and 0.45 on the beads, relief 0.03 and bead edge softness 0.08 are authored approximations; no real soroban dimensions, materials or teaching correctness is claimed, the frame sides are not drawn so the tile repeats as a longer abacus section, and no image is copied or bundled. One UV tile represents 0.056 m (seven rods at an authored 8 mm pitch). The independent kernel stores the seven digits and a 128² wood grain, about 131,128 B of tables (7 Float64 values and 16,384 Float64 grain values), not total RAM; each point handles the one rod it lies on and the one bead slot it lies in with at most seven smooth ramps, so no hypot, square root, trigonometry, hash or allocation runs per point; generic detail is skipped and mortar stays 0. Seeds change only the wood grain while the beads stay fixed; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency or displacement.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: the test rebuilds the bi-quinary rod rules from the page (for every digit 0-9 exactly one of the two upper slots and four of the five lower slots hold a bead, and reading them back gives the digit; the shown digits equal the last seven of 1234567890; 35 beads on seven rods), reads the sampler at the centre of all 7 slots of each rod and decodes them the way a user does (the five-bead at the bar means 5, the empty lower slot index counts the one-beads at the bar), and reads the reckoning bar at every rod for the dot. Every seed decodes 4567890 and the dot string 1001001 (rods 1, 4 and 7 from the right). A 1008 × 1008 pixel-centre raster labelled into connected raised-bead components at a height of 0.6 relief units gives 35 beads, 0 of them more than 0.02 rod pitch from the expected slot centre and 0 whose bounding box is more than 0.09 off the nominal 0.9 × 0.78 (the mask contour sits near 94% of the silhouette), the width halfway to the tips is 0.5 of the equator width within 0.041 (allowed 0.12, a double cone halves the width), the mean area is within 0.08% of the diamond area (allowed 15%), the equator ridge stands 0.1397 relief units above the shoulders (above 0.1) with 0 asymmetry, and a column between two rods crosses the bar threshold exactly four times at 0.52, 2.08, 2.58 and 6.48 within 0.033 (allowed 0.05). Stacking at probes: bead minus frame height 0.55, frame minus rod 0.25 and rod minus board 0.2 relief units, dot minus reckoning bar tone 0.79, rod minus board tone 0.235-0.244, board minus bead tone 0.236-0.245, board minus bead roughness 0.35, metallic violations 0. Seeds 7 and 0xffffffff flip 0 of 1,016,064 bead-mask pixels, decode the same digits and move the wood tone up to 0.0794/0.0781 (above 0.01). Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, sweeps horizontally, vertically and on both diagonals through each of the 49 bead slots (silhouette edges, equator ridge and the tips where beads meet) and across the bar, rod and dot edges, and curvature refinement: worst 0.0000534 for all seeds (below 0.04), signed-UV period error at most 8.1e-16 (below 5e-10). The 512 map has polished-pixel fraction 0.283825, board minus bead roughness 0.3085, luma range 143.9, AO 217-247, maximum tilt² 0.98747 (0.1-3) and all four signed normal directions at 0.1497-0.1604 (above 0.005); the 32 map keeps the same structure at fraction 0.277344, roughness gap 0.3131, tilt² 0.26282 and directions at 0.21-0.29. A regression control accepted 13 legal variants (10 preset variants: lighter and darker wood palettes, board roughness 0.7 and 0.9, bead roughness 0.35 and 0.55, relief 0.02 and 0.04, bead edge softness 0.05 and 0.12; plus three source variants: quintic ramps, a finer wood grain and larger rod dots) and rejected 18 genuine faults (4 preset faults: beads as rough as the board, a board smoother than the beads, unresolved relief, bead edges softened by 0.6; 14 source faults: last digit 1 instead of 0, inverted five-bead states, a mirrored one-bead gap, dots on every second rod, no unit-rod dots, flat beads without the equator ridge, beads wider than the rod pitch, square creased beads, a frame higher than the beads, no reckoning bar, missing rods, metallic beads, seed ignored by the grain, seed moving the digits). Compatibility: all 842 old kinds × four seeds × seven points (23,576 samples, 141,456 channel comparisons) and ten related kinds (`ean13label`, `embossedbraille`, `papertape`, `sevensegment`, `tactilepaving`, `dominoes`, `punchcard`, `seedbeads`, `wiremesh`, `bamboo`) × sizes 32/512 × three seeds (60 map sets, 300 buffers) are identical to the previous build; presets and tile sizes of the old kinds are unchanged. Incidents recorded honestly: the first kernel drew the beads smaller than their nominal size (0.842 × 0.737 instead of 0.9 × 0.78) because the rounded ridge of the double cone offset the silhouette by 0.025, which the bead-size check rejected for all 35 beads, so the kernel now subtracts that offset and rescales the silhouette to the nominal half sizes; the first 512/32 px check required a board-bead roughness gap above 0.25, equal to the gap of the legal board-roughness 0.7 variant (measured 0.2418), so it was relaxed to 0.15; and an unused helper was removed after lint flagged it.

`ean13label` adds an authored EAN-13 barcode label tile: the page's example number 4 003994 155486 printed as 30 black bars on pale paper, the start, centre and end guard bars longer than the rest, the ink slightly rougher and a little raised above the paper. It is not `papertape` (punched ASCII holes), `sevensegment` or any other existing striped preset, and a search of README/PLAN/DESIGN/CURRENT and the preset catalog for barcode, EAN-13/EAN13, UPC, QR code and label found no barcode preset (the barcode hits are search-word lists in the neighbouring `papertape` entries; label hits are unrelated entries). The actually read Wikipedia International Article Number page supplies the structure: 95 equal-width areas (3 start areas 101, 42 areas for the six digits after the first, 5 centre areas 01010, 42 areas for the last six digits, 3 end areas 101), the L-, G- and R-codes of the digits 0-9 (R-codes are the bitwise complements of the L-codes and G-codes are the R-codes in reverse bit order, each digit two bars and two spaces), the table that chooses L or G for the left six digits from the first digit (LGLLGG for 4), the example 4 003994 155486 with parities OEOOEE EEEEEE, and the check digit rule (weights alternating 3 and 1 with the last data digit weighing 3; the worked example 400638133393x gives 89 and x = 1). The fetched page text gives no X-dimension, quiet zone, bar height, printing or text layout, so the 9-area quiet zones on both sides (113 areas across, a square tile), the bars starting 14 areas from the top and running 70 areas down with the guard bars running 5 areas further, the blank band below where printed digits would go (no text is drawn), the paper tone 0.86 + 0.08 × grain against an ink tone of 0.04, the 22,22,26 / 246,244,236 palette, roughness 0.55 on paper and 0.8 on ink, ink 0.3 relief units higher than the paper (relief 0.012), occlusion 0.98 falling by 0.03 on ink, and bar edges that are soft by 0.1 area on each side of every module boundary are authored approximations; no GS1 conformance, scanner readability, print quality or product identification is claimed, and no image is copied or bundled. One UV tile represents 0.0373 m (113 areas at an authored 0.33 mm X-dimension). The independent kernel stores the 113-area bit table, 113 bar-bottom values and a 128² grain, 132,089 B of tables (113 B, 113 Float64 values and 16,384 Float64 grain values), not total RAM; each point picks one module, blends at most two module columns across a boundary with a smooth step and multiplies two smooth steps for the bar ends, so no hypot, square root, trigonometry, hash or allocation runs per point; generic detail is skipped and mortar stays 0. Seeds change only the paper grain while the bars stay fixed; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency or displacement.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: the test types the L-, G- and R-code tables and the first-digit structure table from the page itself (checking that the R-codes complement the L-codes, the G-codes reverse the R-codes, every code has two bars and two spaces, the page's example encodes and decodes to 4 003994 155486 with parity OEOOEE EEEEEE, and the check digits are 1 for 400638133393 and 6 for 400399415548), then reads the sampler at the centre of each of the 95 areas on three rows through the bars (at 20, 50 and 80 areas from the top) and decodes the bits as a scanner would (markers, six left codes choosing L or G, the first digit from the L/G pattern, six right codes, check digit). Every seed reads 4003994155486 with a valid check digit and parity OEOOEEEEEEEE from all three rows, 0 mismatched areas and 0 mismatches on a guard row at 86.5 areas, below the normal bars. A 1130 × 1130 pixel-centre raster (10 px per area) labelled into connected ink components gives 30 bars (the reference has 30 runs: 19 of one area, 4 of two, 6 of three and 1 of four) whose left, right, top and bottom all lie within 0.06 area of the reference (tops at 14, normal bars ending at 84 and guard bars at 89), 0 ink pixels in the quiet zones or outside the bar band, and an ink fraction matching the reference 0.27097 within 1%; the first bar edge crosses 50% ink at the module boundary within 0.02 area and its 10-90% width is 0.1217 area (allowed 0.03-0.3).
