Adjacency, probed by rays through the middle of every tile edge, shows all 56 dodecagons alternating square and hexagon sides over their 12 neighbors, all 112 hexagons alternating square and dodecagon sides, and all 168 squares alternating dodecagon and hexagon sides — the 4.6.12 incidence rather than a raw vertex count. Dodecagon colors are 0.8279–0.9463, hexagons 0.3079–0.4088 and squares 0.09059–0.193 (mean gaps 0.5269–0.5346 and 0.2147–0.2223, above 0.4 and 0.1), grout roughness 0.9014 against tile 0.3168–0.317 (gap above 0.4), AO 0.5147 against 0.9785 (gap above 0.3), and 0 metallic violations; seeds 7 and 0xffffffff move no tile centroid (0 px, below 0.5) while tile tone moves up to 0.1024/0.1061 (above 0.01). Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, column and node-row border lines and curvature refinement along eight lines per axis; the worst value is 0.0054501 for all three seeds (below 0.04) and the signed-UV period error is at most 7.2e-16 (below 5e-10). The 512 pixel map has grout fraction 0.07071 (0.02–0.3), grout/tile roughness 0.89191/0.31898, AO 128–250, luma 58.1728–222.1072 (range above 80), maximum tilt² 0.88043 (0.1–3) and signed direction fractions 0.062–0.0732 (above 0.02); the 32 px map keeps grout fraction 0.15625, roughness 0.92157/0.31544, AO 128–250, luma 59.1006–221.8224 and tilt² 0.02956 (below 0.5), so only the macro tiling survives at 32. A regression control accepted 11 legal variants (10 preset variants over palette, glaze roughness 0.22/0.42, relief, grout 0.02/0.045 and shoulder 0.012/0.03, plus a quintic-smoothstep source variant) and rejected 19 genuine faults (7 preset faults: 0.5 grout that erases the squares, 0.3 shoulders, no color range, glaze as rough as the grout, unresolved or extreme relief, a 5-column stretched lattice; 12 source faults: no hexagons, no downward hexagons, no squares, all squares in one direction, wrong lattice pitch, row wrap without the lattice shift, linear edge shoulders, metallic tiles, squares as light as dodecagons, grout as smooth as glaze, seed-moved lattice, seed-ignored tone). Compatibility: all 830 old kinds × four seeds × seven points (23,240 comparisons) and ten related tile kinds (`snubsquaretiles`, `rhombitrihexagonaltiles`, `elongatedtriangulartiles`, `trihexagonaltiles`, `dodecagontiles`, `cairotiles`, `righttriangletiles`, `rhombille`, `octagondot`, `windmillpavers`) × sizes 32/512 × three seeds (60 map sets) are byte-identical to the previous build; presets, tiles and limits of old kinds are unchanged and the five code catalogs stay unique with old order retained. Incident recorded honestly: the kind was first integrated as `greatrhombitrihexagonaltiles`, but the page actually read is titled Truncated trihexagonal tiling and its alternate-name list was empty, so before commit my uncommitted feature edits were reverted and redone under the verifiable name, with the fixture and the 11/19 regression control rerun and giving the same results.

`elongatedtriangulartiles` adds an authored elongated triangular (3.3.3.4.4) glazed tiling: terracotta unit-edge squares in upright rows alternate with cream rows of up and down equilateral triangles, successive square rows are offset by half an edge, and every vertex meets three triangles and two squares side by side; every face has a narrow bevelled edge inside rougher gray grout. It has the same three-triangle, two-square vertex content as `snubsquaretiles` but in a different order (there the squares are turned ±15° and never share an edge; here they are upright and share vertical edges in rows), and it is not the existing hexagon–triangle (`trihexagonaltiles`), `rhombitrihexagonaltiles` (3.4.6.4), Cairo-pentagon or right-triangle tile presets; a search of README/PLAN/DESIGN/CURRENT/TECHNICAL and the preset catalog for elongated triangular and 3.3.3.4.4 found no preset with this tiling. The actually read Wikipedia Elongated triangular tiling page (a semiregular tiling with three triangles and two squares on each vertex, a triangular tiling elongated by rows of squares, Schläfli symbol {3,6}:e, similar to the snub square tiling but with a different vertex order, Conway's isosnub quadrille) supplies the topology; the geometry is derived and numerically checked rather than copied: for unit edges one period is 1 wide and 2 + √3 ≈ 3.73205 tall with 2 squares and 4 triangles (one square row, an up/down triangle row, a square row shifted by 0.5, a second triangle row), and 300,000 random points in 15 × 4 periods each land in exactly one polygon (no gaps or overlaps). The 15 columns × 4 periods per UV tile (120 squares + 240 triangles, a 0.5% wider-than-tall tile stretched onto the square UV tile), 0.035 grout per side (0.07 edge in total), 0.02 shoulder, terracotta/cream palette (112,48,36 / 232,214,170), glaze roughness 0.34, grout roughness 0.92, 0.012 UV-relative relief and the 1.2 m nominal tile (edge 0.08 m) are authored approximations; no tile thickness, glaze chemistry, grout material, size standard or product is claimed. The independent kernel stores 12 polygon-center values, 48 edge-normal values, 360 per-tile tones and a 96² grain, 9,636 Float64 values / 77,088 B of tables, not total RAM, plus three six-entry constants; each point tests the 3 × 3 period-cell neighborhood × 6 polygons (54 squared circumradius checks) and evaluates one smooth half-plane per edge only for polygons that can contain it, so no hypot, square root, trigonometry, hash or allocation runs per point; generic detail is skipped. Each face is a product of smooth half-planes inset by the grout, so neighboring supports never overlap and corners need no smooth minimum. Seeds change only tile tone (squares 0.1–0.22, triangles 0.78–0.92) and fine grain while the lattice stays fixed; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency or displacement.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: a 768×768 raster (about 51.32 px per unit edge) is thresholded at relief-normalized height 0.41, flood-filled with periodic wrapping, and each tile is classified by its 720-ray radial corner count. Every seed yields 360 tiles, exactly 120 four-corner squares and 240 three-corner triangles (1:2 per period) and no other shapes; areas are squares 0.8208–0.8386 edge² (preset-derived inset prediction 0.8281) and triangles 0.3041–0.314 (0.3086), all within 5%, and the grout fraction is 0.22502 against a predicted 0.22554 (within 0.012). Squares stay upright (corner angle 45–45.5°, 45° within 5°) in two row classes of 60 each whose x phases differ by half an edge; each square's two nearest squares are 0.9961–1.006 edges away (the same-row neighbors, 1 within 0.03) and the next four are 1.931–1.934 edges away (1.9319 within 0.03) with horizontal offsets 0.498–0.5078 (0.5 within 0.03); triangles split 120 + 120 at 30–31° and 87–88.5° (30°/90° within 5°). Adjacency, probed by rays through the middle of every tile edge, shows all 120 squares alternating square and triangle sides and all 240 triangles touching one square and two triangles — the 3.3.3.4.4 incidence rather than a raw vertex count. Square colors are 0.1102–0.2295 and triangle colors 0.7802–0.9241 (mean gap 0.6775–0.6841, above 0.5), grout roughness 0.8997 against tile 0.3612–0.3615 (gap above 0.4), AO 0.5173 against 0.9746 (gap above 0.3), and 0 metallic violations; seeds 7 and 0xffffffff move no tile centroid (0 px, below 0.5) while tile tone moves up to 0.1305/0.1244 (above 0.01). Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, column and row border lines and curvature refinement along eight lines per axis; the worst value is 0.0013534 for all three seeds (below 0.04) and the signed-UV period error is at most 5.0e-16 (below 5e-10). The 512 pixel map has grout fraction 0.23863 (0.04–0.4), grout/tile roughness 0.88006/0.35871, AO 128–250, luma 76.4604–204.4322 (range above 80), maximum tilt² 0.88892 (0.1–3) and every signed direction fraction 0.0965–0.1192 (above 0.02); the 32 px map keeps grout fraction 0.1406, roughness 0.89572/0.36492, AO 128–250, luma 77.3882–203.4322 and tilt² 0.02956 (below 0.5), so only the macro tiling survives at 32. A regression control accepted 12 legal variants (11 preset variants over palette, glaze roughness 0.22/0.44, relief, an 11-column × 3-period lattice, grout 0.02/0.05 and shoulder 0.012/0.03, plus a quintic-smoothstep source variant) and rejected 17 genuine faults (7 preset faults: 0.25 grout that erases triangles, 0.3 shoulders, no color range, glaze as rough as the grout, unresolved or extreme relief, a 9-column stretched lattice; 10 source faults: no triangles, no downward triangles, square rows not offset, wrong row period, linear edge shoulders, metallic tiles, triangles as dark as squares, grout as smooth as glaze, seed-moved lattice, seed-ignored tone). Compatibility: all 829 old kinds × four seeds × seven points (23,212 comparisons) and ten related tile kinds (`snubsquaretiles`, `rhombitrihexagonaltiles`, `trihexagonaltiles`, `dodecagontiles`, `cairotiles`, `righttriangletiles`, `rhombille`, `octagondot`, `windmillpavers`, `subwaytiles`) × sizes 32/512 × three seeds (60 map sets) are byte-identical to the previous build; presets, tiles and limits of old kinds are unchanged and the five code catalogs stay unique with old order retained. Incident recorded honestly: the first 32 px roughness-gap bound of 0.4 rejected the legal 0.44 glaze variant (measured gap 0.3927, because 32 px bevel pixels blend roughness); the bound is now 0.3 and all 17 faults are still rejected.

`rhombitrihexagonaltiles` adds an authored rhombitrihexagonal (3.4.6.4) glazed tiling: regular hexagons on a triangular lattice, a square on every lattice edge turned to one of three directions 60° apart, and an equilateral triangle at the center of every lattice triangle, so each vertex meets a triangle, a square, a hexagon and a second square; the hexagons are cream glaze, the squares slate blue, the triangles navy, and every face has a narrow bevelled edge inside rougher gray grout. It is not `snubsquaretiles` (3.3.4.3.4), `trihexagonaltiles` (hexagons and triangles), `dodecagontiles` (canonical 3.12.12) or the other existing tile presets, and a search of README/PLAN/DESIGN/CURRENT/TECHNICAL and the preset catalog for rhombitri and 3.4.6.4 found no preset with this vertex type. The actually read Wikipedia Rhombitrihexagonal tiling page (a semiregular tiling with one triangle, two squares and one hexagon at each vertex, Schläfli symbol rr{3,6}, an expanded hexagonal tiling, related to the truncated trihexagonal tiling) supplies the topology; the geometry is derived and numerically checked rather than copied: for unit edges hexagon centers sit on a triangular lattice of pitch 1 + √3 ≈ 2.73205 (apothem √3/2 + square edge 1 + apothem √3/2), the hexagon edges face the lattice directions, each lattice edge carries a square centered on its midpoint, each lattice triangle holds one triangle at its centroid, a cell is 1 hexagon + 3 squares + 2 triangles of area (1 + √3)²√3/2 ≈ 6.4641, and 300,000 random points in the 7 × 4 supercell each land in exactly one polygon (no gaps or overlaps). The 7 columns × 4 double rows per UV tile (56 hexagons + 168 squares + 112 triangles, a 1.04% wider-than-tall supercell stretched onto the square UV tile, the same near-square convention as the existing 7 × 8 hexagonal tile presets), 0.03 grout per side (0.06 edge in total), 0.02 shoulder, navy/cream palette (20,52,88 / 244,232,200), glaze roughness 0.3, grout roughness 0.92, 0.012 UV-relative relief and the 1.2 m nominal tile (edge about 0.0628 m) are authored approximations; no tile thickness, glaze chemistry, grout material, size standard or product is claimed. The independent kernel stores 12 polygon-center values, 72 edge-normal values, 336 per-tile tones and a 96² grain, 9,636 Float64 values / 77,088 B of tables, not total RAM, plus three six-entry constants; each point tests the 3 × 3 lattice-node neighborhood × 6 polygons (54 squared circumradius checks) and evaluates one smooth half-plane per edge only for polygons that can contain it, so no hypot, square root, trigonometry, hash or allocation runs per point; generic detail is skipped. Each face is a product of smooth half-planes inset by the grout, so neighboring supports never overlap and corners need no smooth minimum; tone indices wrap across the supercell with the lattice shift of its slanted period. Seeds change only tile tone (hexagons 0.82–0.94, squares 0.3–0.4, triangles 0.08–0.18) and fine grain while the lattice stays fixed; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency or displacement.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: a 768×768 raster (about 40.37 px per unit edge) is thresholded at relief-normalized height 0.41, flood-filled with periodic wrapping, and each tile is classified by its 720-ray radial corner count. Every seed yields 336 tiles, exactly 56 six-corner hexagons, 168 four-corner squares and 112 three-corner triangles (1:3:2 per lattice cell) and no other shapes; areas are hexagons 2.352–2.376 edge² (preset-derived inset prediction 2.3637), squares 0.8396–0.8629 (0.8464) and triangles 0.3204–0.321 (0.3213), all within 5% (observed within 2%), and the grout fraction is 0.14111 against a predicted 0.14212 (within 0.012). Squares come in three orientation classes of 56 each with corner angles 14.5–17.5°, 45.5–46.5° and 72–74° (15°/45°/75° within 5°); triangles split 56 + 56 at 30–31.5° and 86–88.5° (30°/90° within 5°); hexagon corners sit at 29.5–31.5° (30° within 5°), and each hexagon's six nearest hexagons are 2.714–2.74 edges away (pitch 2.7321, within 3%). Adjacency, probed by rays through the middle of every tile edge, shows all 56 hexagons touching six squares, all 168 squares alternating hexagon and triangle sides, and all 112 triangles touching three squares — the 3.4.6.4 incidence rather than a raw vertex count. Hexagon colors are 0.8264–0.9468, squares 0.3097–0.4127 and triangles 0.09205–0.1939 (mean gaps 0.5189–0.5276 and 0.2152–0.2233, above 0.4 and 0.1), grout roughness 0.8956 against tile 0.3195 (gap above 0.4), AO 0.5193 against 0.9764 (gap above 0.3), and 0 metallic violations; seeds 7 and 0xffffffff move no tile centroid (0 px, below 0.5) while tile tone moves up to 0.1038/0.1082 (above 0.01). Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, column and node-row border lines and curvature refinement along eight lines per axis; the worst value is 0.0026823 for all three seeds (below 0.04) and the signed-UV period error is at most 5.0e-16 (below 5e-10). The 512 pixel map has grout fraction 0.15347 (0.04–0.35), grout/tile roughness 0.87118/0.31775, AO 128–250, luma 63.0006–224.0322 (range above 120), maximum tilt² 0.88043 (0.1–3) and every signed direction fraction 0.098 (above 0.02); the 32 px map keeps grout fraction 0.125, roughness 0.90689/0.31509, AO 128–250, luma 65.141–223.1044 and tilt² 0.01479 (below 0.5), so only the macro tiling survives at 32. A regression control accepted 11 legal variants (10 preset variants over palette, glaze roughness 0.22/0.42, relief, grout 0.02/0.04 and shoulder 0.012/0.03, plus a quintic-smoothstep source variant) and rejected 18 genuine faults (7 preset faults: 0.25 grout that erases triangles, 0.3 shoulders, no color range, glaze as rough as the grout, unresolved or extreme relief, a 5-column stretched lattice; 11 source faults: no triangles, no downward triangles, all squares in one direction, wrong lattice pitch, row wrap without the lattice shift, linear edge shoulders, metallic tiles, squares as light as hexagons, grout as smooth as glaze, seed-moved lattice, seed-ignored tone). Compatibility: all 828 old kinds × four seeds × seven points (23,184 comparisons) and ten related tile kinds (`snubsquaretiles`, `trihexagonaltiles`, `dodecagontiles`, `cairotiles`, `righttriangletiles`, `rhombille`, `hextruchettiles`, `octagondot`, `windmillpavers`, `starcrosstiles`) × sizes 32/512 × three seeds (60 map sets) are byte-identical to the previous build; presets, tiles and limits of old kinds are unchanged and the five code catalogs stay unique with old order retained. Incidents recorded honestly: the first fixture used a 3% area tolerance and a 0.07 grout floor at 32 px, which rejected the legal 0.02 narrow-grout variant (area error 3.09%, 32 px grout fraction 0.0654); the tolerances are now 5% and 0.04 and the 18 faults are still all rejected.

`snubsquaretiles` adds an authored snub square (3.3.4.3.4) glazed tiling: unit-edge squares alternately turned about +15° and −15° on a half-lattice checkerboard, with equilateral triangles filling every gap so each vertex meets three triangles and two squares; the squares are dark blue glaze, the triangles cream glaze, and every face has a narrow bevelled edge inside rougher gray grout. It is not the existing hexagon–triangle (`trihexagonaltiles`), Cairo-pentagon (`cairotiles`) or right-isosceles-triangle (`righttriangletiles`) tile presets, and a search of README/PLAN/DESIGN/CURRENT/TECHNICAL and the preset catalog for snub, 3.3.4.3.4 and Archimedean found no preset with this vertex type (the only hit was the Archimedean spiral of `cinnamonbarkcoils`). The actually read Wikipedia Snub square tiling page (a semiregular tiling with three triangles and two squares at every vertex, Schläfli symbol s{4,4}, related to the elongated triangular tiling 3.3.3.4.4) supplies the topology; the geometry is derived and numerically checked rather than copied from a source: for unit edges the square lattice constant is (1 + √3)/√2 ≈ 1.93185, squares sit on lattice nodes (turned +15°) and cell centers (turned −15°), the four outward equilateral triangles on each node square's edges complete the cell, and 200,000 random points each land in exactly one of the 2 squares + 4 triangles per cell (no gaps or overlaps, areas sum to the cell area). The 3 × 3 lattice cells per UV tile (18 squares + 36 triangles), 0.035 grout per side (0.07 edge in total), 0.02 shoulder, blue/cream palette (34,64,98 / 238,226,192), glaze roughness 0.3, grout roughness 0.92, 0.012 UV-relative relief and the 0.6 m nominal tile (edge about 0.1035 m) are authored approximations; no tile thickness, glaze chemistry, grout material, size standard or product is claimed. The independent kernel stores 12 polygon-center values, 48 edge-normal values, 54 per-tile tones and a 96² grain, 9,330 Float64 values / 74,640 B of tables, not total RAM, plus three six-entry constants; each point tests the 3 × 3 lattice-node neighborhood × 6 polygons (54 squared circumradius checks) and evaluates one smooth half-plane per edge only for polygons that can contain it, so no hypot, square root, trigonometry, hash or allocation runs per point; generic detail is skipped. Each face is a product of smooth half-planes inset by the grout, so neighboring supports never overlap and corners need no smooth minimum. Seeds change only tile tone (squares 0.1–0.22, triangles 0.78–0.92) and fine grain while the lattice stays fixed; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency or displacement.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: a 576×576 raster (192 px per lattice cell, 99.4 px per unit edge) is thresholded at relief-normalized height 0.41, flood-filled with periodic wrapping, and each tile is classified by its 720-ray radial corner count. Every seed yields 54 tiles, exactly 18 four-corner squares and 36 three-corner triangles (2:4 per lattice cell) and no other shapes; every square's area is 0.8281 edge² (the preset-derived inset-square prediction is 0.8281), every triangle's 0.3075 (prediction 0.3085), both within 3%, and the grout fraction is 0.22667 against a predicted 0.2255 (within 0.012). Squares come in two orientation classes of 9 + 9 with corner angles 30° and 59.5° (within 3° of 30°/60°); each square's four nearest squares are 1.366 edges away ((1 + √3)/2 = 1.3660, within 0.03) and all have the other orientation. Adjacency, probed by rays through the middle of every tile edge, shows all 18 squares touching four triangles and no square, and all 36 triangles touching one square of each orientation plus one triangle — the 3.3.4.3.4 incidence rather than a raw vertex count. Square colors are 0.1124–0.2273 and triangle colors 0.7872–0.9212 (mean gap 0.6731–0.6937, above 0.5), grout roughness 0.8955 against tile 0.3212 (gap above 0.4), AO 0.5194 against 0.975 (gap above 0.3), and 0 metallic violations; seeds 7 and 0xffffffff move no tile centroid (0 px, below 0.5) while tile tone moves up to 0.1266/0.1051 (above 0.01). Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, lattice-cell border lines and curvature refinement along eight lines per axis; the worst value is 0.00066724 for all three seeds (below 0.04) and the signed-UV period error is 8.1e-14 (below 5e-10). The 512 pixel map has grout fraction 0.2271 (0.12–0.4), grout/tile roughness 0.89575/0.32115, AO 128–250, luma 77.4218–215.0322 (range above 80), maximum tilt² 0.86837 (0.1–3) and every signed direction fraction 0.0699 (above 0.02); the 32 px map keeps grout fraction 0.2344, roughness 0.87721/0.32145, AO 128–250, luma 77.6344–213.8196 and tilt² 0.02956 (below 0.5), so only the macro tiling survives at 32. A regression control accepted 13 legal variants (12 preset variants over palette, glaze roughness 0.22/0.42, relief, 2/4 lattice cells, grout 0.025/0.05 and shoulder 0.012/0.03, plus a quintic-smoothstep source variant) and rejected 15 genuine faults (6 preset faults: 0.25 grout that erases the triangles, 0.3 shoulders, no color range, glaze as rough as the grout, unresolved or extreme relief; 9 source faults: squares only, both squares turned the same way, wrong lattice constant, linear edge shoulders, metallic tiles, one color for squares and triangles, grout as smooth as glaze, seed-moved lattice, seed-ignored tone). Compatibility: all 827 old kinds × four seeds × seven points (23,156 comparisons) and ten related tile kinds (`cairotiles`, `trihexagonaltiles`, `righttriangletiles`, `rhombille`, `diamondcheckertiles`, `pythagoreantiles`, `dodecagontiles`, `tuliptiles`, `starcrosstiles`, `windmillpavers`) × sizes 32/512 × three seeds (60 map sets) are byte-identical to the previous build; presets, tiles and limits of old kinds are unchanged and the five code catalogs stay unique with old order retained. Incident recorded honestly: the first kernel set grout roughness to `p.roughness + 0.6 * mortar`, so the legal 0.42 glaze variant reached 1.02 in the grout and the pipeline clamp produced a slope kink (worst 97.48 against 0.04); grout roughness is now a fixed 0.92 blended by the grout mask, and a glaze nearly as rough as the grout is rejected by the roughness-gap check.

`starfish` adds an authored five-armed sea-star approximation: most owner cells each hold one randomly rotated star made of a rounded central disc and five arms that taper from base to tip, with slightly irregular angular gaps and arm lengths, a raised dome and spine ridge along every arm, three rows of small tubercles along and beside the ridge, and a rougher darker gray-sand host between the stars. It is not Starcoral's corallite cups with radial septa, Staranise's eight woody pods around recessed seed cavities, Seaurchintest's ivory shell with paired pore rows, or the scalloped overlap family (`scalemail`, `fishscales`). The actually read Wikipedia Starfish article (a central disc with usually five arms, an aboral surface that is smooth, granular or spiny with overlapping plates, often red or orange, and a sieve-like madreporite) and the actually viewed Wikimedia Commons Starfish montage supply the shape idea; the silhouette of the orange specimen in the montage, measured with PIL from the downloaded image, gives five tips 171–183 px from the center, valleys at about 0.17–0.26 of the tip radius, arm widths of about 0.195 R at 0.5 R and 0.148 R at 0.85 R, and tip gaps of 63.5–88.5°. Other specimens in the same montage are narrower or wider (about 0.25–0.3 R for the wide horned sandy one), so these numbers describe one specimen, not the species. The 3 × 3 grid, exactly five arms, ±0.1 rad axis jitter, ±6% length jitter, 0.4 cell radius, 0.25 R disc, base/tip half-widths 0.1 R / 0.07 R, tubercle rows (pitch 0.05 cell, radius 0.017), gray-sand host, gray-to-orange palette, roughness 0.66, 0.02 UV-relative relief and the 0.3 m nominal tile are authored approximations: no species, madreporite, tube feet, spines, color calibration, real size or marine-biology data is claimed. The independent kernel stores 180 pose Float64 values (9 owners × 20) plus a 128² grain and a 16² host field, 16,820 Float64 values / 134,560 B of tables, not total RAM; each point picks the arm with the largest projection (no angle trigonometry), uses two hypot calls and twelve SmoothStep calls, and has no per-point allocation, hash or trigonometry (`cos`/`sin` only while building poses); generic detail is skipped. Arm width tapers with a smoothstep so the slope vanishes at the tip and base; arm-owned relief fades in only beyond 0.2 R–0.25 R from the center so the fold line where neighboring arms hand over shows no relief kink; disc and arms combine as `1 − (1 − arm)(1 − disc)`. Seeds change which owners hold a star, plus rotation, arm jitter, tone and fine grain; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency or real arm geometry.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: a 576×576 raster with 192 px cells, a 0.15 height-threshold star mask and 720 rays from each star centroid locate every star's tips, gaps, valleys, widths and tubercles. Each seed has 9/9/8 detected stars (above 0.75 × 9 − 1 and at most 9), each with exactly 5 tips; angular gaps are 62–82° (55–92°), tip radii 0.388–0.4766 cell (0.3–0.5), tip max/min 1.025–1.124 (below 1.25), valley/tip radius 0.1881–0.2216 (0.12–0.3), star area 0.113–0.1389 of its cell (0.08–0.2), and orientation concentration 0.247–0.406 (below 0.75), so rotation is random rather than fixed. Arm full width relative to tip radius is 0.1349–0.1693 at half radius (per-star mean 0.1–0.22) and 0.1112–0.1408 at 0.85 radius (above 0.09 and below the half-radius mean); the viewed orange specimen measured about 0.195 and 0.148, so this preset is slimmer. Spine tubercles number 4–8 per arm (each arm above 0, each star above 12 in total). Star coverage is 0.1111–0.1289 (0.07–0.2), star/host color gap 0.378–0.422 (above 0.3), roughness gap 0.277–0.279 (above 0.2) and AO gap 0.166–0.168 (above 0.12), with 0 metallic/mortar violations. Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, cell-border lines and curvature refinement along eight lines per axis; the worst value is 0.00010103/0.00008106/0.00011772 (below 0.04) and signed-UV period errors are at most 7.2e-15 (below 5e-10). The 512 pixel map has a star fraction of 0.12016 (0.07–0.2), star/host roughness 0.65736/0.93924, AO 156–255, luma 98.0166–147.3082, maximum tilt² 0.86087 (0.1–3) and every signed direction fraction 0.0688–0.0697 (above 0.02); the 32 px map keeps a face fraction of 0.12207, AO 156–254, luma 98.7318–147.3082 and tilt² 0.06807 (below 0.4), so only macro arms and shading survive at 32. A regression control accepted 29 legal variants (28 preset variants over palette, finish, relief, cells, star and disc radius, arm widths, arm/length/center jitter, shoulders, ridge, tubercles and chance, plus a quintic-smoothstep source variant) and rejected 22 genuine faults (14 preset faults: fat, hair-thin or outward-widening arms, huge disc, owner-clipped, tiny or sparse stars, tangled arms, very unequal arms, no tubercles, no color range, mirror finish, unresolved or extreme relief; 8 source faults: disc only, fixed orientation, metallic stars, linear shoulders, unfaded arm relief, star roughness equal to the host, no tubercle relief, inverted dome). Compatibility: all 826 old kinds × four seeds × seven points (23,128 comparisons) and ten related kinds (`starcoral`, `staranise`, `seaurchintest`, `scalemail`, `fishscales`, `cowrieshells`, `musselshells`, `scallopshell`, `oystershell`, `nautilusshell`) × sizes 32/512 × three seeds (60 map sets) are byte-identical to the previous build; presets, tiles and limits of old kinds are unchanged and the seven catalogs stay unique with old order retained. Incidents recorded honestly: the first draft used a linear taper, and a legal 0.56 roughness variant exposed a 0.155 slope kink at an arm tip (default roughness stayed below 0.04, so the default fixture alone would not have caught it), which the smoothstep taper fixed; the first legal 0.74 roughness variant was too close to the host (gap 0.19967, below 0.2) and was replaced by 0.72; per-arm tubercle counts ranged from 2 to 11 across legal variants, so only a loose total bound is asserted, and a 0.2-amplitude grain-speckle source fault plus the reintroduced linear-taper kink remain undetected by this fixture.

`fishscaleshingles` adds an authored fish-scale metal shingle approximation: eight courses of twelve semicircular lower-edge lobes, each shingle two lobes wide, alternate courses staggered by half a lobe, upper courses overlapping lower ones with an under-lap shadow, and narrow dark seams between slightly different painted shingles. It is not Hopsack/Waffleweave cloth cells, Slate/Tiles rectangular tiles, or Snowflaketiles/Circularpavers geometric paving. The actually read Berridge Fish Scale Shingles page (24-gauge steel, lightly textured rounded scalloped profile, each shingle covering 8 1/2 in tall by 11 1/2 in wide, 148 per square; 8.5 × 11.5 × 148 = 14,467 in², roughly consistent with 100 ft² = 14,400 in² (about 0.5% apart)) and two actually viewed white/beige product images (two semicircular lobes along each shingle's lower edge, neighboring courses offset and overlapping, faint wavy embossing) supply the geometry; the Yusheng fish-scale stone-coated tile page (small overlapping scalloped unit, about 2.8 tiles per m²) was read but none of its numbers are used. The Berridge coverage ratio gives a course-height to lobe-width ratio of 2 × 8.5 / 11.5 ≈ 1.478 for two lobes per shingle; this material's 12 lobes × 8 courses give 1.5 (about 1.5% away) and uses 12 × 5.75 in ≈ 1.75 m as the nominal tile. The sources and images give no readable course offset, lip thickness, shadow width, seam width, paint color or per-shingle tone difference, so the half-lobe stagger, lip, shadow, seam and palette values are authored approximations; no embossing, metallic reflection, steel bending, fasteners, specification certification or wood shingle is claimed. The independent kernel has only a 48-entry shingle tone table (8 × 6) and a 128² grain, 16,432 Float64 values / 131,456 B of tables, not total RAM; each point uses four hypot calls and twelve SmoothStep calls, merges the two nearest lobe distances with a polynomial smooth minimum (avoiding a slope crease along the distance-field medial axis), and has no per-point allocation, hash or trigonometry; generic detail is skipped. Each course computes its own color, height and AO before coverage blends them, so a seam's tone step stays hidden under the seam color instead of leaving a jump. The 0.012 UV-relative relief and palette are authored approximations; seeds change only shingle tone and fine grain while lobes, seams and courses stay fixed; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency, refraction or real overlap geometry. It belongs to the same scalloped-overlap family as the existing `scallopedslate` (4 columns × 6 rows, 1.2 m tile, one rounded tail per blue-grey slate tile, no seams between tiles), `fishscaletiles` (4 columns × 8 rows of coplanar glazed mosaic) and `scalemail`, so treat it as a variant derived from the Berridge coverage ratio — 12 lobes × 8 courses, two lobes per shingle, narrow seams with painted tone differences, a 1.75 m tile and under-lap shadows — rather than a new roof-tile class.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: a 576×576 raster with 48 px lobes, shifted by half a course so boundaries stay inside it, locates each course's lower edge from height steps and then measures lobes, semicircularity, course pitch, stagger and seams. The preset must have 8–16 lobes and 6–10 courses; each seed has 8 courses of exactly 12 lobes (equal to the preset), lobe spacing spread 0 px (below 3), depth/lobe-width 0.5 (0.42–0.58), semicircle residual RMS 0.018803–0.018926 radius (below 0.06), and step-detection failure columns of 0 for the standard preset (below 15%; soft cusp columns are interpolated circularly). Course pitch is 71.992–72.007 px (within 72 ± 2) and neighboring-course lobe offset is 24 px (within half a lobe, 24 ± 3). Each course has 6 seams (equal to lobes/2), seam spacing spread 0 px (below 3), seam/face color drop 0.26329–0.32178 (above 0.15), neighboring-course seam offset 24 px (24 ± 3), and per-shingle mean-color deviation 0.02679–0.02943 (0.01–0.08). Minimum AO under the upper edge is 0.55392 (below 0.7), calm AO is 1 (above 0.9), roughness is 0.5–0.53 (0.45–0.58), and metallic/mortar violations are 0. Seeds 0 and 7 differ by 0 px in lobe and seam positions (below 2) and by up to 0.09055 in shingle color (above 0.01), so only tone and grain move. Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, vertical lobe/seam lines, horizontal course/cusp lines and 4096 signed UV samples: worst is 0.000027756 for all seeds, below 0.04, with signed period at most 3.0087e-14. Actual 512 has face/backing roughness 0.51501/0.51507, AO 71–255, luma 144.33–185.83, max tilt² 0.41415 and signed normal fractions 0.053974/0.054024/0.063618/0.0072136; 32 px has AO 144–255, luma 169.83–185.76 and tilt² 0.0037216, retaining only macro shading and coarse normals. Thirty-one valid variants — cool/warm palettes, two roughness values, two reliefs, 10×6/14×8 lobes×courses, sharp/soft shoulders, short/long ramp, low/high base, low/high lip, light/dark shadow, narrow/wide shadow, narrow/wide seams, shallow/deep seams, weak/strong tone spread, light/dark seam color, weak/strong seam AO and quintic shoulders — pass all 18 cases. Twenty-two real faults are rejected with ERR_ASSERTION: absent/faint lip, coarse/fine lattice, no seams, absent/excessive tone spread, no shadow, near-black palette, no color range, mirror finish, unresolved/extreme relief, no stagger, seams without grooves, linear shoulders, metallic shingles, upward steps, no height ramp, diamond lobes, seed-moved lobes and noisy roughness; no color range is rejected only because the pixel observer yields NaN (not an independent diagnostic), extreme relief by the 32 px coarse-normal bound, mirror finish and noisy roughness by the roughness range and the near-black palette by the luma spread. Limits are in ACCEPTANCE.

`waffleweave` adds an authored waffle-weave cloth approximation: rounded float ridges on a fixed 14×16 lattice enclose recessed square pockets with shallow bowl floors, weft-direction (horizontal) ridges stand higher than warp-direction (vertical) ridges, and the two ridge directions rise into small knots where they cross. It is not Matelasse's raised quilted diamonds, Basketweave/Hopsack continuous basket floats, or Honeycomb's golden hexagonal comb. The actually read Wikipedia Waffle fabric (also called honeycomb fabric; long warp and weft floats around plain-weave centers form square ridges and hollows), Ashford's "The Squish is real" (long floats interlock on a grid, ridges outline the cells, pockets are the tighter interlacing, finishing puffs the floats) and LinenMe's waffle-towel guide (recessed cells and raised ridges; new cloth is flatter and washing makes the structure more pronounced), plus the actually viewed Wikimedia Commons "An example of waffle fabric" close-up (stronger horizontal ridges, rounded knots at crossings, hollow cells), are the sources; the Ashford and LinenMe page images were viewed but showed only a loom and hanging towels with no readable structure, so they are not appearance evidence. None gives cell sizes, ridge heights or warp/weft ridge ratios, so the 14×16 cells (about 8.6×7.5 mm on the 0.12 m tile), pocket width, 0.9/0.7 weft/warp ridge heights, bowl depth and knot strength are authored approximations; "horizontal ridges stronger" is one photograph's observation, not a fabric specification. No real warp/weft interlacement, weave draft, cloth physics, absorbency or cotton/linen/microfibre specification is claimed. The independent kernel has no per-cell table: only a 128² grain and 8² cloud, 16,448 Float64 values / 131,584 B of tables, not total RAM; each point uses two fract/abs reductions and at most four SmoothStep calls, with no allocation, hash, trigonometry or square root, and generic detail is skipped. The 0.12 m nominal tile, 0.012 UV-relative relief and cool grey-white palette are authored approximations; seeds move only fine grain and cloud tone while the lattice and ridge positions stay fixed; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparent holes, cloth simulation, real knit/woven structure or specification certification. It belongs to the same fabric family as the existing `wafflecloth` (8×8 symmetric raised rims; measured warp and weft crests differ by only about 0.6% and crossings are no higher than the crests), so treat it as a finer 14×16 variant rather than a new fabric class: here weft crests stand about 0.08 relief units above warp crests, pockets are bowl-shaped, each crossing adds a knot about 0.10 units above the weft crest, and the references are different.

Five behaviors use the actual sampler on three seeds without copying private parameters: a 896×1024 raster with 64 px cells yields each pocket mask, while pocket centers, vertical crests, horizontal crests and crossings are sampled exactly. Each seed must contain 224 cells (14×16; the preset must have 10–20 columns and 12–20 rows), pocket area of 0.25–0.45 of a cell (measured 0.34619–0.35059, cross-cell spread at most 0.00415 and required below 0.02), centroid offset below 0.02 cell (maximum 0.0021735), box width 0.5–0.8 (0.65625) and height 0.5–0.75 (0.59375), width/height 0.9–1.3 (1.1053), area/box fill above 0.75 (0.88847–0.89975; a circular fault measured 0.711–0.715), and edge-position spread below 0.1 cell (0.015625, one raster step). Each height class has cross-cell spread below 0.02 (0.003885–0.003966): pocket floor 0.1–0.25 (0.18005–0.18395), vertical crest above floor by more than 0.25 (0.31608–0.31612), horizontal crest above vertical by more than 0.05 (0.076033–0.076119), and crossing knot above horizontal by more than 0.07 (0.094304–0.094401) and below 0.8 (maximum 0.68218). Crossing/horizontal color gaps over the floor exceed 0.25/0.2 (0.30834–0.36058/0.2663–0.31154), AO gap exceeds 0.3 (0.49824), roughness is 0.78–0.97 (0.88–0.90996), and metallic/mortar violations are 0. Seeds 0 and 7 differ in only 0.002582 of pocket-mask pixels (below 0.005) with mean height difference 0.00097172 (between 1e-5 and 0.02), so only grain moves while the lattice stays fixed. Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized observed-curvature C1 includes a 128×128 grid, vertical/horizontal cell boundaries, pocket-edge scan lines and 4096 signed UV samples: worst is 0.000007228 for all three seeds, below 0.04, with signed period at most 2.2407e-15. Actual 512 has ridge-brightness fraction 0.46283, face/backing roughness 0.89516/0.89486 (difference below 0.05), AO 128–254, luma 185.787–231.711, max tilt² 0.098577 and signed normal fractions 0.26265/0.26175/0.36829/0.36762; 32 px has fraction 0.30469, AO 150–222, luma 190.50–219.57 (spread 29.07, above 20) and tilt² 0.00076894, retaining only macro shading and coarse normals. Twenty-three valid variants — cool/warm palettes, two roughness values, two reliefs, 12×14/16×18 lattices, narrow/wide pockets, low/high vertical crests, low/high horizontal crests, low/high floors, shallow/deep weave, shallow/deep bowls, weak/strong knots and quintic shoulders — pass all 15 cases. Twenty-seven real faults are rejected with ERR_ASSERTION: absent/faint ridges, tiny/huge pockets, vertical crest as high as or above horizontal, knot below crest, coarse/fine lattice, black palette, no color range, mirror finish, unresolved/extreme relief, high floor, circular pockets, sheared lattice, linear shoulders, metallic ridges, ribs only, per-cell height steps, inverted waffle, strong grain height, seed-moved lattice, noisy roughness, no pocket AO and dark ridges; circular pockets are rejected by area 0.2256 rather than fill, per-cell steps and inverted waffle by area, the seed-moved lattice by the cell-box checks (the separate cross-seed case is not proven independently) and extreme relief by the 32 px coarse-normal bound, not by independent diagnostics. Limits are in ACCEPTANCE.

`halite` adds an authored milky cube hopper-face approximation: most owners carry one flush, randomly rotated square cube face with two concentric square terraces stepping down inside it, like the stepped hollows of a hopper crystal; the face is milky and low-roughness on a rough pale grey-pink salt host. It is not Calcite's 75-degree oblique plates, Celestine/Barite rectangular plates, Wulfenite near-square plates, or an unstepped flat square. The actually read Handbook of Mineralogy (isometric, perfect {001} cleavage, skeletal/hopper-shaped faces, colorless or white when pure) and Minerals.net (cubes sometimes distorted with hopper growths), plus its actually viewed pink hopper cluster and white Death Valley cube cluster photographs, give no readable terrace ratio or depth numbers; the face radius, terrace radii of about 0.74/0.44 of the face half-size (each ±0.06) and the 0.08 height-unit drop per terrace are therefore authored approximations, with no measured or certified salt-crystal dimensions, transparency, dissolution physics or mineral identification. The independent kernel stores 9 pose/terrace values for each of 16 seeded owners plus a 128² grain and 16² host table: 16,784 Float64 values / 134,272 B of tables, not total RAM. The largest square face support including jitter is about 0.4455, below the 0.5 owner boundary; each point uses one rotation and at most six SmoothStep calls, with no allocation, hash, trigonometry or square root, and generic detail is skipped. The 0.08 m nominal tile, 0.004 UV-relative relief and palette are authored approximations; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency, refraction, lattice, dissolution simulation or chemical certification. It belongs to the same rotated nested-square hopper family as the existing `bismuthhoppers` (4×4 rotated square faces, six metal terraces, gold-violet, 0.16 m tile); this is only a white dielectric salt variant with two terraces, smaller face half-widths of 0.21–0.27 (versus 0.34), a 0.08 m tile and a rough salt host, not a new crystal class.

Five behaviors use the actual 512² sampler on three seeds without copying private poses: each owner yields an outer-face mask, then a first-terrace mask below height 0.52 and a second-terrace mask below 0.44, checked with centroid-ray corner lobes, four straight-line edge fits and concentricity. Each seed must contain 10–16 faces (measured 16/15/15); the outer face and first terrace each need four lobes, a longest/shortest corner-radius ratio below 1.08 (outer 1–1.0205, first terrace 1.0072–1.0397), four edge-line angles of 84–96 degrees (86.691–90), line RMS below 0.006 cell (maximum 0.0024625), opposite-edge parallel error below 5 degrees (maximum 3.3059), center-symmetry error below 0.06 (maximum 0.039139), and face area of 0.13–0.32 owner (0.16852–0.27765). The first terrace covers 0.4–0.75 of the face (0.48494–0.66324), the second 0.12–0.32 (0.15423–0.25484), with a difference above 0.15 (0.23393–0.49724); centroid offsets are below 0.02/0.04 cell (maximum 0.0039029/0.018096), and the first-terrace turn differs from the outer face by under 5 degrees (maximum 2). Rim/first/lowest heights are about 0.56/0.48/0.40 (0.56087–0.56162/0.48163–0.48246/0.39625–0.39961), with each step down exceeding 0.06 (0.078712–0.079776, 0.082398–0.085779). The lowest terrace is small enough that measured outlines once showed five lobes, a corner-radius ratio up to 1.209 and a turn up to 7 degrees, so only its area, concentricity and height are asserted, not edge straightness or turn (see ACCEPTANCE). Whole-tile coverage is 0.12–0.32 (0.18969–0.23801), face/host color gap exceeds 0.3 (0.40094–0.40647), roughness gap exceeds 0.4 (0.48861–0.48981), AO gap exceeds 0.2 (0.27271–0.27608), face roughness is 0.3–0.55 (0.43649–0.43709), metallic/mortar violations are 0, and random-turn concentration is below 0.75 (0.16348–0.38433). Seventeen valid variants — cool/warm palettes, two roughness values, two reliefs, small/large faces, shallow/deep terraces (0.07/0.095), narrow/wide first terrace (0.71/0.78), small/large second terrace (0.42/0.47), low/high coverage and quintic shoulders — pass all 14 cases. Twenty-six real faults are rejected with ERR_ASSERTION: absent/shallow/excessive terrace depth, tiny/owner-clipped/sparse faces, huge first terrace, tiny terraces, second terrace as large as the first, black palette, no color range, same roughness, mirror finish, unresolved/extreme relief, circular faces, oblique rhomb faces, fixed orientation, metallic faces, C0 host step, linear shoulders, smooth host, nonconcentric lowest terrace, 45-degree-rotated first terrace, face height speckle and inverted terraces; circular faces are rejected by lobe count 14≠4, extreme relief by the 32 px coarse-normal bound and the nonconcentric floor by edge RMS 0.006625, not by independent diagnostics. Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized C1 worst is 0.0000711/0.0000455/0.0000467 below 0.04, with signed period at most 2.6645e-15. Actual 512 has face fraction 0.1885, face/backing roughness 0.43539/0.92481, AO 153–252, max tilt² 0.28349 and signed normal fractions 0.022064/0.022057/0.021996/0.022015; 32 px keeps only macro color and coarse normals (tilt² 0.0015071). Limits are in ACCEPTANCE.

`calcite` adds an authored milky oblique cleavage-plate approximation: most owners carry one flush rhomb-shaped plate whose two edge pairs meet at about 75 degrees, with a parallel nested step plate slightly off center; the plate is milky and low-roughness on a rough dark brown mineral host. It is not Celestine/Barite rectangular plates, Wulfenite near-square plates, or Pyromorphite/Vanadinite hexagonal ends. The 75-degree angle is authored: the actually read Handbook of Mineralogy (perfect {1011} cleavage), Wikipedia and LibreTexts (rhombohedral cleavage, commonly white cleavage fragments) and the Minerals.net transparent Iceland spar photograph provide no readable face-angle value, so no measured or certified cleavage angle, crystal optics, transparency or birefringence is claimed. The independent kernel stores 10 pose/step values for each of 16 seeded owners plus a 128² grain and 16² host table: 16,800 Float64 values / 134,400 B of tables, not total RAM. The largest plate support including jitter is about 0.4692, below the 0.5 owner boundary; each point uses one rotation, one oblique conversion and at most four SmoothStep calls, with no allocation, hash, trigonometry or square root, and generic detail is skipped. The 0.1 m nominal tile, 0.0035 UV-relative relief and palette are authored approximations; opaque dielectric four maps, default metallic 0, optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership stay unchanged, without added meshes, transparency, refraction, lattice or chemical certification.

Five behaviors use the actual 512² sampler on three seeds without copying private poses: each owner mask yields corner lobes from centroid rays, four straight-line edge fits, then the outer plate and nested step are checked. Each seed must contain 10–16 plates with four lobes each, a long/short corner-radius ratio of 1.22–1.38 (measured 1.2808–1.3266; about 1.303 for 75 degrees), four edge-line angles of 72–78 degrees (73.824–75.717), line RMS below 0.006 cell (maximum 0.002428), opposite-edge parallel error below 3 degrees (maximum 1.2788), center-symmetry error below 0.03 (maximum 0.016793), and plate area of 0.12–0.3 owner (0.16431–0.24902). The nested step covers 0.12–0.55 of the plate (0.20323–0.45133), has shape ratio 0.9–1.1 (0.95963–1.0311), axis difference below 4 degrees (maximum 2.2677), stays fully inside and rises 0.05–0.09 relief units above the plate (0.067794–0.069066). Whole-tile coverage is 0.12–0.3 (0.15759–0.19664), plate/host color gap exceeds 0.5 (0.66433–0.67151), roughness gap exceeds 0.4 (0.5385–0.53955), AO gap exceeds 0.3 (0.41917–0.41946), plate roughness is 0.25–0.5 (0.37055–0.37081), metallic/mortar violations are 0, and random-turn concentration is below 0.75 (0.28922–0.36418). Seventeen valid variants — cool/warm palettes, two roughness values, two reliefs, 74/76 degrees, small/large plates, low/high step rise, low/high coverage, small/large steps and quintic shoulders — pass all 14 cases. Twenty-six real faults are rejected with ERR_ASSERTION: 90/70/80 degree angles, tiny/owner-clipped plates, sparse plates, absent/excessive step rise, tiny/filling/escaping steps, black palette, no color range, same roughness, mirror finish, absent/extreme relief, rectangular plates, circular plates, fixed orientation, metallic plates, C0 host step, linear shoulders, smooth host, axis-aligned square step and plate height speckle; circular plates are rejected by lobe count and extreme relief by the 32 px coarse-normal bound, not by independent diagnostics. Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized C1 worst is 0.000110/0.0000905/0.000106 below 0.04, with signed period at most 1.6653e-16. Actual 512 has face fraction 0.16197, face/backing roughness 0.37486/0.90829, AO 140–250, max tilt² 0.2018 and signed normal fractions 0.01513/0.01529/0.01382/0.01378; 32 px keeps only macro color and coarse normals (tilt² 0.0015). Limits are in ACCEPTANCE.

`flangenuts` adds an authored top-view approximation of steel hexagonal flange nuts: a trimmed six-flat hex body stands on a lower circular conical flange with an open central bore revealing rougher dark steel backing; it is neither flangeless Hex Nuts nor the closed dome of Cap Nuts. Proportions follow only the DIN 6923 M5–M20 table and drawing trend: flange diameter dc is about 1.38–1.48 across-flats s, minimum across-corners e is about 1.10–1.115 s, minimum bore da is about 0.60–0.67 s, and the drawing shows a 15–30 degree flange face; this material uses a flange/flat ratio near 1.40 and a bore/flat ratio near 0.68. Sixteen seeded owners cache six plane normals; 16×12 plane values plus 96² grain use 9,408 Float64 elements / 75,264 table bytes, not total RAM. The 0.413 flange radius stays inside the 0.5 owner; each point uses six smooth plane products, one hypot and a few SmoothStep calls, with no allocation, hash, trigonometry or neighboring-cell scan, and generic detail is skipped. The 0.08 m nominal tile follows Hex Nuts; the 0.03 UV-relative relief and palette are authored approximations. The whole tile including dark steel backing is metallic 1, the root-factory default is 1, and explicit factor 0 does not rewrite maps. It does not include underside serrations, full 3D nuts, internal threads, transparency, steel hardness, corrosion data or DIN/ISO certification.

Eight behaviors use the actual sampler on each seed’s 16 owners without copying private planes: 720-ray bisection first finds the mask’s flange edge and bore edge, then a height isocontour finds the hex-body outline. It requires six corner lobes, about 60-degree spacing, a 1.05–1.15 corner/flat ratio, flat-edge projection spread below 0.006, near-circular flange and bore (max/min below 1.03), a minimum flange radius 1.25–1.6 times the flat radius and at least 1.1 times the corner radius, a bore of 0.45–0.85 flat radius whose floor is within 0.02 of backing, a hex top 0.2–0.5 above the flange rim, a flange rim 0.15–0.4 above backing, a 0.03–0.15 cone rise, and bounded wall AO, backing roughness/color separation and random-orientation concentration. Twenty-one valid variants — cool/warm steel palettes, two roughness values, two reliefs, larger/smaller nuts, two flange ratios, two bore sizes, two cone heights, two hex and flange heights, two wall-AO strengths and a quintic flange shoulder — pass all eight cases. Twenty-eight real faults are rejected with ERR_ASSERTION: flange hugging the hex corners, flange smaller than the hex, owner-clipped flange, closed/oversized bore, no height step, flange as tall as hex, no/steep cone, no wall AO, no backing roughness/color separation, black palette, absent/extreme relief, tiny nut, height speckle, over-clipped octagon corners, pentagon/octagon body, square/oval flange, oval bore, nonmetal, C0 grain, linear bevel, fixed orientation and missing hex body. Oversized bore, same-height flange, steep cone and missing body are rejected because the hex isocontour lobe count degenerates, not through independent diagnostics. Three-seed observed-curvature C1 over hex bevels, corner trims, bore rims, wall rings, flange rims and signed UV passes; 512 maps include steep bevel normals, while 32 keeps macro color and coarse normals. Forced WebGPU/WebGL2 and shipped root factory, native Worker, cache and ownership paths were exercised; limits are in ACCEPTANCE and do not extend cross-browser, Canvas2D or fastener certification.

`capnuts` adds an authored top-view approximation of steel hexagonal cap nuts: six straight flats on a lightly trimmed hex body, a closed circular dome and rougher dark steel backing; it is not the through-bored Hex Nuts material. Proportions follow only the DIN 1587 table trend: dome diameter dk is about 0.93–0.96 of across-flats s, minimum across-corners e is about 1.106–1.113 s, and a lower 30-degree chamfer ring approximates the corners. Sixteen seeded owners cache six plane normals in the constructor; 16×12 plane values plus 96² grain use 9,408 Float64 elements / 75,264 table bytes, not total RAM. The 0.421 corner trim radius stays inside the 0.5 owner; each point uses six smooth plane products, one hypot and a few squares/SmoothStep calls, with no allocation, hash, trigonometry or neighboring-cell scan, and generic detail is skipped. The 0.08 m nominal tile follows Hex Nuts; the 0.03 UV-relative relief, dome crown and palette are authored approximations, not real nut dimensions, threads, corrosion or material certification. The whole tile including dark steel backing is metallic 1, the root-factory default is 1, and explicit factor 0 does not rewrite maps. It does not include full 3D nuts, internal threads, transparency, steel hardness, DIN/ISO certification or measured steel data.

Eight behaviors use the actual sampler on each seed’s 16 owners: the dark-backing mask centroid and 720 bisected true-contour rays require six corner lobes, about 60-degree spacing, a 1.05–1.15 corner/flat ratio and flat-edge projection spread below 0.006, without copying private planes. The dome must be closed, descend monotonically from the center in every direction, have a near-circular half-height isocontour at 0.6–0.85 of the flat radius, a rim at about 0.88–1.0 of the flat radius, stand 0.35–0.55 above the hex top, and the hex top must stand 0.35–0.55 above backing; corner chamfer drop, neck AO, backing roughness/color separation and random-orientation concentration are also bounded. Nineteen valid variants — cool/warm steel palettes, two roughness values, two reliefs, larger/smaller nuts, two dome ratios, two crown heights, tighter/wider and unclipped corners, a true spherical cap and quintic shoulder — pass all eight cases. Twenty-seven real faults are rejected with ERR_ASSERTION: no/tiny/oversized dome, over-clipped octagon corners, no/excessive chamfer, hex top collapsed to backing, over-tall dome, no neck AO, no roughness/color separation, black palette, absent/extreme relief, tiny or owner-clipped giant nuts, height speckle, pentagon/octagon, through bore, hex-shaped/oval dome, nonmetal, C0 grain, linear bevel, fixed orientation and pointed dome. Unclipped corners are accepted because the observed ratio cannot distinguish them from wide trim; this is not claimed as detectable. Three-seed observed-curvature C1 over hex bevels, corner trims, dome rims, necks and signed UV passes; 512 maps include steep bevel normals, while 32 keeps macro color and strong low-resolution normals. Forced WebGPU/WebGL2 and shipped root factory, native Worker, cache and ownership paths were exercised; limits are in ACCEPTANCE and do not extend cross-browser, Canvas2D or fastener certification.

`bedfordcord` adds an authored khaki warp-direction woven-rib surface approximation: twenty vertical cords are each made of twenty-four flat weft-direction dashes, adjacent cords are staggered by half a dash pitch, and continuous fine grooves separate the cords; the cord body lies below the dashes and the grooves lower still. It is neither corduroy cut-pile wales, rib-knit loops, faille/grosgrain transverse weft ribs nor a yarn-by-yarn draft float. A separate case is used instead of forcing this lattice into the existing warp/weft yarn kernel: each point performs only floor/fract, squares and SmoothStep composition, with no allocation, hash, trigonometry, square root or neighboring-cell scan, and generic detail is skipped. The 20×24 dash-tone table plus 96² grain use 9,696 Float64 elements / 77,568 table bytes, not total RAM; dash half-extents 0.45/0.46 remain inside the 0.5 owner. The seed changes dash tone and fine grain only; cord pitch, dash pitch and stagger are fixed. The 0.07 m nominal tile, 0.0018 UV-relative relief, 20 cords / 24 rows and khaki palette are authored approximations, not calibrated to fabric weight, yarn count or photograph scale; metallic maps and the root-factory default are 0, and explicit factor 1 does not rewrite maps. It does not include real warp/stuffer interlacement, cloth physics, transmission, fuzz, fiber meshes or cotton specification/provenance certification.

Eight behaviors measure a 480² actual sampler raster for the whole tile without copying private tables: column means find continuous dark grooves and the cord count; each cord center yields 24 dashes with uniform spacing, 0.8 of the row pitch in length and 0.833 of the cord pitch in width; every adjacent pair, including the last cord wrapping to the first, must be offset by half a dash pitch; groove/cord-body/dash height, color and roughness ordering and dash height/tone scatter must remain bounded. Twenty valid variants — warm khaki, olive, neutral-gray and green palettes, two reliefs, 16×20 and 24×28 cord/row layouts, 20/28 rows, two roughness sets, stagger 0.45/0.55, two dash proportions, stronger tone variation, softer crown, quintic shoulders and four-power rounded rectangles — pass all eight cases. Twenty-nine real faults are rejected with ERR_ASSERTION: zero/quarter stagger, odd-cord seam, coarse/needle cords, too few/many rows, absent/wide grooves, flat/over-tall dashes, body as deep as groove, wrong color/roughness, collapsed contrast, absent/excessive relief, short dashes, dashes spilling into grooves, tone/height speckle, transposed axis, drifting stagger, metal cloth, C0 grain, linear shoulders, random pile spikes and nonperiodic rows. Three-seed relief-normalized observed-curvature C1, cord/row/half-row join and signed UV checks pass. Actual 512 maps resolve low-amplitude four-direction normals; 32 retains macro color and tiny normals, not resolved cord detail. Forced WebGPU/WebGL2 and the shipped root factory, native Worker, cache and ownership paths were exercised; limits are in ACCEPTANCE and do not extend cross-browser, Canvas2D or cloth certification.

`staurolite` adds authored dark-brown 60-degree crossed-prism surface profiles: two straight-sided, blunt-ended prisms join into four arms and share one flat top rather than stacking double height at the crossing. Both the light coarse-grained host and lower-roughness crystals are opaque dielectric. Sixteen seeded owners cache both axis cosine/sine pairs, centers, inverse scales and tints; the relative angle is calculated in the constructor. Sixteen × eight pose values plus 128² grain use 16,512 Float64 elements / 132,096 table bytes, not total RAM. The per-axis support bound hypot(0.32,0.07)×0.98+0.07=0.391016 stays below 0.5; point sampling has no allocations, hashes, trig, square roots or neighboring-cell scans and skips generic detail. The 0.12 m nominal tile, 0.0035 UV-relative relief and 60-degree UV angle are authored surface approximations, not calibrated crystal dimensions; no full prism meshes, schist foliation, transmission/refraction, mineral identification or chemical/specimen certification.

Eight behaviors find centers from an actual 128² full-tile / 32²-per-owner dark mask, using 4-connected crystals and 8-connected background so diagonal openings are not false holes. Actual contour rays in 720 directions identify four long arms, 60/120-degree gaps, parallel straight sides and blunt ends; transverse measurements stop at the first exterior boundary instead of re-entering the other arm. Center and arm tops stay level without copying private hashes/poses or pinning a smoothing formula. Fourteen valid palette, finish, relief, length/width, 58/62-degree, equivalent supplementary 120-degree, quintic-shoulder and slightly unequal-twin variants pass all eight cases. Twenty-six real missing-arm, wrong-angle, stacked/open/enclosed-hole crossings, fixed orientation, wrong finish/color/metal, owner clipping, C0 and linear-shoulder faults are rejected. Three-seed relief-normalized observed-curvature C1, owner/grain joins and signed UV checks pass. Actual 512 maps resolve four-direction rim normals; 32 has low-amplitude macro normals, not resolved fine rims. Forced WebGPU/WebGL2 and the shipped root factory, native Worker, cache and ownership paths were exercised; browser and diagnostic-probe caveats are in ACCEPTANCE, without extending cross-browser, Canvas2D or physical-mineral certification.

`dowelpins` adds authored silver solid unthreaded headless pin side elevations: parallel straight shafts, one rounded end, an opposite small entry bevel and a shallow transverse cylindrical crown. There is no Clevis Pin head/tail bore, Linchpin loop or flat key face. It reuses the Clevis Pin seeded-pose, smooth-cover and crown kernel, selecting headless/holeless branches and precalculating end curvature in the constructor without evaluating skipped head/hole profiles or adding another pin kernel. Sixteen six-value poses plus 96² grain remain 9,312 Float64 values / 74,496B of tables, not total RAM; there is no new table. Maximum rotated support hypot(0.36,0.095)×0.95+0.016=0.369708 stays inside the 0.5 owner boundary. Sampling allocates nothing and uses no hashes, trigonometry, square roots or neighbor scans; backing skips grain and generic detail is skipped. Steel is metallic 1 and opaque gray backing 0, with default material factor 1/explicit 0. The 0.12m nominal tile, 0.0032 UV-relative relief and proportions are authored approximations, not calibrated DIN dimensions or hardness. Four maps/optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership remain intact, without full cylinder meshes, transparency, threaded extraction bores, press/interference-fit physics, load, rustproofing or DIN/ISO certification.

Eight behaviors locate each actual metal mask with PCA, refine its axis from measured parallel-side midlines, then binary-search the actual contour for distinct end forms, uniform shaft width, no head/enclosed bore and a shallow transverse crown without copying private seeded poses. Progressive rounded curvature is recognized without pinning a quadratic formula: eleven valid palette, finish, relief, shaft length/width, cap/bevel and smooth non-quadratic cap variants pass all eight cases. Twenty-five actual head, bore, single-direction, metal-backing/nonmetal-shaft, thread-like rib, flat/excessive crown, missing/wrong cap/bevel, thin/broad/short/owner-clipped, wrong pigment/finish/relief, linear-rim and tiny visible-grain C0 faults fail assertions; an added but unused head calculation is inert and not counted as a fault. Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized C1 covers observed-curvature refinement, a 128² grid, owner joins and 4,096 signed UV probes. Actual 32/512 five maps verify steel/gray nonmetal backing separation, finish, AO and opacity; 512 checks four signed rim normals while 32 requires only resolvable macro normals. All 817 prior kinds retain 3,268 four-seed seven-point signed-UV sampler streams exactly; 48 cases across eight related metal surfaces at 32/512 retain byte-identical five maps, preserving prior Clevis behavior.

`europeanbeech` adds an authored pale pink-tan fine straight-grained radial face with numerous diffuse shallow longitudinal vessels and minute flush ray flecks, distinct from White Ash coarse earlywood bands, broad White Oak flecks and raised burls. It reuses the existing White Ash/White Oak cached-noise, compact-vessel and curved-ray kernel, equalizing early/late vessel widths and probability, reducing band contrast and using constructor-only pore jitter to reduce regular column structure. Rays change pigment/finish and suppress vessels without adding raised height. The 48×24×4 vessel values, 6×18×6 ray values and 4×3, 96×4, 128² noise total 22,036 Float64 values / 176,288B of tables, not total RAM. Support including jitter stays within vessel X≤0.445/Y≤0.485 and ray X≤0.42/Y≤0.25844 owner-cell units, below 0.5. Sampling allocates nothing and uses no hashes, trigonometry, square roots or neighbor scans; generic detail is skipped. The 0.24m nominal tile, 0.0012 UV-relative relief, pigment and density are authored approximations, not calibrated timber dimensions or vessel diameters. Opaque dielectric M0/explicit override, four maps/optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership remain intact; there are no vessel meshes, transmission, species identification, steaming/chemical, waterproofing, structural or provenance certifications.

Eight behaviors use actual 512² roughness/AO masks and four-connected PCA to measure short fleck axes, flush height and pigment contrast, shallow longitudinal openings and measured vessel distribution over frequencies 1–64, rejecting ring bands or fixed columns without copying private hashes/placement tables. Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized C1 covers observed-curvature refinement, a 128² grid, vessel/ray owners and 4,096 signed UV probes. Actual 32 and 512 five-map output checks warm pink-tan pigment, finish and opaque M0; only 512 requires resolved micro normals in four directions, without claiming resolved tiny pores at 32. Nine valid palette/finish/relief/density/fineness/fleck/jitter variants pass all eight cases. Twenty-five real missing/broad fleck, missing/ring/coarse/sparse/deep/unrecessed vessel, fixed-column, absent ray pigment/finish/grain, wrong pigment/finish/relief, metal, raised/grooved ray, linear vessel/ray shoulder, tiny C0 and unshaded-opening faults fail assertions. All 816 prior kinds retain 3,264 four-seed seven-point signed-UV sampler streams exactly; 48 cases across eight related woods at 32/512 retain byte-identical five maps.

`habotai` adds an authored pearl-white fine dense 1/1 plain silk surface with same-dye warp/weft, shallow crossing relief and soft sheen, distinct from matte cotton Batiste, Surah twill, long-float Sateen and irregular Shantung slubs. It reuses the existing balanced-plain kernel and smooth yarn shoulders, adding only a preset/constructor selection; the 128² grain totals 16,384 Float64 values / 131,072B of tables, not total RAM, with no extra float table. Sampling allocates nothing and performs no hashes, trigonometry or square roots; generic detail is skipped. The 84/84 yarn and 336/336 filament-shading counts, 0.04m nominal tile and 0.0002 UV-relative relief are authored selections, not calibrated textile density or thickness. Pearl pigment and lower roughness give soft reflection without adding an anisotropic silk BRDF. Opaque dielectric M0/explicit overrides, four maps/optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership remain unchanged. Real Habotai may be sheer; this preset selects an opaque surface approximation, without transmission, fold/fiber meshes, cloth physics, momme/composition or conservation-performance certification.

Eight behaviors infer actual over/under arm heights, neighboring 1/1 transitions, same-dye/equal-height yarns, shallow crossover/binding drops, recessed gaps/AO and lower roughness. An 8,192-point output profile measures 84 dominant yarns on each axis while accepting a valid 80-thread variant instead of pinning preset literals; private hashes are not copied. Three-seed six-channel relief-normalized C1 covers observed-curvature refinement, a 128² grid, yarn-owner/filament joins and 4,096 signed UV probes. Actual 512 five-map output verifies pearl dye, gentle normals in four directions, finish and opaque M0. At 32px only supported color/finish/opacity are required, not unresolved microholes or normals. Nine valid variants pass all eight cases, including coarser 80/80 yarns and two weft-filament groups; twenty missing/excess/inverted lift, wrong finish/dye, coarse/unbalanced yarn, narrow weft/open mesh, quantized-away/excess normals, dark tone, unequal dye, always-warp/basket topology, linear shoulder, metal and tiny-C0 faults fail genuine assertions. All 815 older kinds retain exact seven-point signed-UV sampler output across 3,260 seeded streams; eight related fabrics retain byte-identical five-map output in 48 cases at 32/512.

`azurite` adds authored deep-blue broad tabular/prismatic faces with strongly oblique parallel ends, shallow longitudinal striae and locally parallel triples rotated between clusters above dark rough mineral backing. It differs from radiating short Cavansite needles, narrow strongly ridged Kyanite blades and near-square orange Wulfenite plates. It reuses the existing Celestine/Barite/Kyanite seeded-plate kernel, adding only a profile: twenty-five six-value poses plus 128² grain total 16,534 Float64 values / 132,272B of tables, not total RAM. Rotated support hypot(0.23,0.3446)×0.975+0.025=0.428948 stays inside the 0.5 owner boundary. Sampling allocates nothing, performs no hashes or neighbor scans, retains one existing stria cosine and skips generic detail. Low-roughness opaque dielectric M0 faces do not bake photographic white specular reflections into metallic maps or white pigment. The 0.1m nominal tile, 0.0034 UV-relative relief, colors and cluster density are authored approximations. Four maps/optional height, tint/repeats, Worker/cache and individual texture ownership remain unchanged. No complete crystal meshes, transparency/refraction, pleochroism, growth, mineral/chemical or copper-safety certification is added.

Seven behaviors locate faces through a 512² actual color mask and four-connected components, refine PCA with a local minimum-area fit, and regress both observed end contours over eleven transverse sections to measure obliquity, parallelism and straightness without copying private hashes/poses. They also check local parallel triples, inter-cluster directions, broad aspect, shallow striated core span, raised smoother faces and dominant rough host. Existing observed-curvature refinement plus a 128² grid, owner joins and 4,096 signed UV probes checks three-seed six-channel relief-normalized C1. Actual 512 five-map output verifies deep blue, host, finish, four signed shoulder-normal directions and opaque M0. Eight valid variants pass all seven cases; twenty square/weakly/excessively oblique ends, narrow/wide/single/sparse plates, absent/deep striae, tilted cores, wrong dye/finish/relief, linear end, metal, tiny C0 and flush-face faults fail genuine assertions. All 814 older kinds preserve exact seven-point signed-UV sampler output in 3,256 seeded streams; eight related minerals retain byte-identical five-map output in 48 cases at 32/512.

`whiteoak` adds an authored warm olive-brown quarter-sawn face with broad long flush transverse ray flecks across straight grain and shallower recessed longitudinal vessels. It differs from White Ash without broad flecks, compact dense Lacewood scales and raised burls. It reuses the White Ash ring-porous/cached-noise kernel, adding eighteen six-value curved-ray records only for White Oak: 18,680 Float64 values / 149,440B of tables, not total RAM. Ray support including jitter stays within X≤0.46 and Y≤0.47124 owner-cell units, below 0.5.
